\section{Computational Information Value and Reusable Summaries}
\PROVED\ (exact generalized-support frontier; linear persisted summaries).
Let a fixed parity batch have row code \(C\le\mathbb F_2^b\) of dimension
\(r\), and let \(d_k(C)\) be its \(k\)-th generalized Hamming weight.  If
preprocessing persists at most \(L\) linear source bits while continuation
retains the original source and may adaptively probe raw coordinates, then
\[
 Q^*_{\rm lin}(L)=d_{r-L}(C).
\]
At each reachable leaf, exactness gives \(C\subseteq S+E_J\), so
\(C\cap E_J\) contains an \((r-L)\)-dimensional subcode supported on the
queried coordinates.  Conversely, store a complement of an attaining
subcode and read its support.  The theorem permits stored linear forms
outside \(C\) and adaptive continuation, but not arbitrary nonlinear
summary fibers.

\FALSIFIED\ (compact representation implies cheap construction).
At \(L=r-1\), the optimum is \(d_1(C)\), the minimum distance of \(C\).
Computing this value for general binary linear codes is NP-hard.  A supplied
attaining subcode is polynomially verifiable and executable, but finding it
is a separate \(P_{\rm discover}\) obligation.  In the finite nonlinear
control through \(b=4\), 1,672,200 one-bit summaries produced no advantage
over the linear frontier; this is \COMPUTATIONAL\ evidence only.  The
arbitrary-nonlinear frontier, strict22 authentication, and native tape/UTM
transport remain \OPEN.  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_2048_MAIN_GENERALIZED-SUPPORT-FRONTIER-REFEREE212.tex}.

\PROVED\ (batch answer-vector transcript bound; indexed binary-source model).
For a fixed batch of future contexts let \(R\) be the number of distinct exact
batch answer vectors induced by the source family.  If preprocessing uses at
most \(P_{\rm src}\) binary source probes and leaves at most \(L\)
source-dependent persisted bits, while the continuation uses at most
\(Q_{\rm src}\) binary source probes across the entire batch with arbitrary
adaptive caching and recomputation, then
\[
 2^{\min(P_{\rm src},L)+Q_{\rm src}}\ge R.
\]
All source-dependent side channels must be charged in the persisted state.
For a linear parity batch \(Wa\) this gives
\(\min(P_{\rm src},L)+Q_{\rm src}\ge\rank W\); coordinate batches attain
every point \(P_{\rm src}=L,\ Q_{\rm src}=\rank W-L\).
Thus multiplying a one-query probe lower bound by the number of contexts is
\FALSIFIED\ when retained-source access and persistent caching are allowed:
all \(b\) source bits can be read once and reused for any polynomial parity
batch.  Query input, output, arithmetic, verification and peak storage remain
charged.  This is not a SAT lower bound.

\PROVED\ (affine contextual rank potential).
For current certified source set \(a_0+U\) and target batch \(W\), the
independently computable potential \(\Phi_W=\dim W(U)\) drops by exactly zero
or one under one consistent certified linear fact.  Duplicates have value
zero; XOR shares exhibit complementarity, so initial singleton values are not
additive.  Acquisition, verification and application cost are separate from
\(I\mid_{v_{\Phi_W}}\).  Exact generalized-support refinement for rank
\(r<b\), authenticated SAT-context transport, nonlinear potentials, and
machine-model conversion remain \OPEN.  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1948_MAIN_BATCH-RANK-AMORTIZATION-REFEREE211.tex}.

\PROVED\ (exact finite interaction model; restricted application).
For a declared state, known certified facts, target, cost model and independently
defined potential, write \(I\mid_v\) with
\(v_\Phi(I\mid s,K)=\Phi(s,K)-\Phi(s,K\cup\{I\})\).
For a finite fact menu \(E\), the gain set function
\(g(S)=\Phi(s,K)-\Phi(s,K\cup S)\) has the unique interaction dividends
\[
 d(U)=\sum_{V\subseteq U}(-1)^{|U|-|V|}g(V),\qquad
 g(S)=\sum_{U\subseteq S}d(U).
\]
The marginal of fact \(I\) after \(S\) is the sum of precisely those dividends
containing \(I\) and contained in \(S\cup\{I\}\).  Positive higher-order
terms capture complementarity; negative terms capture redundancy.  Actual
marginals telescope along a certified execution, but initial singleton labels
are not additive.  An arbitrary normalized Boolean ledger on \(m\) facts has
\(2^m-1\) independent bits; fixed certified interaction order \(k\) leaves only
\(\sum_{j\le k}\binom mj=O(m^k)\) coefficients, whose discovery and bit lengths
must still be charged.

\PROVED\ (detached reusable-summary and retained-source quantifier firewall).
For source family \(\mathcal A\), future contexts \(\mathcal B\), and answer
vectors \(r_A=(D(A,B))_{B\in\mathcal B}\), any deterministic fixed-length
detached summary exact for all later contexts satisfies
\(L\ge\lceil\log_2|\{r_A\}|\rceil\).  For all pin contexts these vectors are
the boundary functions \(f_A(x)=\exists y\,A(x,y)\).  Report228 realizes
every \(b\)-bit boundary function by a canonical proper 3CNF
\(A_f\) of \(N_b=\Theta(b^2 2^b)\) bits, so a source-discarding summary
prepared before the pin and exact for all pins needs at least \(2^b\) bits.

\PROVED\ (same-family targeted-source escape, restricted promise).
After a canonical point continuation \(B_a\) reveals \(a\), a fixed
source-retaining controller reads the first literal word of block \(a\):
a boundary-variable identifier means \(f(a)=0\), while a private identifier
means \(f(a)=1\).  Thus one source-word probe and \(O(b)\) address/work
bits decide \(\operatorname{SAT}(A_f\wedge B_a)\).  On the native indexed
bit model the word's \(\Theta(b)\) bits and address arithmetic are charged;
recognizing an unpromised source costs a scan and nonmembers require exact
fallback.  Hence the detached all-query floor does not lower-bound a joint or
source-retaining solver, and a realized run is not charged for all
counterfactual pins.  This easy-family accessor is not an all-input ARC
successor.  Since at most \(2^{N+1}-1\) source descriptions have length at
most \(N\), storing the original source remains a legal control and counting
alone cannot force exponential summary length relative to \(N\).  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1748_MAIN_ARC-SOURCE-RETENTION-QUANTIFIER-FIREWALL229.tex}.

\FALSIFIED\ (unrestricted value policy).
Duplicate facts and two XOR shares respectively require negative redundancy and
positive synergy terms.  A policy that selects only a strictly positive current
singleton value can pay an arbitrarily costly direct-answer action while two
initially zero-valued XOR shares solve the target cheaply.  Value acquisition,
authentication, verification, application and score computation remain separate
charged costs.  These are policy and accounting theorems, not SAT lower bounds.
Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-29_1848_MAIN_CONTEXTUAL-VALUE-DIVIDEND-REFEREE210.tex}.

\section{Known Unconditional Lower Bounds}
The strongest unconditional circuit lower bounds for explicit functions remain modest relative to the thresholds needed to separate P from NP.

\begin{itemize}
\item For general Boolean circuits over the $B_2$ basis, the best known lower bound for an explicit function in P is $3.5n - O(\log n)$ (Mishra--Santhanam, ECCC TR26-260, October 2026).
\end{itemize}

\section{Time-Space Tradeoffs}
\KNOWN\ (Fortnow--Lipton--van Melkebeek--Viglas, Williams).  
In the indexed read-only-input random-access model, SAT cannot be decided simultaneously in time $N^{t+o(1)}$ and space $N^{s+o(1)}$ whenever
\[
s < \beta(t) := \frac12\Bigl(\frac{t+2}{t^2}-t\Bigr)
\]
for the applicable range of $t$, and in particular cannot be decided in time $N^{2\cos(\pi/7)-o(1)}$ and space $N^{o(1)}$ (Williams). These are the strongest published simultaneous bounds used in the present investigation. Numerical evaluation confirms $2\cos(\pi/7)\approx 1.801938$. Buss--Williams established that this endpoint is optimal within the framework of alternation-trading proofs for the subpolynomial-space case.

\section{Constructive Single-Child Route and Oracle Firewall}
\PROVED\ (elementary sufficient theorem, peer review pending).
Let $\mathcal C$ be a closed encoded class containing an NP-complete language.
If one fixed deterministic total uniform algorithm physically produces, on
every valid nonbase $x\in\mathcal C$, one answer-preserving child
$G(x)\in\mathcal C$ with $|G(x)|\le |x|^\lambda$ for fixed
$0<\lambda<1$, and all construction/access cost is $O(|x|^c)$, then
\[
 T(N)\le N^c+T(N^\lambda)=O(N^c).
\]
Fixed bounded bases therefore give SAT in P.  Bare existence of such a map is
a reformulation rather than progress: if SAT were already in P, a decider
could output one of two fixed YES/NO bases.

\FALSIFIED\ (theorem transfer).
At OpenAI \texttt{math} commit
\texttt{adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a}, the unitary-synthesis
preprint proves a uniformly generated polynomial quantum skeleton with one
smaller quantum call per level, but the target chooses unrestricted Boolean
oracle contents.  The source explicitly gives no efficient classical
constructor for those contents and states that the generator never computes a
table entry.  The quantifiers $\forall x\,\exists f_x$ alone give no
classical upper bound: a one-query skeleton can use the hindsight bit
$f_x(0)=L(x)$ for any language $L$.  A valid classical repair needs one fixed
polynomial evaluator $E(x,q)=f_x(q)$, or a uniformly materialized and charged
oracle circuit; then ordinary composition is polynomial.

\PROVED\ (restricted representation audit, peer review pending).
For
\[
 F_t(z)=\bigwedge_i(z\vee A_i)\wedge
        \bigwedge_j(\neg z\vee B_j),
\qquad
 \exists z\,F_t=
 (\bigwedge_iA_i)\vee(\bigwedge_jB_j)
 =\bigwedge_{i,j}(A_i\vee B_j),
\]
with disjoint two-literal $A_i,B_j$, the auxiliary-free projection has
$t^2$ prime-implicate clauses, while a shared fan-in-two circuit has $4t-1$
gates.  Full Tseitin re-entry has $12t-2$ clauses and remains
$\Theta(t\log t)$ bits, the same exponent as the original selector.
Reintroducing one selector can reproduce the original encoded instance exactly.
Thus compact representation is useful but supplies no fixed-power, recursively
closed shrink on this family.  Exhaustive checks covered 69,904 assignments;
finite evidence remains \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.

\PROVED\ (restricted decision--context separation; independently refereed).
For the connected proper 3CNF family $F_{t,s}$ in report202, every
unaugmented source is SAT and therefore has one decision state and a constant
YES child on its recognizable promise class.  Yet four-pin continuations
$Q_i$ satisfy $\operatorname{SAT}(F_{t,s}\land Q_i)=s_i$, so any source-free
state supporting an adversarially selected continuation has $2^t$ values and
at least $t$ bits on some source.  This exact INDEX floor is for the richer
online query contract; it is \FALSIFIED\ as a lower bound on decision-only
contraction.  The promise class does not contain arbitrary SAT.

\OPEN.  The decisive constructive arrow is an unconditional, answer-oblivious,
all-input child constructor with physical encoded shrink, effective choices,
closure at a second genuine recursive level, and charged failed attempts,
storage, access, and model transport.  A local failure or a restricted
representation lower bound does not imply P$\ne$NP.

\section{Conditional Resource Contraction}
Under the temporary assumption that a fixed uniform deterministic total correct SAT solver $A$ exists with
\[
T_A(N)\le N^{K+o(1)},\qquad S_A(N)\le N^{L+o(1)}
\]
for unknown finite $K,L\ge 2$, the parameters $\lambda=1/(5K)$, $\zeta=0.1$ would, if an all-input transformation of the stated budget existed, yield a simultaneous profile
\[
t'\le 1.3-\frac1{5K},\qquad s'\le 0.2
\]
that lies strictly inside the FLMV forbidden region for every finite $K\ge 2$. Independent numerical verification confirms the membership (e.g., for $K=2$, $t'=1.2$, $\beta(1.2)\approx 0.511 > 0.2$; for large $K$, $t'\to 1.3$ and $\beta(1.3)\approx 0.326 > 0.2$). The recurrence
\[
t'\le\max(1.1,0.6+t/2),\qquad s'\le\max(0.5,s/2)
\]
has fixed point $(1.2,0.5)$ satisfying the tangent certificate $433t+216s<630$.  

\OPEN. The required universal all-input construction of $\le N^{1-\lambda+\zeta+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children of length $\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$, with aggregate local work $\le N^{1.1+o(1)}$ and live parent space $\le N^{0.2+o(1)}$, including every discovery, correctness, access, memory and combination cost, remains unproved. Numerical membership alone does not establish a contradiction. Primary literature supplies no such universal fully charged transformation. Exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 is NP-complete, while Horn and 2-SAT are in P and may serve as restricted test cases. Reconfirmed 2026-10-29 01:17 UTC (GROK): no new primary source closes the transformation; numerical membership verified.

\RESTRICTED. On Horn and 2-SAT instances, polynomial-time algorithms exist independently of any hypothetical solver $A$. Thus restricted contractions are feasible in principle without black-box access to $A$, but this does not transfer to the NP-complete case.

\FALSIFIED\ (restricted model). Literal clause partitions fail on expander graphs. After Gaussian elimination of a Tseitin affine shell conjoined with an arbitrary non-affine core $Q(B)$, the surviving core retains an encoding length that can be $\Theta(N)$, forcing parent-sized queries to $A$ that exhaust the local-work budget $N^{1.1+o(1)}$.

\begin{remark}[Residual Core Size]
Let $F=\mathrm{Tseitin}(G,c)\land Q(B)$ where $G$ is an expander of linear size and $Q(B)$ is an arbitrary exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 formula on $|B|=\Theta(N)$ variables. Gaussian elimination eliminates the affine shell in linear time and produces an equisatisfiable residual instance whose encoding length remains $\Theta(N)$ whenever $Q$ is not itself reducible by the same linear algebra. Under black-box access to $A$ the residual must be submitted essentially intact.
\end{remark}

\section{Proposed Mechanisms (2026-10-28)}
The following falsifiable mechanisms address the open all-input transformation:
\begin{enumerate}
\item Explicit cost accounting for black-box discovery and verification of partitions or projections on ordinary 3-SAT instances; falsify if any concrete uniform procedure meets the $N^{1.1+o(1)}$ local work and $N^{0.2+o(1)}$ space bounds on a positive-density family of hard instances.
\item Residual core analysis after affine/XOR elimination on Tseitin formulas conjoined with an arbitrary non-affine core $Q(B)$; quantify description size and evaluation cost under black-box $A$. The high-treewidth affine shell disappears linearly, but $Q(B)$ can remain an arbitrary NP-complete instance of parent size $\Theta(N)$.
\item Circuit-simulation overhead via Pippenger--Fischer before transferring any conditional decision-circuit lower bound; falsify if the simulation exhausts the margin to the FLMV region.
\item Near-boundary UNSAT certificate portfolios for the seed $T_A\le N^{1.802+o(1)}$, $S_A=N^{o(1)}$; construct minimally unsatisfiable exact-(2,2)-CNF-3 instances forcing aggregate verification outside the van Melkebeek window via $\sum m_i^d\le M^{d-1}V$.
\item Black-box query complexity lower bounds for discovering useful semantic children on expander Tseitin instances.
\item Adaptive reconfiguration and state-dependent overhead accounting when the solver $A$ is adaptive or memoizing.
\end{enumerate}
No mechanism has been established as a fully charged universal transformation.


\section{Moving-Guard Physical Coefficient Firewall}
\PROVED\ (direct compiler).
An exact boundary relation represented by forbidden rows can be
compiled to proper 3CNF by a fresh ternary implication chain per row.
For width $w=\alpha\log_2N+O(1)$, direct guard materialization costs
$N^{\alpha+o(1)}$ physical bits.  Thus logarithmic width must always
carry its coefficient, retained-source size, relation acquisition,
compiler, and recursive re-entry ledger.  The restricted four-end
consecutive-chain construction has $\alpha=0$ and a square-root
retained suffix, so it survives; no comparable arbitrary-SAT order or
summary constructor is known.  A compact circuit may beat the direct
truth table, but its construction, evaluation and Tseitin re-entry are
then separate charged obligations.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-VALID-STATE-FIREWALL285: original signed hypergraph payload isolation versus answer distance (31 October 2026, 16:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED; independently checked finite both-label controls; global ARC and target FLMV transport OPEN.}
Under a fixed Elias-gamma header and one-sign-plus-three-binary-original-identifier source-clause encoding for D\"ocker's canonical duplicate-free monotone $(2+,2-)$ language, \emph{every single payload bit change violates the source grammar}: a sign flip spoils the two-each polarity quotas, while an identifier flip spoils the original name's occurrence quota or goes out of range. Nevertheless the verified U198 UNSAT and V198 SAT original sources, and their disjoint conjunctions with any $k$ copies of verified Y6 SAT, have \emph{exactly two} different input payload bits, the same valid header, and strictly increasing encoded source lengths. Every length-two Hamming trajectory between the opposite labels passes through a malformed input. The source input is read-only: such a path is an \emph{offline} source-code distance, not a physically mandatory execution or a lower bound on writable state activity. On a valid accepting input, deterministic exact rejection of every malformed one-bit payload neighbor requires probing every source payload bit, giving only the known $\Omega(N)$ SAT-side indexed source-reading floor, never a superpolynomial time bound or a general UNSAT scan floor. For $k=0,1,4,16$ the independently checked source length is 6632, 6832, 7432, 11010 bits; each pair has distance two. The producer symbolically certified 63,552 one-bit malformed mutations across both endpoints, with direct sampled reparses; the no-import verifier passed 789 assertions and independently replayed the original U198 64-boundary-row UNSAT certificate and V198 original-witness check. A 512-node UP-DPLL comparison yielded SAT on Y6 and UNKNOWN on both U198/V198, never a fabricated UNSAT result. Original clause-indexed $S_j/U_j$ targets, inversions, $SS/SU/US/UU$ masks, coherent $0^n/1^n$, flip trails and logical XOR reside in the local diagnostic archive. The separate physical FLMV ledger includes source-index reads, head/jump/index resets, selector discovery, retries, output and peak live work/index storage. This is an audit of \emph{representation and quantifier typing}, not a constructed $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ and not a target numerical FLMV bound. Code, independent verifier and compact raw JSON are in
\path{experiments/arc/lspv1_285/} and
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-31_1600_LSPV1_ARC_VALID_STATE_FIREWALL285_raw.json}.
Full role-owned proof:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-31_1600_LSPV-1_ARC_VALID_STATE_FIREWALL285.tex}.

% LSPV1_ARC_PAYLOAD_SENSITIVITY284_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-PAYLOAD-SENSITIVITY284: SAT-side source-read floor and certified UNSAT early-NO escape (31 October 2026, 15:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared compact source encoding for arbitrary fixed deterministic total indexed-input deciders, with external mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL independent both-label original-source controls; OPEN hereditary ARC, quantitative same-machine FLMV transfer and P versus NP.}
For the canonical NP-complete duplicate-free proper clausewise-monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) language, use one clause sign bit and three source identifiers of width $w=\lceil\log_2 n\rceil$ per original clause, with self-delimiting $n$ header and $m=4n/3$. Every one-bit payload mutation of every valid signed target source violates the exact original positive/negative occurrence quotas or proper clause grammar. Consequently every fixed uniform deterministic total indexed-language SAT decider must query all $P(n)=m(1+3w)=\Theta(N)$ read-only payload bits on \emph{every valid SAT source}; otherwise it would falsely accept a one-bit malformed input by an indistinguishable source-query transcript. This yields an exact $\Omega(N)$ read-time floor but \emph{no} forced writable-space or physical-XOR floor.
The analogous all-source UNSAT claim is \textbf{FALSIFIED}: the disjoint union of the independently certified 198-variable original UNSAT $U_{198}$ with arbitrarily many valid six-variable $Y_6$ SAT modules remains a valid UNSAT target source. One uniform exact decider reads only the $O(\log n)$-bit header and its fixed 264-clause original $U_{198}$ prefix, returns NO even if any unread suffix is malformed, and uses exhaustive exact fallback otherwise. The worst-case fallback is not polynomial.
A source-authenticated producer and a no-import independent checker tested 13,280 one-bit payload mutants, all malformed; the full independent checker passed 291,212 tests including 277,840 four-way indexed clause-pair modes. The original $U_{198}$ is UNSAT by a 64-boundary-row complete original-source six-module refutation; the matched canonical $V_{198}$ has a complete 198-bit original-clause SAT witness; $Y_6$ has 20 exhaustively verified models. $U/V$ have the same 351 shared-same-sign, 786 opposite-sign and 33,579 disjoint clause pairs and matching binary/Gray count-XOR sweep totals despite opposite labels. Reads are charged as source acquisition, not presumed physical work writes. Full report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-31_1500_LSPV-1_ARC_PAYLOAD_SENSITIVITY284.tex};

% LSPV1_ARC_INDEX_RECHARGE283_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-INDEX-RECHARGE283: model-matched amortization of native FLMV index-tape erasures (31 October 2026, 14:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted native-machine operational lemma; COMPUTATIONAL independent both-label controls; OPEN hereditary ARC, numerical target FLMV transfer, and P versus NP.}
On an actual fixed-tape finite-alphabet FLMV indexed machine, indexed jumps atomically erase all index-tape contents in one transition. Role-4 ARC-XOR-INDEX-RESET281 already proved the correct local $D_{\rm all}\le qT+E$ and falsified an unchanged one-step popcount/k bound. The new full-trace recharge lemma counts actual physical index \emph{one-bit} creations $P$, bulk-erased one-bits $E$, and opening index population $\Phi_a$, obtaining $E_{a,b}\le\Phi_a+P_{a,b}$ and, for any chronological checkpoint partition, $X_{a,b}\le2q(b-a)+\Phi_a$. Hence every complete initially blank-index run satisfies $T\ge X/(2q)$, with a machine-specific constant $q$ of ordinary changed physical bit cells per native step. A single index reset may still erase $h$ bits in one step; the coefficient $2$ is asymptotically sharp for one local write port by $h$ charged writes and one reset. Head-address encodings and lazy logical clause states are not counted as physical writes; arbitrary SAT runtime lower bounds do not follow.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} A separately written verifier passed 963,201 assertions on 478,499 toy-machine states and original source-indexed SAT/UNSAT controls. Canonical Y6 has 20 exhaustively verified models, U198 is UNSAT by previously independently replayed source-module certificate, and matched V198 has a fresh complete witness with original-clause histogram $(0,158,80,26)$. Both U198/V198 have 351 same-sign-shared and 786 opposite-sign-shared original clause pairs and the same assignment-sweep logical XOR totals 1254 (binary counts plus assignment) or 990 (Gray counts plus assignment). The comparative signed nonmonotone W1 is exhaustively UNSAT. No target hereditary child compiler or quantitative same-machine SAT-to-D\"ocker transfer exists.
\textbf{AUDIT AND PROVENANCE.} Primary FLMV Section 2.1 PDF visually inspected, current verified LSPV-4/5 and prior role reports credited. Full report:

% LSPV1_H22_DANGEROUS_HITSET282_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 H22-DANGEROUS-HITSET282: two-edge dangerous-clause transversal for source-authenticated matching SAT terminals (31 October 2026, 13:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted via Edmonds, external mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL independently checked same-source YES/NO; OPEN hereditary target ARC, matched FLMV transfer and P versus NP.}
For every valid duplicate-free proper monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) source, the positive original-clause dual is a labelled cubic multigraph. Let $q$ count negative original clauses whose three positive edges are pairwise disjoint, and $\tau$ be the minimum number of original variable-edge labels intersecting all those dangerous clauses. A positive-perfect-matching witness is source SAT if and only if no dangerous triple lies wholly in the matching, equivalently if some choice of one forbidden label per dangerous clause leaves a perfect matching (at most $3^q$ calls). If the positive dual is bridgeless and $\tau\le2$, Edmonds' uniform one-third edge-marginal polytope guarantees a matching avoiding the hitting labels, so a deterministic polynomial source-verified SAT terminal exists. The symmetric negative-dual theorem also holds. The exact signed quota gives $q\le2\tau$, and the bound is attained by a new valid connected nonbipartite $12$-variable example with $q=4$, $\tau=2$, chosen hitset $\{1,8\}$, safe matching $\{2,6,9,12\}$, and $228$ fully enumerated SAT models. A different valid nine-variable source has $18$ SAT models despite \emph{no} safe positive matching, so failure of the terminal is never an UNSAT certificate.
The independently replayed original D\"ocker $198$-variable UNSAT source has $q_+=q_-=126$, six dual bridges per sign, and a complete $3666$-node six-module plus $64$-boundary-row source refutation. The independent audit checked $9280$ full assignment evaluations including corruption rechecks ($5184$ unique base assignments), $1600$ original-clause-pair modes, $192$ S/U signed occurrences, $214$ resolution-cube valuations and four corruption rejections. Four small SAT controls required only $22$ baseline UP-DPLL nodes. Full Python source/raw/certificate is available in a validated downloadable conversation ZIP; Library transfer reported \texttt{container\_session\_unavailable}; GitHub contains verified compact LaTeX/code/JSON and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-31_1300_LSPV-1_H22_DANGEROUS_HITSET282.tex};

\path{results/summaries/arc/2026-10-31_1300_LSPV1_H22_DANGEROUS_HITSET282_summary.json}.

% LSPV1_MONOTONE_QUOTA_CUBE281_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 MONOTONE-QUOTA-CUBE281: signed partial-cube quota subsumption and formula-independent first-shell signatures (31 October 2026, 12:26 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary restricted; independent mathematical review pending;
COMPUTATIONAL independent both-label audit; OPEN hereditary target ARC, FLMV transport, and P versus NP.}
For each valid proper duplicate-free monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) source with $n$ original names and each coherent partial cube assigning $t$ TRUE and $f$ FALSE, let $q_{r,-}$ and $q_{r,+}$ count original negative/positive clauses with exactly $r$ source literals not already fixed FALSE.
Exact two-per-sign occurrence accounting gives
\[
q_{0,-}=2(t-2n/3)+q_{2,-}+2q_{3,-},\qquad
q_{0,+}=2(f-2n/3)+q_{2,+}+2q_{3,+}.
\]
Here each $q_0$ counts \emph{original falsified clause IDs}. Hence exceeding $t>2n/3$ or $f>2n/3$ already falsifies source clauses and cannot reject any additional partial cube beyond complete original-clause checking; the converse fails by pinning just one three-variable clause false.
Every canonical source has original truth-count four-bucket signature $(2n/3,0,0,2n/3)$ at both coherent endpoints and $(2n/3-2,2,2,2n/3-2)$ after any single original-variable flip from either endpoint. Thus \emph{coarse first-shell histograms alone} cannot classify the SAT label, without making claims about source-indexed algorithms.
An original Appendix-C $U198$ retains its previously independently replayed complete UNSAT refutation; valid occurrence-swapped $V198$ has a verified complete SAT witness with truth-count histogram $(0,158,80,26)$. Both share all endpoints and 396 singleton flip signatures. Y6/Z9 exhaustively audited 729/19,683 partial cubes. Independent no-import verifier checked 278,104 original source-clause-pair modes, both-label witness/original-clause evidence and 4 malicious raw corruptions; baseline DPLL on large sources ended UNRESOLVED at its 1,000-node cap, not UNSAT. Physical raw and source-tape costs are charged as experimental events, not native FLMV exponents.
The full original-source S/U inversions, pair modes, resolvent-forbidden cubes, producer, distinct independent verifier and four case-separated raw files were published with exact GitHub readback; the over-1MB combined raw file required split-file readback.

% LSPV1_MONOTONE_PORT_CUT280_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 MONOTONE-PORT-CUT280: two-port congruence and a source-faithful separator obstruction (31 October 2026, 11:58 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL full original-source both-label audit; OPEN hereditary canonical-target ARC, FLMV transfer, and P versus NP.}
The revised target is every valid instance of the NP-complete proper duplicate-free clausewise-monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) language, with malformed encodings rejected. For a connected canonical source, boundary \(B\) of \(b\ge1\) original names, \(h\) all-boundary clauses, and \(k\) nonempty private primal components, count original positive and negative boundary ports \(e_i^+,e_i^-\) into component \(i\). Exact signed occurrence quotas yield
\[
3m_i^+=2|P_i|+e_i^+,\qquad
3m_i^-=2|P_i|+e_i^-,\qquad
e_i^+\equiv e_i^-\pmod3.
\]
Each connected nonempty component consumes at least two boundary ports, so
\(2k\le 4b-3h\), in particular \(k\le2b\).
At most \(4b\) original clauses touch \(B\). Hence for every coherent boundary assignment a component retains at least \(2n/(3b)-2\) \emph{unchanged original clauses}. On an explicit \(N=\Theta(n\log n)\)-bit original-source representation any \emph{source-clause-retaining} child has size \(\Omega(N/(b\log N))\); a logarithmic boundary cannot yield \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) children on all connected target inputs by this raw ordered-separator emitter. This \emph{does not exclude semantic compression, certified terminal policies, arbitrary algorithms, or prove an unconditional time bound}. An explicit infinite \emph{canonical} family attains \(k=2b\) with \(n=9b,m=12b\), but is readily SAT and therefore is not a hardness witness. The previously verified mixed-sign \(k=3b+1\) constructions remain valid outside the sign-monochromatic theorem.

A dependency-free original-source audit checked five sharp sizes (\(b=1,2,3,4,7\)), 1245 alternate cuts, all original S/U-inverse and SS/SU/US/UU modes, signed-resolution forbidden cubes, eight malformed controls and independent complete SAT/UNSAT target labels. The original D\"ocker U198 (198 variables, 264 clauses) is independently refuted via six source-authenticated 42-clause modules and 64 boundary assignments: permitted row counts \((48,56,56,48,56,48)\) have empty common intersection with the 48 globally permitted rows. The matched canonical V198 has a replayed complete SAT witness with \(N_0,N_1,N_2,N_3=(0,158,80,26)\), although both coherent all-zero and all-one assignments fail. Both 2500-node-capped direct UP-DPLL controls reported UNKNOWN; the fully charged algorithmic performance is not improved by the supplied decomposition. Python instrumented counts are not native FLMV transitions. Full source-indexed raw SHA-256:
\texttt{a98c0fb6733180c6a15d4dcb7b0cc5d484d0c57639d5f9400034d14b3402dd23}.
The readback-authenticated research proof, compact checker, summary and handoff are at

\path{research/coordination/2026-10-31_1158_LSPV1_MONOTONE_PORT_CUT280_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.
The complete raw/producer archive is a documented local fallback pending full GitHub mirroring.
The exact OR-of-ANDs separator SAT join and its dual UNSAT join remain mandatory; raw pinned children generally exit the canonical target and any re-entry must be costed. No general-target \(\Phi(A)\), near-unit SAT-to-target numerical FLMV reduction or universal complexity separation is established. Both \(P=NP\) and \(P\ne NP\) remain \OPEN.

% LSPV1_ARC_ORDERED_SEPARATOR_STAGE276_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-ORDERED-SEPARATOR-STAGE276: charged certified separator decomposition (31 October 2026, 10:12 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted parent-stage lemma; KNOWN THEOREM TRANSFER for separator semantics; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; CONDITIONAL arbitrary-guest native simulation; universal ARC OPEN.}
For a source-recognizable block-ordered proper 3CNF with interface $b\le\tfrac1{10}\log_2N+O(1)$, at most $N^{1/2+o(1)}$ private blocks of physical length $N^{1/2+o(1)}$, and every original clause touching at most one private block, there are authenticated ordinary proper-3CNF children obeying
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(F)\ \Longleftrightarrow\
 \bigvee_{\sigma\in\{0,1\}^{b}}\ 
 \bigwedge_{i\text{ populated}}\operatorname{SAT}(G_{\sigma,i}).
\]
Failed separator candidates, complete original-source scans, $O(N^{1/2+o(1)})$ retained block offsets, repeated indexed block reads, proper-3CNF serialization, source provenance, Boolean joins and one live child are charged. The \emph{parent stage} has time $N^{11/10+o(1)}$, peak writable bits $N^{1/2+o(1)}$, at most $N^{3/5+o(1)}$ actual children each of $N^{1/2+o(1)}$ bits. A complete arbitrary-$A$ FLMV indexed-guest simulation is not inferred from restricted tape interfaces, and a rejected source's compulsory full-parent $A(F)$ fallback defeats the uniform recurrence under DELAY wrappers. No all-input hereditary $\Phi$ is obtained. Same-source three-block SAT control permits only interface $(1,0)$ although coherent $0^n,1^n$ both fail; its fourth-block extension is UNSAT while every block is separately SAT. Thus the OR-of-ANDs and its exact UNSAT dual are mandatory; independent block SAT bits or wrong joins are invalid.

The 15-source computational suite has 8 SAT/7 UNSAT, 12 recognized (6/6), 97 physically serialized source-authenticated SAT/UNSAT children (64/33), 1344 original clause-pair extreme modes, 182 resolution blockers, 159 verified complete projection cubes, four independent tamper rejections and 97 byte-exact independent streaming reconstructions. Baseline selected logical touches 10435 versus 69704 for the certified controller; matched Python wall time 13.243 ms versus 164.320 ms. \emph{No computational speedup or native FLMV machine bound was observed.} S/U local all-literal extrema, inversions, coherent endpoints and source resolution remain adversarial controls, not a primary clause-extreme route. Full raw and Python code are archived locally after GitHub/Library transfer failures; compact public proof and checker were read back at

% LSPV1_ARC_QUERY_SPECTRUM275_BEGIN
\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-QUERY-SPECTRUM275: adaptive heterogeneous DELAY query-cost identity (31 October 2026, 09:35 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent mathematical review pending;}
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} finite original-source SAT/UNSAT controls;
the arbitrary-decider ARC edge and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \OPEN.
For a boolean-answer-driven, code-opaque residual-SAT controller executing a completed sequence of ordinary-SAT queries of lengths $M_i$, all query transcripts are unchanged by a correct $M^D$ DELAY wrapper.
Let $q_b$ count calls in size band $2^b\le M_i<2^{b+1}$ and $B=\lfloor\log_2\max_i M_i\rfloor$.
The fully charged delay-only guest workload has the exact band bounds
\[
\max_b q_b2^{Db}\;\le\; W_D=\sum_i(M_i+1)^D\;
\le\;2^D(B+1)\max_bq_b2^{Db}.
\]
For polynomial query lengths, this yields the sharp exponent of the sequential guest workload up to $o_D(1)$.
It falsifies an opaque ARC architecture that compulsorily calls an arbitrary guest on any $N^{1/2+\epsilon}$ query on infinitely many sources, or on $N^{3/5+\epsilon}$ near-$N^{1/2}$ queries; it does \emph{not} exclude a code-aware compiler that bypasses such calls.
Physical materialization of $N^{3/5}$ children each of length $N^{1/2}$ already costs $N^{11/10}$ writes, saturating the candidate parent-stage exponent.
An independently implemented finite verifier passed 723 checks and rejected three injected corruptions on one seven-of-eight signed-ternary SAT source and its eight-of-eight UNSAT counterpart; 16 separately parsed ordinary Shannon children preserve both labels.
Baseline UP-DPLL used 11 nodes/185 literal probes, while 16 padded-child calls used 140 nodes/3146 guest literal probes plus 1403 output bytes; no speedup or native FLMV timing is claimed.
The complete local four-page LaTeX/PDF, Python producer, independent checker, 80 KB indexed raw record and ZIP have verified SHA-256 checks.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-HORN-INTERFACE-HELLY275: sharp meet-closed module compatibility core (31 October 2026, 09:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, external mathematical review pending;} \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM TRANSFER} for Horn least-model closure and source-consistent separator joins, \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} for exact small proper-3CNF controls.
For a fully authenticated original-source module decomposition
$F=\bigwedge_{i=1}^{q}H_i(B,U_i)$ with disjoint private variables,
$k=|B|$, and every existential boundary projection
$P_i=\{b:\exists u_i\,H_i(b,u_i)\}$ certified closed under
coordinatewise conjunction, $\operatorname{SAT}(F)$ holds exactly when
$\bigcap_iP_i\ne\varnothing$. If UNSAT, at most $k+1$ original
modules already have empty intersection; the bound is sharp for
$k$ positive-unit supports and one all-negative support.
This is classical Horn forward chaining, \emph{not} an arbitrary
SAT lower bound. Pairwise projection compatibility is insufficient,
while arbitrary non-Horn supports may require $2^k$ modules,
as already distinguished by LSPV-3's affine/arbitrary Helly work.
Authenticating the modules, discovering their boundary,
constructing/proving projections and verifying any source proof
are \emph{charged}; the short semantic core supplies neither a
cost-free policy selector nor a power-short ordinary-SAT child.

Four SAT and four UNSAT matched proper-original-3CNF controls passed
3,219 independent source/witness/projection/proof/ordinary-child checks,
2,096 indexed extreme compatibility modes, 45 original-source
resolution proof steps and 16 independently checked both-label
Shannon child encodings; four corrupted evidence variants were rejected.
At $k=4$, one UNSAT parent has 191-byte DIMACS encoding and 172/152-byte
ordinary children, so no $N^{1/2}$ physical contraction is demonstrated.
The selected host event ledger on that source records 27,100
heterogeneous augmented events versus 15,429 baseline DPLL literal
probes (479 nodes), \emph{not} comparable native FLMV time/space.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-UNKNOWN-EXPONENT-EPOCH274: polynomial-generation reset without guest-exponent advice (31 October 2026, 07:54 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted, external mathematical review pending;}
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} original-source and abstract tape controls.
The previous \texttt{ARC-EPOCH-TAPES173} generation-tag simulator required
a supplied fixed polynomial upper bound for its $C\log N$ epoch width;
\texttt{ARC-UNKNOWN-HORIZON-SCRUB-REFEREE235} correctly observed that a
physical full-tape reset can be expensive in the FLMV largest-visited-position
space gauge.  For any one fixed uniform polynomial guest $A$ with
\emph{unknown finite} $(K,L)$ and polynomially many sequential ordinary-SAT
child calls, a \emph{uniform exponent-oblivious} epoch width
$B(N)=\lceil(\log_2(N+2))^2\rceil+2$ is eventually larger than the binary
length of every guest-call and indexed-tape erasure counter, since the total
number of such events is $N^{O_A(1)}$.  Tagged cells yield exactly fresh
logical guest work/index tapes without a full scrub; if a generation ever
exhausts its range at finitely many small parent lengths, the implementation
clears the specific tape only at a transition where its \emph{logical}
contents become blank.  The dense physical block layout multiplies guest
writable-tape high-water position by only $(\log N)^2$; tag comparisons,
unchanged writes, address arithmetic, and generation updates remain charged.

This removes \emph{known exponent advice for reset/erasure counts only}.
A full indexed-guest simulation with $N^{o(1)}$ transition overhead is
proved here only under the additional \emph{restricted} hypothesis that
\emph{all} guest index-tape head extents are $O(\log M)$; it does not
cover arbitrary correct $A$ with far index-tape heads, suspended overlapping
guests, or a missing source-authenticated ordinary-child emitter.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 15 original CNFs (nine SAT, six UNSAT),
964 SS/SU/US/UU original clause-pair modes, 87 authenticated resolution
blockers, 30 physically serialized Shannon children (14 SAT and 16 UNSAT)
with both-label OR joins, 450 fresh-vs-tagged observed events, and 3,737
independently checked assertions.  Standard UP-DPLL visited 65 states
and inspected 967 literals; the guest-child experiments visited 123 states
and inspected 921 literals \emph{plus} serialized output and tagged-tape
traffic.  The 1,032/151,367 baseline/guarded selected-event proxies have
different charged units and are not native FLMV tape measurements.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-BOUNDARY-ZERO-COVER273: exact adaptive two-sided source-query transcripts (31 October 2026, 07:24 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent review pending.}
For a syntactically authenticated proper-3CNF pair with a shared
$k$-bit boundary and independently indexed membership pins
$s_a,t_a$ ($a<q=2^k$), the source is SAT exactly when
$\bigvee_{a<q}(s_a\wedge t_a)$ holds.
Any partial adaptive source-bit query transcript is a \emph{universally
valid SAT certificate} precisely when it has observed
$s_a=t_a=1$ at some common index; it is a \emph{universally valid
UNSAT certificate} precisely when it has observed at least one zero
pin at every index. The converses use explicit opposite-label
completions of unqueried source bits. Thus every negative transcript
needs at least $q$ informative indexed reads, whereas a sequential
source-aware exact algorithm uses at most $2q$ pin reads and
$O(k)$ scratch \emph{after charged grammar authentication}.
This is a restricted instance of known decision-tree certificate
principles, \emph{not} an unrestricted SAT query or space lower bound.
The predecessor MAIN228 already established a related one-way reusable
summary floor; the new lemma emphasizes two-sided exact decision
transcripts and the source-rereading escape.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Exhaustive tests of all $256^2=65{,}536$
source-mask pairs gave 58,975 SAT and 6,561 UNSAT;
12 source-indexed 13-variable, 24-clause proper CNFs (six SAT,
six UNSAT) passed 98,304 full-assignment trials, 13,248 indexed
$SS/SU/US/UU$ checks and exact complement/flip checks.
Six UNSAT controls have independently checked 15-node
\emph{original-source} resolution refutations; SAT controls have
checked coherent source witnesses.
The source-query model is not the native FLMV indexed-input
Turing machine: full access-head/blank/guest and simultaneous
space costs remain unbounded by this experiment. The 700KB raw
dataset and expanded Python implementations are in a verified local
reproducibility archive; a compact executable, source-wise summary,
and the formal report are GitHub-published with readback:

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-CHILD-SCAN-AMORTIZATION272: hereditary cold-source-rescan gate (31 October 2026, 07:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending;}
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} original-indexed controls; the all-input universal
ARC compiler and P versus NP remain \textbf{OPEN}.
For any claimed hereditary time-exponent successor
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\}$, $0<\lambda<1$, with
$K_*=\max\{\alpha,\gamma/(1-\lambda)\}<1/\lambda$ and
$\alpha<1+\gamma$, an architecture that at \emph{every} successor
level has worst-case sources producing $\Omega(N^\gamma)$ guest-child
attempts, each \emph{really} rereading $\Omega(N)$ original-input bits,
is incompatible with the recurrence.  Physical source reads alone require
$\Omega(N^{1+\gamma})$ time, but iterating the recurrence from any unknown
finite initial $K$ yields a successor upper exponent strictly below
$1+\gamma$.  For $(\alpha,\gamma,\lambda)=(11/10,3/5,1/2)$
the cold read floor is $8/5$, while the conditional fixed point is $6/5$;
once parent $K<2$ the asserted child successor exponent is $<8/5$.
This falsifies the repeatedly \emph{cold-scanning} compiler architecture,
not arbitrary SAT algorithms.  Source-aware recognition, cached one-pass
sharing, reduced child multiplicity, changed policies, checked terminals
and other paid access designs explicitly escape the hypothesis.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} An independently implemented Python checker
passed 2,618 assertions over 12 original CNFs (7 SAT, 5 UNSAT):
185 indexed signed occurrences, 732 source-clause SS/SU/US/UU
modes, 76 original-resolution prohibited cubes and 48 physically
serialized and reparsed ordinary children with correct both-label
cofactor joins, source endpoints, inversion and flip trails.
Standard UP-DPLL explored 51 nodes and 878 selected events; the
cold-scan guest-controller explored 53 guest nodes and 6,866
nonstandard selected events, including 2,832 executed original
character reads, 894 child output characters and 740 cofactor
literal visits.  These differing host-event profiles are
\emph{not} FLMV tape-step or simultaneous-space upper bounds;
the audit intentionally retains all child transcripts.
An exact rational recurrence checker covers initial exponents
$K_0=2,4,8,16,32,100$ and strict collisions after parent
levels $1,2,4,5,6,7$ respectively.
The audited report, two standalone programs, full original-indexed
raw data and five-role handoff were published with exact GitHub readback:

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-PARITY-EXPOSURE270: exact source-bit disclosure and cached both-label ordinary children (31 October 2026, 05:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
For canonical signed proper-3CNF $F_{m,b}$, with $4m+8$ source clauses enforcing
$x_0=x_m=0$ and $x_{i-1}\oplus x_i=b_i$, SAT holds exactly when
$\bigoplus_i b_i=0$; SAT members have eight models and UNSAT members zero.
Every varying bit of its fixed-width physical source encoding depends on at most
one $b_i$. Therefore deterministic adaptive \emph{cold source-bit} decision-tree
complexity of the SAT label, and a parity-selected bit in a physically constant-size
ordinary SAT child, is \emph{exactly} $m$ on the declared promise.
A fully charged source scan and one persistent parity bit, however, amortize
repeated child reads: the naive $q m$ repeated cold-source tariff is invalid.
The family is syntactically recognizable and decidable in $N\operatorname{polylog}N$
time and $O(\log N)$ working bits; a canonical both-label child exists only
under the recognized grammar, and no arbitrary-input short-child compiler follows.
Independent verification passed 8,859 checks on all 126 signed sources for
$m=1,\ldots,6$ (63 SAT/63 UNSAT), including 312 detailed original occurrences,
5,488 SS/SU/US/UU pair modes, coherent endpoints, complements, flip trails
and authenticated resolvent cubes. A baseline UP-DPLL used 1,479 nodes;
selected host event totals were 258,928 baseline versus 104,058 recognized
controller, \emph{not} native FLMV time or space measurements.
The full verified report, producer, independent checker and raw dataset are
in \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-31_0500_LSPV-1_ARC_PARITY_EXPOSURE270.tex},

and \path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-31_0500_LSPV1_ARC_PARITY_EXPOSURE270_raw.json}.
\textbf{OPEN}: The uniform hereditary all-input $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ with both
labels and complete same-machine FLMV charges; $P$ versus $NP$ remains OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-ADAPTIVE-COFACTOR-MASS269: universal raw-cofactor survival invariant for deterministic adaptive source-aware branch trees (31 October 2026, 04:10 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} for clause falsification by uniform complete assignments and exact Shannon polarity; \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical referee pending} for a tree-wide mass identity. For every proper original width-$w$ CNF $F$ with $m$ clauses and every finite deterministic \emph{source-aware, adaptively selected, early-terminal} binary branch tree whose coherent leaves partition the complete assignment cube, let $R(\rho)$ count original clauses not yet satisfied by leaf cube $\rho$, including now-empty clauses. If $u_j(\rho)$ is the number of unset literals in a surviving source clause, then
\[
\sum_{\ell}2^{-|\rho_\ell|}R(\rho_\ell)=
\sum_{j=1}^m2^{-|C_j|}
+\sum_\ell2^{-|\rho_\ell|}
\sum_{j:C_j\text{ survives }\rho_\ell}(1-2^{-u_j(\rho_\ell)}).
\]
In particular $\max_\ell R(\rho_\ell)\ge m/2^w$. Thus a \emph{pure raw original-clause cofactor tree}, keeping all surviving clause records at every leaf, cannot make \emph{all} raw children $o(m)$ clauses merely by arbitrary adaptive pivots. This is classical averaging and a restricted source-accounting obstruction, \emph{not} computational hardness: the large residual may contain an empty source clause and be an immediate, cheap original-source UNSAT terminal. Structural solvers, guard-based pruning, arbitrary representation changes and physical post-simplification children escape this \emph{raw-count} hypothesis.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Two independent JavaScript implementations exhaustively verified ten source CNFs (8 SAT, 2 UNSAT), including H7/H8, XOR both labels, signed renaming, Horn, dual-Horn, Krom and dense seeded CNF. Sixty adaptive source-aware decision-tree controls partitioned 284 leaf cubes, with exact dyadic tree mass identity, 756 four-way original indexed SS/SU/US/UU pair modes, 448 triple modes, 16,896 flip-clause updates, 176 occurrence inversion records and 92 original-resolution prohibited cubes. Same-source SAT and UNSAT labels were independently enumerated. A fixed-policy source-aware controller searched 78 nodes and recorded 2,525 selected operations versus 44 nodes and 816 selected operations for standard UP-DPLL, with no demonstrated speedup or native-FLMV comparability. Shallow guest controls \emph{materialized and reparsed} thirteen ordinary ASCII-DIMACS children, including two globally UNSAT sources; output length 441 characters total, 245 additional source-literal construction reads, 183 guest-literal reads. The full original-indexed raw JSON (488,349 characters, including 15 independently checked byte-exact physical children: 10 SAT and 5 UNSAT), both executable checkers, and formal report were committed with exact readback. Large retained host arrays prevent any claim of $N^{1/2}$ FLMV space.

\textbf{CORPUS RECONCILIATION.} Reuses authenticated predecessor LSPV-1 AFFINE-CUBE-DUAL268/PRIME-CHAIN267/PIVOT-ANTICHAIN265 and peer LSPV-2 LAZY-INTERFACE-ACCESS269, LSPV-3 INTERFACE-SOURCE-SELECTOR-GATE272, LSPV-4 PARETO269 and LSPV-5 MAIN239. Unlike the affine-family-specific model-cover obstruction 261 or LSPV-4's exact one-pivot cofactor count, this result is an \emph{arbitrary adaptive tree} accounting identity with no inference to unrestricted SAT cost. Symbolic DELAY wrappers still falsify mandatory full-parent guest calls. The affine MCSP small-bias theorem remains a restricted no-go, not a SAT lower bound. The actual simultaneous FLMV exclusion and the unconstructed ARC recurrence remain distinct.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-AFFINE-CUBE-DUAL268: certified affine residual cubes and exact pin-descriptor admission (31 October 2026, 03:10 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (linear-algebra consistency duality and XOR truth-table encodings); \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted, independent mathematical referee pending}. For an authenticated original proper-3CNF made from disjoint-private XOR components with shared boundary $B$, indexed source rows $V\in\mathbb F_2^{q\times b}$, and pins $r$, every boundary-only coherent source cube $\rho$ is UNSAT exactly when some $w\ne0$ annihilates the remaining free columns $V_U$ and satisfies $w^\top(r+V_J\rho)=1$. This is a sound source-scoped parity-combination rejection certificate with an exact converse, not an unrestricted SAT shortcut. The common-source pin-only decision $\operatorname{SAT}(F_{V,r})\Leftrightarrow(r=0^q\lor r=1^q)$ is correct \emph{for every pin vector} exactly when all rows of $V$ are the same nonzero vector; $V$ with different rows admits mixed-pin SAT and same-pin UNSAT counterexamples. For identical nonzero rows and equal pins, minimal boundary blockers are exactly the $2^{|\operatorname{supp}(v)|-1}$ wrong-parity full-support cubes. A SAT parent can have an UNSAT boundary cube, requiring scope guards. The earlier LSPV-2/4 canonical identical-parity pin-presence descriptor itself decides both SAT and UNSAT once fully source-authenticated, so MAIN238's restricted arbitrary-guest virtual-child composition remains correct but the guest call is optional; this does not close the all-input or hereditary ARC requirement.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Complete matrix/right-hand-side/cube controls (full for $b\le3,q\le3$, deterministic sampled slice for $b=3,q=4$) checked 1181 matrices, 13216 right-hand sides, 264672 residual cubes, including 213693 blocked cubes, 50979 extendible cubes and 4553 falsifications of the unrestricted two-pin descriptor. Six original proper 3CNF sources (3 SAT/3 UNSAT) and three adverse controls (renamed SAT, H7 SAT, H8 UNSAT) passed independently. Fully retained original source S/U targets, their inversions, SS/SU/US/UU pair flags, complements, endpoint counts, flip trails and authenticated affine blockers were checked: 8884 pair modes, 69632 independent complete source assignments and four explicit guarded dual witnesses. Baseline DPLL selected event work was 17230 on the six sources, versus 742096 selected work for the template-discovery controller, including 12509 rejected candidates and 727704 candidate-generated literal events. No native FLMV speedup or simultaneous memory bound follows. The code, 547783-character original-indexed raw JSON, independent GF(2) checker and checker output were committed and verified by GitHub readback.
\textbf{CORPUS RECONCILIATION.} Prior PRIME-CHAIN-GUARD267, PIVOT-ANTICHAIN265, scope-guard and affine-cube controls remain correct at their restricted scope. LSPV-2 PARITY-INDEXED-ACCESSOR268 and LSPV-4 PIN-DISCOVERY-GATE268 are adopted with full source authentication; LSPV-5 MAIN238 supplies a valid restricted total guest-input transfer. Rejected is the incorrect extension of their two-bit descriptor to arbitrary affine row families or to boundary-conditioned residuals. DELAY wrappers still defeat compulsory parent-size guest calls, and affine matrix/rank discovery plus retained space remain charged and unbounded by ARC's proposed $(11/10,1/2)$ setup envelope on arbitrary sources. The absolute FLMV strict point remains $(241/200,11642479/23232400)$.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-PRIME-CHAIN-GUARD267: tight prime-chain local support and paid all-input controller falsifier (31 October 2026, 01:58 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} for propositional resolution and prime implicates;
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}, mathematical referee pending:
for $h\ge2$ source clauses
$(x_1\lor a_1\lor a_2)$,
$(\neg x_{j-1}\lor x_j\lor a_{j+1})$ for $2\le j<h$,
and $(\neg x_{h-1}\lor a_{h+1}\lor a_{h+2})$,
existential projection on $a_1,\dots,a_{h+2}$ is exactly
$R_h=\bigvee_{i=1}^{h+2}a_i$.
$R_h$ is a prime implicate with an $h$-leaf, $(h-1)$-resolution
source proof, and every proof of this \emph{specific clause} using
only those original $h$ clauses must use all $h$: deleting any
one source clause makes the all-zero boundary extendible.
A source-indexed four-clause ternary pin gadget for each $\neg a_i$
produces a complete original-source UNSAT refutation; the unpinned
chain is SAT. This is an easy restricted projection, \emph{not}
a lower bound against general SAT solvers or an all-input ARC successor.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} A no-randomness original-source producer
and independent checker passed $12$ formulas ($8$ SAT, $4$ UNSAT),
$2060$ clause-pair $SS/SU/US/UU$ mode checks,
$142048$ independent full-assignment model evaluations,
$10912$ independent projection evaluations and $49$
source-resolution replay steps.
Both DPLL variants visited $3164$ nodes; the guarded policy paid
$171045$ selected host work events versus $163138$ baseline,
including source recognition/proof traffic, with no observed advantage.
Original clause-local S/U inversions, source occurrence IDs, coherent
$0^n/1^n$, complements, flips and forbidden-cube proofs are retained.
No native FLMV time/space claim follows.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted syntactic re-entry self-loop, referee pending:}
the exact sequential proper-3CNF encoding of the width-$(h+2)$
projection clause $R_h$ introduces $h-1$ bridge variables, and,
with canonical variable renaming and literal ordering, reproduces
the original $F_h$ indexed clause records exactly: $h$ clauses,
$2h+1$ variables and $3h$ literal occurrences both before and after.
Thus this named eliminate-then-reencode policy has zero physical
size reduction even though its source proof and both-label semantics
are sound. The result is \emph{not} a minimum-encoding or
arbitrary-SAT lower bound. A separate checker passed $396$ finite
ordered-clause and signed-renaming assertions for $h=2,\ldots,100$;
symbolic DELAY checks for $K=4,8,16,32$ exclude compulsory completed
parent-scale guest calls under the proposed time recurrence, not
all source-aware compilers.

\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-PIVOT-ANTICHAIN265: exact original-source two-endpoint blocker antichain (30 October 2026, 23:58 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (one-step propositional resolution and subsumption); \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} original-source indexed characterization, independent mathematical review pending; \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} same-source SAT/UNSAT controls. For any coherent source cube $\rho$ leaving pivot $x$ unset, two \emph{original} clauses reduce immediately to contradictory units $x$ and $\neg x$ if and only if $\rho$ extends the literal-falsifying cube of a nontautological original-clause resolvent $R_{jk}$ on $x$. The inclusion-minimal direct blockers at fixed pivot correspond to inclusion-minimal $R_{jk}$ under literal-set inclusion, with width at most four for proper 3-CNF. All deductions are original-source authenticated and scope-guarded; $F\models B_\rho$ but an UNSAT residual cube does not make an SAT parent UNSAT. The converse fails for semantic UNSAT or deeper propagation: the eight-of-eight signed ternary source is UNSAT with no initial source unit clauses. The SAT source $(x\lor a\lor b)\land(x\lor\neg a\lor\neg b)$ has no feasible SS/SU/US/UU extreme pair, but is satisfied at $x=1$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} A deterministic producer and independent checker passed 12 original SAT/UNSAT sources (9/3), 65 source clauses, 191 signed occurrences with S/U and inversion, 736 pair-mode checks, 672 complement checks, 486 source cubes, 567 pivot/cube comparisons, 82 nontautological source resolvents, 72 minimal blockers and 48 independently checked source-proof leaves. Conventional UP-DPLL searched 54 nodes with 800 literal reads; the guarded arm searched 32 nodes with 1004 literal reads, \emph{before} paying 433 source-pair screening probes, candidate checks, derived-clause output and retained storage. These are host-language event proxies, not native FLMV joint time and space. Compact report/checker/summary were published and read back, full raw and expanded scripts retained in a local reproducibility archive:

ARC-INDEXED-GUARD263 verifies assumption-scoped proof transfer only when the proof generator is supplied and charged. LSPV-2 certified affine projection, LSPV-3 guarded cross-restart caches, LSPV-4 index-tape simulation and LSPV-5 output/wrapper tariffs are compatible, but none constructs an all-input physical short-child interface. 
\textbf{OPEN.} The one load-bearing universal arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ hereditary source-authenticated controller, with physically short ordinary SAT children or fully certified terminals and simultaneous charged time/space inside actual FLMV 5.7, remains unconstructed. P versus NP OPEN.



\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-INDEXED-GUARD263: paid indexed proof regeneration and scoped original-source deductions (30 October 2026, 21:56 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted and independent review pending.} For a fixed uniform source-authenticated indexed resolution-proof bit generator $G$ with fully charged $t_G,s_G$ on source $F$, assumption cube $\rho$, and proof size $P$, an exact verifier can check and export the clause $B_U\lor D$ using $(N+P)^{O(1)}(t_G+1)$ time and $s_G+O(\log^2(N+P))$ \emph{temporary} work bits. Here $U$ is the set of actual assumption leaves and $D$ is the checked proof conclusion; no unchecked local contradiction becomes a global UNSAT claim. Full generator acquisition, retained $\rho$, persisted clause/proof, index source/child access, replay, failed trials, output and guest DELAY calls remain charged. The isolated four-clause gate $(\ell\lor\pm y\lor\pm z)$ entails $\ell$ iff all four sign patterns occur, with a three-resolution derivation; this is a known elementary rule, not a new general complexity lower bound.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Expanded local original-clause signed S/U and complement controls checked nine sources (seven SAT, two UNSAT), 448 pair modes, 1504 triple modes, 308 complement-trail checks and ten rejected counterfeit proofs. \paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-SPACE-CAPPED-DOVETAIL262: charged exact portfolio selection (30 October 2026, 20:55 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted schedule theorem (classical dovetailing; independent referee pending), COMPUTATIONAL paired SAT/UNSAT controls, universal arbitrary-decider ARC OPEN.}
For an explicitly enumerable portfolio of $q$ deterministic residual-SAT strategies with \emph{verified} witnesses or source-resolution refutations, sequential space-capped geometric restarts find any eventually successful policy in fewer than $4qt$ charged ideal-executor steps, when the winning full certificate computation takes $t$ steps and fits the declared cap $M$. Failed trials are erased, so only one policy's writable tape is live at a time; policy enumeration, simulator, source/index access, proof checking and physical high-index space must still be counted. The opposite black-box hidden-winner model has a worst-case $\Omega(qt)$ tariff, but this says nothing about arbitrary source-aware or code-inspecting SAT solvers. For a per-child portfolio of $q=N^\delta$ independent sequential guest-policy trials, the \emph{hypothetical} ARC exponent certificate becomes $K'\le\max(\alpha,\gamma+\delta+\lambda K)$ (plus any unabsorbed discovery/source/emulator cost). At $(\alpha,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, its fixed time is $6/5+2\delta$, strictly below the actual FLMV $c=241/200$ only if $\delta<1/400$; its workspace must independently be below $d_*(c)=11642479/23232400$. This is not a construction of the required all-input contractor or an unconditional lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Across 12 exact original CNFs (7 SAT and 5 UNSAT), the instrumented producer recorded 203 candidate restarts, 144 deliberately capped aborted trials with a separately complete fallback, 492 original clause-pair extreme modes, 1,720 triple extreme modes and 48 original-source resolution blockers. The checker replayed exact global assignments, occurrence S/U targets and inversions, coherent endpoints, complements and partial flips. Seven-of-eight proper-3CNF SAT baseline used 31 enumerated clause-visit/branch events versus 124 in the portfolio; eight-of-eight UNSAT used 99 versus 480. These \emph{host-language event counts} do not incorporate all physical checker/output/tape operations and are not native FLMV timing or simultaneous memory bounds. A separate standard unit-propagating DPLL control used 42 total nodes, 608 literal reads and 949 selected work-proxy events across all twelve original sources, compared with 6,004 original-source literal reads and 8,811 selected operations for the restarted verified portfolio after accounting for trial source reads, replay, selected proof-verifier work and proof-node output. The proxies do not constitute matched FLMV bit-level timing. A full-parent fallback to arbitrary $A(F)$ defeats the wanted DELAY-robust exponent contraction. See
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_2055_LSPV-1_ARC_SPACE_CAPPED_DOVETAIL262.tex},

The earlier affine source-cover obstruction, scoped-clause reuse and cube-faithfulness theorems remain restricted correctness/cost guards, not a separation. P versus NP remains OPEN.



\paragraph{LSPV-1 ARC-AFFINE-CUBE-COVER261: source-relative cover versus power-short raw cofactors (30 October 2026, 20:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted syntactic-cover counting, independent referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL paired SAT/UNSAT controls; universal arbitrary-decider ARC OPEN.}
For $b\ge2$ source-disjoint three-variable XOR-parity blocks with alternating target parities, each block consists of four proper signed ternary clauses, each variable occurs twice positive and twice negative, and the original formula $F_b$ has $4^b$ models despite both coherent $0^{3b}$ and $1^{3b}$ assignments failing. For a model-compatible source-variable cube $\rho$, raw syntactic restriction leaving $c$ nonempty original clauses retains at most $2^{\lfloor c/2\rfloor}$ original models; hence any \emph{source-relative model-cover} family of $q$ such children with $c\le s$ satisfies
\[
q\ge 4^b/2^{\lfloor s/2\rfloor}.
\]
With explicit source length $N=\Theta(b\log b)$, children of physical length $\le N^\lambda$ for fixed $\lambda<1$ have $s=o(b)$, forcing $q\ge2^{2b-o(b)}$ \emph{only for this source-cover raw-cofactor architecture}. This rules out a purported polynomial-query syntactic cover compressor, not any semantic simplification, authenticated SAT witness, XOR/2SAT recognizer, or arbitrary SAT decider. The family is an intentionally easy affine adversarial control; no SAT lower bound follows. Each local original-clause S/U and its complement is tracked, all four pairwise extreme modes are infeasible within a block, and this does \emph{not} imply formula UNSAT. The companion UNSAT source adds the four opposite-parity original clauses on one block, with a seven-step source-resolution refutation.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} Full producer checked 729 and 19,683 coherent cubes exhaustively at $b=2,3$, plus 1,800 seeded cubes at $b=4$, and 376/744/1,240 clause-pair extreme modes on matched SAT/UNSAT sources, with zero failures. Standard UP-DPLL used SAT/UNSAT nodes $(5,7),(7,7),(9,7)$ while the fully enumerated source-cover experiment demanded $16,64,256$ ordinary guest calls on each label and 960/5,376/27,648 repeated source-literal reads for the paired sources. These are selected host-language operation counts, not native FLMV running-time or peak-space claims. The detailed report is
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV-1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_PUBLIC.tex};
the independent checker is
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV-1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_compact.js};
public raw summary is
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_2000_LSPV1_ARC_AFFINE_CUBE_COVER261_summary.json}.

\textbf{Integration and OPEN arrow.} LSPV-5's global residual-cover theorem is a correctness gate rather than a polynomial-cost guarantee. LSPV-2's guarded proof export, LSPV-3's direct-cofactor overlap, LSPV-4's realized query/space tariffs, and LSPV-1's paid batch source selector remain restricted, noncontradictory components. The affine MCSP no-go stays restricted. The missing load-bearing theorem is a uniform hereditary source-authenticated arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ that avoids this syntactic cover blowup on easy structural sources \emph{and} works on every other SAT/UNSAT input, with all discovery, source, guest, physical child, failed strategy and simultaneously live writable-tape costs inside the actual FLMV envelope. P versus NP remains OPEN.




\paragraph{ARC-CUBE-FAITHFUL-PROJECTION259: exact contextual encoding and paid certification (30 October 2026, 18:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary source-projection theorem and KNOWN quantified-CNF hardness transfer, independent referee pending.}
For original CNF $F(X)$ and an auxiliary encoding $E(X,Y)$, correctness
on \emph{every} coherent source partial cube $\rho$ is equivalent to
\[
\forall a\in\{0,1\}^X:
 F(a)\longleftrightarrow\exists b\in\{0,1\}^Y E(a,b).
\]
With this pointwise source projection, any source-only clause $D$ satisfies
$E|_\rho\models D\iff F\models B_\rho\vee D$, where $B_\rho$
blocks $\rho$. Root equisatisfiability alone does not license
guarded deductions exported from an encoded residual. In the proper-3CNF
example $F\equiv w$, $E$ has an auxiliary projection
$w\wedge(z\vee x_1\vee\neg x_2)$: the source endpoints
$x_1x_2=00,11$ are extendible, but the interior source pin
$(x_1,x_2,z,w)=(0,1,0,1)$ satisfies $F$ and refutes $E$.
Thus endpoint tests cannot certify safe switching. Via a polynomial
source-preserving reduction from $\forall X\exists Y$-3SAT,
recognizing \emph{generic} all-cube faithfulness for proper ternary
CNF pairs is $\Pi_2^P$-complete even with both root formulas SAT.
This is a certification/discovery obstacle, not an unconditional
arbitrary-SAT lower bound, and syntactically certified encodings escape
generic verification.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} The independently replayed suite tested all 256
signed three-variable CNF subsets, finding 81 universally extendible
and 175 nonuniversal instances; 8,192 exact complete-source
projection checks used 9,218 decision calls and 20,613 DPLL nodes.
Three same-source SAT/SAT-bad, SAT/SAT-faithful and UNSAT/UNSAT
controls exhaustively checked 243 partial cubes each, with respectively
6, 0, 0 contextual mismatches. The original clause-indexed S/U and
four-mode compatibility audits, both coherent endpoint assignments,
complements, intermediate flips and a source-indexed proof blocker are
retained only as adversarial controls. The encoded trial used more
selected DPLL work on both labels; no native FLMV time or peak
writable-space improvement was measured.

\textbf{OPEN:} A single hereditary, fully paid, all-input ordinary
SAT arbitrary-decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ producing physically
power-short ordinary children, maintaining sound source-cube semantics
under all adaptive policy switches, and reaching the same-machine
actual-FLMV simultaneous time--space forbidden region, remains
unconstructed. P versus NP remains OPEN. Verified sources:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_1800_LSPV-1_ARC_CUBE_FAITHFUL_PROJECTION259_PUBLIC.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_1800_LSPV-1_ARC_CUBE_FAITHFUL_PROJECTION259_compact_check.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_1800_LSPV-1_ARC_CUBE_FAITHFUL_PROJECTION259_public_summary.json}.



\paragraph{ARC-ASSUMPTION-SCOPE258: source-authenticated conditional clause reuse (30 October 2026, 17:25 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN propositional deduction/assumption rule with independently
checked source-paired COMPUTATIONAL controls; referee review pending.}
For any original CNF $F$, coherent partial assignment $\rho$, source
blocking clause $B_\rho$ and candidate clause $D$,
\[
F\upharpoonright\rho\models D
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
F\models B_\rho\vee D,\qquad
F\upharpoonright\rho\text{ UNSAT}
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad F\models B_\rho.
\]
A residual policy may export a source-checked conditional clause
to a different policy or restart only after adding its assumption
guard. UNSAT needs a genuine replayable source refutation; a bounded
search timeout is UNRESOLVED. The tiny SAT formula
$(x\vee y)\wedge(x\vee\neg y)$ is UNSAT under $x=0$ and therefore
produces the sound global blocker $x$; unguarded export of the local
units $y$ and $\neg y$ falsely turns the original SAT source into
UNSAT. An original-clause pair sharing same-polarity $x$ and
opposite-polarity $y$ has no SS/SU/US/UU simultaneous extremes,
yet both original clauses are disjunctively SAT when $x=1$.
The full eight signed ternary-clause UNSAT source has minimum UP
split-refutation depth two, so bounded local proof recognition is
sound but incomplete. These are standard logical rules, not a new
algorithm or hardness result.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 256 proper signed three-variable original
formula subsets, 27 coherent cubes and 27 target clauses each:
186,624 independently checked contextual/global entailment
equivalences and 1,492,992 complete valuations. An independent
Node.js checker agrees. On 23 additional original SAT/UNSAT
sources (15 YES, eight NO, with 12 variable/polarity renamings),
39 checked source UNSAT subtree certificates and 53 unsuccessful
SAT residual trials were charged. Certified guarded learning
preserved every source model. UP-DPLL nodes fell from 107 to 52,
but selected charged logical events \emph{rose} from 4,466 to 15,793.
Input scans, source clause/proof checks, failed trials, guard writes
and final DPLL were counted; native FLMV machine operations and
simultaneous peak writable bits were \emph{not} measured. Original
clause-indexed S/U targets, inversions, coherent all-zero/all-one,
complement and flip counts, and source-indexed compatibility matrices
remain adversarial controls.

\textbf{OPEN:} the uniform hereditary all-input ordinary SAT
$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ power-short physical-child compiler for arbitrary
correct deterministic $A$ of unknown finite exponents, together
with fully charged dynamic policy acquisition, both-label joins,
guest simulation, original-source access and same-machine
simultaneous actual-FLMV time/space contraction. The restricted
affine MCSP no-go remains restricted. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
is proved. Readback-verified public sources:

\paragraph{ARC-JUMP-RESET-BISIM256: indexed-jump invariant and native transport firewall (30 October, 16:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input-head subsystem, mathematical review pending.}
For an honestly bounded remaining total of $B$ ordinary head moves on a complete
ordinary input of length $m$, representing exact positions below $m+B$ and all
higher positions by FAR preserves all read observations \emph{only when each
indexed jump resets the quotient from any prior state}. This uses
$m+B+1$ logical states. A previous HEAD-HORIZON255 proof sentence claiming
\emph{no later} in-range read is possible after FAR was false: an indexed
jump to address zero can reenter immediately. Its actual algorithm already
resets on jumps, so the restricted theorem is repaired rather than retired.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT conditional on index-tape erasure retaining the tape-head
coordinate:} a sparse high-address guest can perform $q$ jumps using
$H+2q+O(1)$ operations while a naive full-address rescan pays
$\Omega(qH)$ inspections; this is a simulator-architecture counterexample,
\emph{not} a general SAT lower bound or established interpretation of every
FLMV machine variant. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 17,342 random head-instruction
equivalences, 560 original-clause SS/SU/US/UU checks, 2,754 higher-order
extreme comparisons, 112 coherent complement pairs and five source
resolution-cube checks; both SAT and UNSAT labels retained. Paid full-child
head wrapping increased selected work on all tested cases, and the constructed
child remains parent-sized. Predecessor evidence remains under its original
strict status. Standalone checked public report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-30_1600_LSPV-1_ARC_JUMP_RESET_BISIM256_PUBLIC.tex}.
The fully charged, hereditary, all-input ordinary SAT $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$
power-short child generator and simultaneous actual-FLMV exponent
contraction remain \OPEN. P versus NP remains OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-1.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted), independent review pending.}
Any child plus sidecar that permits complete reconstruction of the
$2^m$-member SAT or UNSAT source fibers in ARC209 must retain
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ bits.  This does not constrain lossy
answer-preserving compression.  Composing an all-input embedding of
size exponent $\alpha$ with a child reduction of exponent $\lambda$
gives a closed power shrink only when $\alpha\lambda<1$, up to
polylogarithms.

ARC210 independently confirms the static connected-interface bound and
constructs a connected strict exact-$(2,2)$ torus family with
$\Theta(\sqrt{N/\log N})$ syntactic frontier but an explicit
satisfying assignment.  Thus large static or moving syntactic width is
not a SAT runtime lower bound.  An exact projection to the empty
frontier has a one-bit extensional representation, while uniformly
constructing that bit for all SAT is equivalent to deciding SAT; summary
size and acquisition remain distinct.  Uniform arbitrary-SAT lossy
contraction remains \OPEN.  Reports:

and

\paragraph{ARC211 static-interface sharpness, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED (independently reviewed; marker repair pending).}
For every integer $r\ge2$, an explicit connected proper strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ source has $n=9r^2+3r$, a fixed syntactic modular
decomposition with $b=k=3r$, and exact minimum private clause-hitting
parameter $q=r$ in each module. Thus $b+q=4r=\Theta(\sqrt n)$;
the inherited static lower bound has the correct square-root
\emph{exponent}. Literal swapping preserves every signed occurrence,
and the period-three $110$ assignment satisfies every instance.
Consequently no larger static-coverage exponent follows from the
stated connectedness/degree hypotheses alone, and this easy
counterexample gives no decision-time lower bound.
The supplied archive's original $4680/7191/139$ audit counts
were reproduced byte-for-byte; the independent ARC211

\paragraph{ARC212: rank-value descent and semantic residuals (28 October, 21:00).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a conditional sufficient theorem:} a single
uniform answer-preserving one-child evolution with complete intermediate
encodings bounded by $N^a$, fully charged polynomial step time,
and a strictly decreasing nonnegative integer rank initially bounded
by $N^d$ in \emph{value} has at most $N^d$ steps and polynomial
total time. This allows temporary encoded-size growth but does not
construct the required universal SAT evolution. Merely bounding the
rank's binary bit length is \textbf{FALSIFIED} as a runtime shortcut
by an exponential countdown.

\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED (known OBDD phenomenon, explicit grid proof):}
for every variable order of the connected $L\times L$ monotone
2CNF grid $\bigwedge_{uv\in E}(x_u\lor x_v)$, a balanced prefix
has at least $2^{L/64}$ distinct satisfiable complete-residual
Boolean functions. Yet the all-one valuation solves every such SAT
instance. This is not the signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF source
class and gives no general SAT time or circuit lower bound.
Finite audit: $12954$ balanced-cut checks, $110$ sampled interface
checks, $12$ full extensional suffix controls, all passing.
All-input source-authenticated moving summaries, strict recursive
power shrink, ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.
