\paragraph{LSPV-2, EXACT22-IMPLICATION-SPLICE222 (29 October 2026, 11:12 UTC).}
\textbf{RESTRICTED PROVED/EXACT, independently rerun and refereed.}
For any connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF source $H$,
swapping one negative literal occurrence with the fixed 15-variable $G$ module
preserves satisfiability, connectedness, signed quotas, and clause width.
The exact $(x,p)$ projected relation is the triangular matrix
$\left(\begin{smallmatrix}H_0&Q_0\\0&H_1\end{smallmatrix}\right)$,
where $Q_0\Rightarrow H_0$; full equality transport is therefore false
for arbitrary sources. Splicing the explicit affine $R_r$ source yields
the nonaffine implication relation $\neg x\lor p$ for all odd $r\ge5$.
This compiler increases physical DIMACS length by $\Theta(\log N)$;
it does not make a power-short child or decide arbitrary SAT.
The fixed $G$ edge is nonbridging via $p-C_3-x_{11}-C_2$,
so connectivity remains valid even if the removed $H$ incidence edge is a bridge.
The original Connected Interface Audit and the prior equality/XOR controls reproduced;
the self-contained run and a second run produced identical JSON.
Repeating the same splice at its transported clause is exactly idempotent:
the endpoint relation remains
$\left(\begin{smallmatrix}H_0&Q_0\\0&H_1\end{smallmatrix}\right)$
at every depth because $Q_0\Rightarrow H_0$. Thus repetition does not
remove $Q_0$ or approach a physical contraction; each level adds a fresh
fixed module. This repeatability theorem is proved and independently checked
in LSPV-5 referee report197.
Source and proof: \path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_1112_LSPV-2_EXACT22_IMPLICATION_SPLICE222.tex}.
The current peer file lacks the required opening/final markers; an
owner-preserved framing repair is pending.
Full arbitrary-SAT source-port preservation, profile acquisition,
and recursively closed power contraction remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-2, TWO-SWAP-EXACT-PORT-TRANSPORT223 (29 October 2026, 12:12 UTC).}
A deterministic pointwise cofactor-preserving two-occurrence splice
has been constructed on \emph{every} connected proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
3CNF source $H(X)$ and selected source variable $x$.  A fixed
15-variable connected exact22 guard with its positive and negative designated
clauses exchanged in matching sign with one positive and one negative $x$ clause
produces a connected proper strict exact22 formula $\mathcal T(H,x,p)$ such that
\[
\exists Z\ \mathcal T(H,x,p)(X,p,Z)
\ \equiv\ H(X)\wedge(p\leftrightarrow x)
\]
for every complete assignment to the original source variables.
The fixed guard puncture table has supported signatures
$(p,A,B)=000,010,100,101$ with respective counts $85,153,85,119$; it rules out
both off-diagonal port pairs.  LSPV-5 referee198 replaces reliance on that
table alone by four source-indexed Resolution refutations of the forbidden
branches, with respectively $(39,23),(55,36),(48,31),(90,71)$
(total,derived) proof rows.  The proof generation and replay remain
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}, not kernel verification; the universal pointwise
source theorem is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT under these fixed certificates}.
All eight three-variable exact22
sources, the UNSAT W1, G and affine R5/R7 passed 48 independently checked
joint cofactors.  A wrong guard negative slot instead permits every port pair,
showing that signed quotas alone do not prove equality.
The physical output grows as $N'=N+\Theta(\log N)$ and is not a power-short
ordinary-SAT child; arbitrary ordinary-SAT regularization, general summary
acquisition, ARC-EDGE-90, and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.
Artifact: \path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_1212_LSPV-2_TWO_SWAP_EXACT_PORT_TRANSPORT223.tex}.
The current report has the opening role marker but lacks the canonical final
sentinel; owner-preserved framing repair is pending.


\paragraph{LSPV-2, CONNECTED-ALLPORT-COMPILER224 (29 October 2026, 13:12 UTC).}
The all-input proper-3CNF to strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ occurrence
normalizer is \emph{prior work} of LSPV-4 PC22\_ALLFIELD1, not a new
SAT reduction.  This Builder run adds a satisfiability-neutral
component hub for disconnected ordinary sources (including unit,
binary, empty, duplicate, and tautological clause controls), and
retains every original source variable as a physically named free
port via deterministic clone renaming.  The resulting \emph{connected}
proper strict signed exact22 formula $C(F)$ obeys the pointwise identity
\[
  \forall\xi\in\{0,1\}^n:\quad
  \exists Z\,C(F)(\xi,Z)\ \Longleftrightarrow\ F(\xi),
\]
without a SAT-solving call, for both SAT and UNSAT sources.
The known two-swap guard additionally transports exact equality to
a fresh physical port, at an increasing encoded-size cost.
For a normalizer intermediate with $M$ clauses and $g\le9M$
transparent occurrence pads, the output has exactly $9(M+7g)$
variables and $12(M+7g)$ clauses; total encoded output is polynomial
but not power-short.
A separate \textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED} physical floor applies to
\emph{every} strict exact22 output retaining $n$ distinct named free
ports in ASCII DIMACS:
\[
 N_{\rm out}\ge32\sum_{j=1}^{n}
  (1+\lfloor\log_{10}j\rfloor)=\Omega(n\log n)\ \text{bits}.
\]
Thus on canonical connected bounded-occurrence families with
$N_{\rm in}=\Theta(n\log n)$ such all-port-preserving output
\emph{cannot} satisfy $N_{\rm out}\le N_{\rm in}^\lambda$
for any fixed $\lambda<1$ on unbounded inputs. This is an architectural
\emph{output-size} obstruction, not a runtime lower bound:
a correct answer-only child is free to forget the original ports.
Exhaustive $Q$ and guard fixtures, 10 hostile ordinary-source controls,
10 independent MILP cofactor checks, and a byte-identical independent
rerun of the 4,680/7,191/139 Connected Interface Audit are
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} evidence. Source and full physical/machine ledgers:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_1312_LSPV-2_CONNECTED_ALLPORT_COMPILER224.tex}.
Independent referee and kernel verification are pending; uniform
charged semantic forgetting, one-child power contraction,
ARC-EDGE-90 and both sides of $P$ versus $NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{PARITY-PORT-FORGETTING225: auxiliary-free projection versus forgetful collapse (29 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED}, independent referee pending.
For each $b\ge2$ and $t\in\{0,1\}$ an explicit connected proper
$4b$-clause ordinary 3CNF with $2b+1$ variables has exact external
relation $\bigoplus_{j=1}^b x_j=t$. Every auxiliary-free CNF for this
relation requires $2^{b-1}$ distinct width-$b$ clauses, whereas the
Tseitin extension-variable 3CNF is linear. Existentially forgetting
\emph{any one} free parity input makes the projected relation TRUE;
conjoining opposite parity targets on shared inputs remains UNSAT
under any such forgetting. Thus local variable count, exact projection
size, and physical re-entry size are distinct resources. Applying the
previously established connected all-port exact22 compiler preserves
the pointwise relation but \emph{expands} measured source bit lengths:
for $b=3,4,6$, $(1016,56176)$, $(1320,62128)$,
$(2128,74032)$ (ordinary parent, strict22 all-port child).
The named-port lower bound strengthens quantitatively: retaining $r$
distinct strict22 physical ports costs $\Omega(r\log r)$ ASCII bits,
so with $N=\Theta(b\log b)$, power contraction requires
$r=O(b^\lambda/(\log b)^{1-\lambda})$ \emph{when physical port retention
is required}; answer-only descendants need not retain those ports.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 16 source controls, 3064 boundary assignments,
248 complete hidden-variable enumerations, 13824 forget-pair checks,
and an independent byte-identical 4680/7191/139 connected-interface
archive audit; raw SHA256 \path{20646d178a5cedca3ba67f312e73a2d14f3fe9c44379c2be0910af355973159c}.
No all-input polynomial summary acquisition, ordinary-SAT power-short
recursive child, ARC-EDGE-90 or $P$ versus $NP$ resolution follows.
Report and reproducer:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_LSPV-2_PARITY_PORT_FORGETTING225.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_LSPV-2_PARITY_PORT_FORGETTING225_repro.py}.

\paragraph{LSPV-2, ONE-SHOT-PARITY-PADDING226 (29 October 2026; restricted theorem, independent referee pending).}
The fully explicit connected proper ordinary-3CNF parity-padding family
$P(H)$ takes any arbitrary connected proper 3SAT core $H$ with $m$
clauses and attaches $\max(2,m^2)$ fresh XOR inputs to one original
variable, encoded by four ternary clauses per gate. For every assignment
to the original core variables, forgetting one private parity input
and all internal chain wires projects $P(H)$ exactly to $H$. The
canonical one-child extractor validates every source clause and is
polynomial-time, answer-oblivious and correct on both SAT and UNSAT.
The promised padded family is NP-complete yet its child has physical
bit length at most $N^{3/4}$ for $m\ge4096$. Crucially the child $H$
need not be in the padded family; canonical re-padding gives the
two-cycle $P(H)\to H\to P(H)$, hence no decreasing descent rank.
This is an elementary padding/closure counterexample, not a new
general SAT algorithm or an all-algorithm lower bound. The independent
finite audit verified 14 growing SAT/UNSAT controls, 592 small
pointwise projections and five malformed inputs; the original
Connected Interface Audit matched its archived JSON byte for byte.
The complete local LaTeX/code/raw/PDF/ZIP are available as conversation
artifacts; cross-session Library upload failed, so full-file GitHub
publication remains pending. The verified GitHub handoff is
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_LSPV-2_ONE_SHOT_PARITY_PADDING226_PUBLICATION_PENDING.md}.
Recursively closed arbitrary-SAT power contraction and P versus NP
remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-2, TWO-UNIVERSE-CROSS-SPINE227 (29 October 2026; conditional synthesis and computational controls).}
The two conditional universes were audited against both the
constructive single-child and the FLMV/Williams separation spines.
\textbf{PREVIOUSLY PROVED}: RACE77 shows that a correct total
deterministic SAT decider with a uniform polynomial runtime bound on
\emph{either} the SAT side or the UNSAT side alone gives $P=NP$ by
opposite-answer timeout. ARC207 establishes that an arbitrary-input,
uniform, recursively closed, physically $N^\lambda$-short
($\lambda<1$), answer-preserving one-child algorithm with polynomial
full cost is equivalent to $P=NP$. The converse uses a presumed
polynomial SAT decider and is not an unconditional construction.
ONE-SHOT-PARITY-PADDING226 remains an NP-complete non-closed
counterexample compatible with both hypothetical worlds.

\textbf{CONDITIONAL numerical tests}: a materialized source-to-exact22
entry exponent $\alpha$ followed by a child exponent $\lambda$ yields
a strict power-size upper contraction only if $\alpha\lambda<1$,
assuming polylogarithmic overhead. If a virtual child has length
$N^\alpha$, construction cost $N^c$, child-solver time $M^d$, and
per-access cost $N^u$, the naive fully paid time upper exponent is
$\max(c,\alpha d+u)$, with the native and simultaneous peak-workspace
bound still to be proved. Williams excludes a correct random-access
SAT algorithm with $N^{o(1)}$ workspace and time exponent below
$2\cos(\pi/7)\approx1.80194$; an assumed $P=NP$ decider has an
\emph{unknown} polynomial exponent and need not inhabit this forbidden
region. The compiler224 all-port $\Omega(n\log n)$ physical floor
applies in both universes, whereas answer-preserving semantic forgetting
may evade it. Hirahara's unconditional P-immunity concerns
superpolynomial-time-bounded random strings; $P=NP$ does not put that
predicate in coNP via a polynomial simulation, and the polynomial
defining-time case has an unbounded search-exponent gap.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 69,904 exact projection assignments,
10,912 toy-parity clocking outcomes (not SAT), 20 composition
parameters, 12 port-profile sizes, five hypothetical resource
ledgers and rank/value controls. All passed, raw SHA256
\path{a27776130e4eb9366f28120774bddb0624355ceda3051997549eff6cc52e3e9f}.
The complete source and PDF/ZIP are in the Library; GitHub
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-29_0755_LSPV-2_TWO_UNIVERSE_CROSS_SPINE227_SNAPSHOT.md}
and the
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-29_0755_TWO_UNIVERSE_CROSS_SPINE227_LSPV1_LSPV4_LSPV5_HANDOFF.md}
were verified by fresh readback. Full GitHub TeX/code/raw upload remains
\textbf{PENDING}. No new priority is claimed for RACE77 or ARC207.
Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-2, STREAMING-FRONTIER-REFEREE228 (29 October 2026, 08:12 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (RESTRICTED; independent source referee):}
A canonical connected proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ cyclic-scope
3CNF family, with arbitrary balanced signs, admits one uniform deterministic
source-authenticated solver on a model with one sequential read-only input
tape and one writable work tape. Its four-live-name variable-order
frontier has at most 16 states and 32 transition candidates per variable.
A fully charged input reader validates every clause and all $2P/2N$
occurrence quotas by source rescanning. For $n$ variables and physical
bit length $N=\Theta(n\log(n+2))$, native bounds are
$T_2=O(nN\operatorname{polylog}N)$,
$S_2=O(\log N)$, input-head moves $O(nN)$ and
$O(n)$ full sweeps; no free occurrence index or source table is used.
A conservative one-tape simulation with its initial source counted gives
$T_1=O(nN^2\operatorname{polylog}N)$,
$P_1=O(N)$; indexed random-access lower bounds are not transferred
from the sequential model. A fixed YES/NO instance gives a one-child
answer-only \emph{restricted} shrinker, but the canonical class is easy
and no all-SAT closure follows.

\textbf{Previously PROVED/RESTRICTED; independently rechecked:}
under the exact static connected-interface syntax, $k\le4b$,
$n\le3b+12bq$, $b+q\ge\sqrt{n/3}-1/4$ for $b>0$,
and $q\ge n/3$ when $b=0$. The obstruction does not bind moving
or semantic interfaces, nor any general SAT algorithm.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} unsigned scopes and balanced signed
occurrence counts alone determine SAT. The connected 15-variable
exact22 $W_1$ and the same-scope two-sign-swap $W_1'$ have opposite
SAT/UNSAT labels, as independently checked on all $2^{15}$ assignments.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} original Library Connected Interface Audit
replayed byte-for-byte, including 4,680 boundary sets, 7,191
local-discovery checks and 139 brute-force controls; 65 authenticated
cyclic signed-source comparisons, three malformed-source controls,
and additional $6^3$ and $6^6$ balanced-sign exhaustive cases,
all passed. The Python input-head instrument does not measure native
TM time or writable-space usage; the latter is justified by the
separate algorithm construction. The source-inspected CaDiCaL,
CUDD, d4, and OpenAI Boolean-oracle unitary synthesis implementations
supply architectural comparisons only: none gives uniform polynomial
classical discovery of all SAT summaries or oracle contents.
\textbf{OPEN:} uniform all-SAT moving-interface discovery,
repeated physically power-short ordinary-SAT children,
same-native-machine ARC/FLMV edge, $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\paragraph{LSPV-2, AFFINE-LIFT-BVE229 (29 October 2026, 09:12 PDT; restricted local non-shrink).}
\textbf{PROVED/RESTRICTED.} An unbounded connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF family comes from cyclic connected incidence lifts of the nine-point, 12-line affine plane over $\mathbb F_3$, with $n=9r$, $m=12r$ and pairwise linear clauses. Every source pivot has two positive and two negative clauses with eight distinct other names. Context-independent Davis--Putnam elimination produces exactly four non-tautological width-four resolvents. Independent exact width-four-to-3CNF Tseitin conversion produces $n'=n+3$ live names and $m'=m+4$ clauses. For the specified four-word encoding $N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$ the child has $N'-N\ge16\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil>0$ bits. The numerical ratio $m'/n'=4/3$ survives, but eight source neighbors have degree five and the fresh auxiliaries have degree two, so the strict exact22 class is \emph{not} closed. The \emph{isolated} projected eight-input relation has four essential width-four prime implicates and no auxiliary-free exact 3CNF, but a seven-gate shared circuit; no whole-formula or arbitrary-algorithm lower bound follows. Eliminating a newly introduced split auxiliary and re-entering ternary CNF reproduces the same two-clause gadget, a zero-progress recursive cycle. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 459 direct first pivots, 1,584 sign-randomized pivots, 1,536 exhaustive eight-input projection evaluations and 108 second-step controls pass. The original Connected Interface Audit was independently replayed byte-identically. \textbf{OPEN:} globally shared paid projection with repeated ordinary-SAT reentry, all-input recursive contraction, universal same-machine lower bound, $P=NP$, and $P\ne NP$. In either complexity universe these restricted local observations remain valid. Standalone source and verified executable and raw JSON:

The separately declared information-value interpreter records certified

These controls do not provide an exact22 compiler or imply a

\paragraph{LSPV-2, NONLINEAR-ONE-PROBE236 (29 October 2026, 18:38 PDT; restricted theorem; independent referee pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, RESTRICTED.}
For the two-stage canonical unary source $A_a(X)=\bigwedge_i(x_i=a_i)$
and subsequent arbitrary parity continuation $B_{w,t}(X)=[w\cdot X=t]$,
suppose the source remains indexed read-only and the preprocessor stores
at most $L$ source-dependent bits before $w$ is known. Even permitting an
\emph{arbitrary deterministic nonlinear}, variable-length summary and
adaptive nonlinear continuation, exact response to every $(a,w)$ with
\emph{at most one original-source bit probe} requires and permits exactly
\[
 L_1(b)=b-\lfloor\log_2(b+1)\rfloor.
\]
The lower bound follows because each summary fiber has affine-span dimension
at most $\lfloor\log_2(b+1)\rfloor$: all restricted parity characters,
modulo complementation, must be one of at most $b+1$ constant/coordinate
character classes. A uniform Hamming-style encoder attains equality in
$O(b\log b)$ Boolean work beyond canonical parsing, with retained source
and separately charged source probes. This extends HAMMING235's
\emph{linear-summary} lower bound to arbitrary nonlinear preprocessing;
no novelty beyond the declared restricted data-structure model is asserted.
For $b=7$, a four-bit summary achieves universal one-probe parity queries,
whereas literal storage of four source bits needs three probes on a worst
query. Source-free all-query reuse still needs $b$ bits.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 349,524 complete source/query comparisons for
$b\le9$, 65,792 exhaustive one-bit nonlinear-summary tests at $b=3,4$,
10,920 independently enumerated two-stage SAT semantic controls at $b\le6$,
and separate two-answer 2SAT/XOR/mixed/connected strict22 checks.
The contextual $(I)\mid_v$ interpreter charges source-row authentication,

\]
Each summary fiber supports all restricted Fourier characters, which
span the full space of real-valued functions on that fiber; each depth-$Q$
bit decision tree is a real polynomial of degree at most $Q$, giving
the stated dimension upper bound.  On the infinite, \emph{realized}
family $b=2(2^m-1)$, $m\ge1$, this bound is tight at $Q=2$:
\[
L_2(b)=b-2m.
\]
A uniform two-group XOR summary stores $b-2m$ physical bits and answers
any subsequent parity mask using at most one original-source bit in each
group. The exact first three fixed-length storage/probe Pareto points
are $(Q,L)=(0,b),(1,b-m),(2,b-2m)$, where the $Q=1$ point invokes the
already established NONLINEAR-ONE-PROBE236 theorem. For $b=14$, these
realizable points are $(0,14),(1,11),(2,8)$.

\textbf{MODEL FIREWALL:}
This is an \emph{indexed bit-RAM, reusable future-query} lower bound,
not a generic SAT decision-time bound. Preprocessing construction,
mask reading, cached synopsis, algebraic source reads and all address
arithmetic are charged; the source formula remains physically retained.
The construction has $P=O(N+bm)$ and per-query
$T=O(N+bm)$ for canonical $N=\Theta(b\log(b+1))$, stored bit length
$L=b-2m$, and $O(L+m+\log N)$ peak writable bits, separate from
immutable source $N$. Two random-access source probes do not transfer
to two sequential-tape reads. Arbitrary non-prefix variable-length
summaries are \emph{not} claimed optimal at $Q=2$; only the weaker
$(2^{L+1}-1)\sum_{j\le Q}\binom bj\ge2^b$ follows directly.
A joint SAT solver receiving source and future query together may
evaluate parity by a polynomial scan, without a reusable summary.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
The reproducible interpreter passed 4,112 exhaustive $(a,w)$ tests for
$b=2,6$, 70,216 larger tested pairs including all masks on four
$b=14$ source controls, 2,728 independent finite SAT-context checks,
2SAT/XOR/mixed and connected strict22 SAT/UNSAT tests, and a charged
contextual $(I)\mid_v$ XOR-share synergy and negative-realized-entropy
control. Python time/peak memory are same-execution observations,
not native machine resource measurements.
\textbf{OPEN:} non-prefix variable-length two-probe optimum; extension
to authenticated connected strict22 future interfaces; native time-space
frontiers for every correct SAT solver; $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\paragraph{LSPV-2, VARIABLE-LENGTH-SATURATION238 (29 October 2026, 20:17 PDT; exact restricted non-prefix saturation).}
\PROVED\ \RESTRICTED.  In a deterministic two-stage indexed bit-probe model,
preprocessing of a six-bit source (before a future arbitrary parity mask is known)
may produce an arbitrary nonlinear, non-prefix binary summary of maximum length $L$,
while continuation may adaptively probe at most two retained source-data bits.
The exact optimum is $L=2$.  The preceding degree-volume fiber ceiling is
$V(6,2)=22$; saturation forces orthogonality of the quadratic Walsh-evaluation
matrix, hence an orthogonal array of strength four, impossible since
$16\nmid22$.  Consequently every summary fiber has at most $21$ sources,
and three labels of length at most one cover at most $63<64$ source words.
Two cached triple parities with one raw bit probe per triple attain $L=2$
uniformly.  More generally, with
$V(b,Q)=\sum_{j=0}^{Q}\binom bj$, if
$2^{\min(2Q,b)}\nmid V(b,Q)$, a reusable summary fiber has size at most
$V(b,Q)-1$, improving the arbitrary-nonlinear degree-volume count.
\textbf{OPEN:} arbitrary-SAT/connected strict22 transport and all-algorithm
time--space implications; no generic SAT lower bound or $P$ versus $NP$ result
follows.  Complete standalone proof, executable, raw data, and independent-role
handoff:

\paragraph{LSPV-2, MERGE-FILTER239 (29 October 2026, 21:16 PDT; clause-equity and merge-only UP audit).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, RESTRICTED; independently refereed status pending.}
For each integer $k\ge2$, the connected proper width-3 source
$F_k=\bigwedge_{r=1}^k[(x_r\lor a\lor b)\land(\neg x_r\lor a\lor b)]$
has every exact source-clause equity $Q_j=0$, but $k$ opposite-occurrence
resolvents all equal $(a\lor b)$. Their naive additive forbidden-cube
weight is $k/4$, while their exact union density is $1/4$.
This disproves the proposed transfer from raw pair weight to
independent forbidden assignments or clause criticality;
the source is \emph{not} signed strict exact-$(2,2)$.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (Babka--Balyo--\v{C}epek et al.,
Artificial Intelligence 203 (2013), Lemma 3.3):}
one-step non-merge resolvents are non-empowering; the independently
reproved saturation argument shows that deleting them while retaining
both parent clauses preserves full unit-propagation closure at every prefix.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 39 source-verified finite controls, including
five connected strict exact22 SAT/UNSAT sources and 160{,}566 checks.
Six policy executions gave full-resolvent versus merge-only total
DFS node counts $181=181$ and selected primitive-work counts
$46{,}319$ versus $36{,}754$; all times, failed probes,
source reads, and same-run Python memory peaks are recorded, but
are not native Turing-machine resource lower bounds.
\textbf{OPEN:} an authenticated unbounded connected strict22 analogue,
a universal SAT resource bound, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\paragraph{LSPV-2, EXTREME-CYCLE-UP240 (29 October 2026, 22:12 PDT; restricted, independent referee pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, RESTRICTED}: The source-clause signed-occurrence multigraph
has a globally coherent assignment making every original clause all-literals-TRUE or
all-literals-FALSE precisely when every signed cycle has even XOR parity.
An odd cycle forces a middle literal-count state in at least one clause,
\emph{not} source UNSAT. For proper width-$r$ balanced degree-$d$ CNF
with $m=dn/r$, any partial assignment making
$k>(dn/2-m)/(r-1)$ original clauses fully TRUE is refuted by saturated
ordinary unit propagation. In signed exact-$(2,2)$-3CNF this threshold is
$k>m/4=n/3$; every SAT model has $N_3\le m/4$.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: this count provides no new pruning after saturated UP.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: on authenticated 15-variable connected strict22 W1 UNSAT
the source-parent bounded-merge DPLL search visited 65 nodes versus 151 baseline
(and charged 13848 versus 15158 literal reads), while strict22 S SAT had the
same 12 nodes and increased reads (1631 versus 694). These are 13 finite
source-controlled examples, not an asymptotic algorithm theorem. Full source,
occurrence-indexed local S/U and inversions, pair matrices, global endpoint/flip
trails, original-clause resolution provenance and finite controls are in
the paired reproducibility archive. GitHub audit reports:

\paragraph{LSPV-2, THIRD-ORDER-CLUSTER245 (30 October 2026; restricted).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted), independent mathematical review pending.}
For proper source 3CNF with at most constant $d$ clause occurrences
per original variable, third-order original-clause finite differences,
singleton-signature buckets, exactly-one-interaction-edge corrections,
and explicit $O(n\Delta^2)$ connected three-variable clusters compile
the \emph{exact} four-class histogram of all $\binom n3$ coherent flips
in $O(n+m)$ indexed-word-RAM work and words, with $\Delta\le2d$.
The source remains clause indexed, with local $S_j/U_j$ and inverted
extrema, full complement comparison, proof-bearing non-tautological

can be recovered and verified in the same fixed-degree bound.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 15 three-CNF controls plus two separately
tagged 2SAT controls passed independent checks; same-input histogram
versus enumeration times crossed between $n=12$ and $n=24$ on

\paragraph{LSPV-2, CONNECTED-LOG-FLIPS247 (30 October 2026; restricted).}
\PROVED\ (restricted, independent mathematical referee pending): For every coherent assignment of proper original width-three CNF with maximum source variable occurrence degree $d$ and every radius $0\le r\le n$, the \emph{complete aggregated} four-category histogram of all size-$r$ source-variable flips can be constructed in $f(r,d)(n+m)$ indexed-word-RAM arithmetic operations and working words. Define $Z_F(\mathbf u,\mathbf t)=\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\mathbf u_S\mathbf t^{\mathbf N(a\oplus S)-\mathbf N(a)}$. The ordinary formal logarithm has no monomial with disconnected support in the original variable co-occurrence graph; enumeration of connected supports and truncated formal exponentiation yields the exact histogram. A source-checked satisfying witness at radius $r$, when it exists, can be extracted with at most $n+1$ further conditioned compilations, charging each failed feasibility test. For fixed $r,d$ the histogram compiler is linear in source size; dependence $f(r,d)$ may be superpolynomial when $r$ grows with $n$. This is \emph{not} an all-input SAT decision theorem.
\COMPUTATIONAL\ independent tests: 12 named original-source controls, 219 signed clause-indexed ternary clauses, 2,839 pairwise $SS/SU/US/UU$ matrices checked against 149,320 coherent assignments, and 11,684 exhaustive radius-$r$ masks. The strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ W1 UNSAT instance has an independently replayed 76-leaf source-UP-DPLL refutation and all 32,768 assignments independently falsify the source. On W1, baseline 151 DPLL states and 0.026 seconds versus endpoint/cluster/fallback 151 states and 0.842 seconds; on $H_1$ SAT, baseline 7 DPLL states and 0.0006 seconds versus compiled witness without DPLL and 0.333 seconds. Thus a smaller search tree can cost more total time. Independent local $S/U$ and global coherent endpoint/flip analyses are distinct; the $O(m^2)$ pair matrix is not free solver preprocessing. Related bounded-degree coefficient algorithms are already known from Patel--Regts, and signed conflict matrices from Kullmann. Sources:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-30_0316_LSPV-2_CONNECTED_LOG_FLIPS247.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-30_0316_LSPV-2_CONNECTED_LOG_FLIPS247.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-30_0316_LSPV-2_CONNECTED_LOG_FLIPS247_independent_check.py}.


\paragraph{LSPV-2 DERIVATIVE-WITNESS248 (30 October 2026, 04:12 PDT; exact bounded-radius constructive theorem, independent referee pending).}
\PROVED\ \RESTRICTED: For a proper original 3-CNF of maximum variable occurrence $d$, the weighted radius-$r$ satisfying-flip polynomial
$P(\boldsymbol w)=\sum_{|S|=r,\ F(a\oplus S)=1}\prod_{i\in S}w_i$
admits an explicit source-indexed arithmetic circuit of $C=f(r,d)(n+m)$ gates using the verified connected-cluster expansion. For each already chosen flip set $T$,
$\partial_TP(\boldsymbol1)$ counts the still-viable satisfying radius-$r$ flips containing $T$, and
$\sum_{i\notin T}\partial_i\partial_TP(\boldsymbol1)=(r-|T|)\partial_TP(\boldsymbol1)$.
A forward nilpotent extension and reverse automatic differentiation therefore select a complete verified flip witness in at most $r$ passes, with $O(r3^r C+n+m)$ arithmetic operations and $O(2^r C+n+m)$ words, instead of the previous at-most-$n$ complete conditioned recompilations. The bound is linear in the source size for fixed $r,d$ only; all bit costs must be charged when $r$ grows, and general SAT remains open.

\COMPUTATIONAL: A separate source-indexed experiment first recorded every original clause's all-literals-true/false targets and inversions, $SS/SU/US/UU$ pairs, coherent global endpoints and multivariable flips, source-parent resolvents and excluded cubes. Fifteen adversarial SAT/UNSAT controls passed independent replay: 603 original signed occurrences, 1,522 clause-pair matrices, 69,928 pair-assignment checks, 1,805 radius masks, and 176,408 coherent source assignments. A strict exact-$(2+,2-)$ SAT source with both endpoints failing had 11 radius-2 satisfying flips; reverse differentiation required 2 gradient passes versus 14 separate conditioned evaluations. \FALSIFIED: fewer conditional recompilations do \emph{not} imply faster complete SAT decisions: the measured hybrid was slower and used more peak memory than UP--DPLL on this SAT source and the W1 strict22 UNSAT control, where fallback still visited 151 DPLL nodes. No equity scoring or universally applicable SAT lower bound is asserted. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-2: BALANCED-TWO-COORD249 (2026-10-30 04:13 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted, independent referee pending.}
For any proper original ternary CNF with equal positive and negative occurrence
counts for each source variable, every coherent complete assignment satisfies
\[
N_1=\frac m2-2N_0+N_3,\qquad N_2=\frac m2+N_0-2N_3.
\]
Consequently all exact four-category changes under original-variable flip sets
are determined by the two coordinates $(\Delta N_0,\Delta N_3)$; a weighted
radius-$r$ Laurent encoding can replace the four formal variables with
$U=z_0z_2/z_1^2$ and $V=z_1z_3/z_2^2$.  The joint-label
box has at most $(2dr+1)^2$ potential indices when variables have degree at most
$d$; this is a representation theorem and \emph{not} a SAT time lower bound.
The two-coordinate linear rank is attained already by original single-variable
flips in the 15-variable W1 connected strict22 UNSAT source.  The prior LSPV-1
SAT-slice count identities and LSPV-2 connected-support compilation remain
valid and distinct.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} An independent source-only checker passed eight
SAT/UNSAT/2SAT/Horn/XOR/dense controls: 275 signed occurrences,
785 source pair matrices, 34,928 coherent pair assignments,
47,272 forbidden-cube extensions, 3,366 exact radius masks and
152 verified UNSAT proof leaves.  On balanced controls the sparse
two-coordinate and four-coordinate compilers executed the \emph{same}
number of nonzero term-pair multiplications: reducing coordinate labels
does not automatically reduce algebraic work.  W1 UP--DPLL stayed at
151 states with the two-coordinate compiler added, while end-to-end
runtime rose from 0.0173 to 0.0420 seconds on the tested Python
machine.  One-coordinate $N_0$ compilation can count SAT targets but
cannot reconstruct all four clause-status categories.
Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ is established.

\paragraph{LSPV-2 HAMMING-HUNTER251 (2026-10-30 06:12 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM TRANSFER / PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent referee pending.}
For original proper 3CNF source clauses, local all-literals-FALSE targets
$U_j$ specify coherent falsifying event cubes, with opposite-sign clashes
forbidding pair intersections. Restricting assignments to one Hamming
layer $\Omega_r(a)$, exact source-local event counts are
$|E_j|=\binom{n-3}{r-t_j(a)}$ and, when two $U$ targets agree on all shared
source variables, $|E_j\cap E_k|=\binom{n-u}{r-h}$, where $u$ is the
union-support size and $h$ is the required flips relative to $a$.
Hunter's known spanning-tree union bound yields the \emph{SAT-only}
certificate
\[
H_T=\sum_j |E_j|-\sum_{\{j,k\}\in T}|E_j\cap E_k|
<\binom nr.
\]
A uniform deterministic conditional-expectation construction fixes
original-variable flip bits, charging both child tests, and verifies
the reconstructed witness in polynomial bit complexity.
A connected, distinct-clause strict exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with
$n=9,m=12$ has both coherent endpoints false with
$(N_0,N_1,N_2,N_3)=(3,3,3,3)$; at $a=0^9,r=3$ the uncorrected
count $126\ge84$ is inconclusive, whereas maximum tree overlap $43$
gives $H_T=83<84$ and a verified witness mask $265$.
The independent exhaustive oracle found $10$ witnesses at radius $3$
and $61$ in total, but is not part of the algorithm.
Source-indexed local $S/U$ and inversions, all four clause-pair
extreme modes, signed conflict reasons, complement/flip trails,
source-parent resolvent forbidden cubes, W1 strict22 UNSAT, XOR,
Horn and 2SAT controls passed independent replay.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: baseline UP--DPLL on the nine-variable source
used 5 nodes and 398 selected events, while Hunter used zero fallback
DPLL nodes but 3658 selected events and greater runtime.
Failure to certify is \emph{UNRESOLVED}, never UNSAT, and neither
$P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ is proved.

\paragraph{LSPV-2, UNIVERSAL-RESIDUAL-POLICY1 (30 October 2026, 10:09 America/Los\_Angeles).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted syntactic raw-child count; independent review pending).}
For a proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ width-three source with $n$
variables, fixing one variable by \emph{raw} literal restriction deletes
exactly two satisfied original clauses and two additional falsified pivot
literals, leaving exactly $m-2$ clauses, $m-4$ original ternary clauses,
two binary clauses, and $4n-8$ of its $4n$ original literal occurrences.
An explicit source-ID encoding has $N'/N\to1$, so this \emph{uncompressed
one-pivot cofactor emitter} does not realize a fixed ARC $N^\lambda$
child bound for any $\lambda<1$ on unbounded connected strict22 inputs.
A connected $n=3k$ strict22 SAT family for every $k\ge2$ explicitly
realizes the ratio; the historical 15-variable W1 strict22 UNSAT source
independently exhibits $52/60$ retained literals. This is \emph{not} a
hardness or arbitrary-compression theorem.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} An exact uniform source-preserving adaptive Python
policy (UP, certified 2SAT/Horn/dual-Horn, complete-parity GF$(2)$,
verified low-width DP, searched size-one backdoor, branch/memo fallback)
matched both SAT and UNSAT on 29 original controls plus 360
independently brute-verified random formulas in each policy mode.
Across the 29 inputs, search nodes fell from 232 to 96 but
\emph{instrumented selected work} rose from 33,271 to 132,221.
On W1, 67 to 25 nodes nevertheless cost 11,932 to 44,573 events,
with simultaneous per-execution Python peak 53,018 versus 134,088 bytes.
These are not native FLMV bit operations or machine-matched time-space
bounds. The raw cofactor emitter fails the required ordinary child
size, and the arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ hereditary ARC successor and
all-input indexed-bit accessor remain \textbf{OPEN}; both $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.
Verified standalone proof, compact reproducer, summary, and handoff:

\paragraph{LSPV-2, COFACTOR-VOLUME-ARC252 (30 October 2026, 11:20 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted elementary counting, independent review pending).}
For a set of $b$ pinned original variables and original source clause
$C_j$ meeting the pins in $t_j$ variables, the total number of surviving
raw source-clause copies over all $2^b$ coherent cofactors is exactly
$\sum_j2^{b-t_j}$; the summed surviving literal count is
$\sum_j(|C_j|-t_j)2^{b-t_j}$, including empty residuals as conflicts.
These unsigned support statistics admit a source-indexed single-pass
symbolic compiler, avoiding the explicit $2^b$ child expansion.
For signed strict exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF, every raw $b$-pin residual
retains at least $m-4b$ untouched original ternary clauses;
on explicit $N=\Theta(n\log n)$ source encoding, a physical
$O(N^\lambda)$ raw child with fixed $\lambda<1$ requires
$b\ge n/3-o(n)$. This is a limitation only of raw restriction,
not a general SAT or arbitrary-$A$ lower bound.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} unsigned aggregate cofactor volume cannot
decide SAT: matched $n=15,m=20$ strict22 W1 UNSAT (zero models) and
S15 SAT (861 models) have identical ordered unsigned original clause
supports, hence the same volume for \emph{every} pin set $B$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} nine same-source SAT/UNSAT, renamed,
Horn, XOR, wide-easy and cyclic strict22 controls passed an independent
checker (340 signed occurrence targets, 918 original indexed pair modes,
135184 complement checks, 135252 complete truth assignments, 41 materialized

\paragraph{LSPV-2, POLICY-ENTRY-GATE253 (30 October 2026, 12:15 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted fixed-candidate theorem; independent review pending).}
For a specified set $B$ of original variables and each indexed clause
$C_j$, write $D_j=C_j\cap\mathrm{Lit}(B)$ and
$R_j=C_j\setminus D_j$. The source-preserving entry formula for
Horn is $\bigwedge_{j:\#\mathrm{pos}(R_j)>1}D_j$; dual-Horn uses
$\#\mathrm{neg}(R_j)>1$ and Krom uses $|R_j|>2$.
A coherent assignment $\beta$ to $B$ enters that class if and only if
it satisfies the associated entry formula. Such membership does not
decide SAT of the parent. At source width four Horn/dual-Horn entry is
2SAT, and at source width five Krom entry is 2SAT; one higher width
suffices to encode arbitrary proper 3SAT in this fixed-$B$ discovery
problem, even when every parent input is trivially SAT. This is a
policy-selection limitation only, not universal SAT hardness.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 67 SAT/UNSAT source controls (61/6),
37071 independent cofactor/class checks, 8 source-indexed implication

portfolio reduced ordinary DPLL nodes $311\to137$ but increased

same-machine resource bounds. The fully charged hereditary

\paragraph{LSPV-2, TYPED-TRANSACTION260 (30 October 2026, 18:15 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted logical theorem, independent mathematical review pending.}
For same-source variables $X$ and any auxiliary CNF $E(X,Y)$, define
$P_E(a)\equiv\exists y\,E(a,y)$.  Exact one-sided source-model
coverage $\forall a\,[F(a)\Rightarrow P_E(a)]$ is equivalent to
the soundness of \emph{every} source-only UNSAT cube export
$\neg\mathrm{SAT}(E|_\rho)\Rightarrow\neg\mathrm{SAT}(F|_\rho)$,
and equivalently of each source-only consequence
$E|_\rho\models D\Rightarrow F\models B_\rho\lor D$.
This does \emph{not} require two-sided pointwise equivalence for
the narrower operation of exporting learned UNSAT source clauses.
For a source-clause-subset policy $E\subseteq F$, COVER is syntactically
certified; an underapproximating strengthening's UNSAT may not
be exported.  Regardless of policy type, a full SAT witness must be
checked on every original clause.  Only source-proved resolvents
or guarded consequences can survive a failed policy switch.
These are established overapproximation principles and refine the
previous TRANSACTIONAL259 interface, not novel SAT hardness.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Independent checking passed 53 original SAT/UNSAT
sources (48/5), including W1 strict22 UNSAT and same-support S15 SAT,
68,216 complete source assignment checks, 136,432 one-sided semantic
checks, 8,488 indexed original-clause four-mode S/U checks,
1,392 source signed-occurrence checks, 63 checked resolution steps
and 33,480 guarded-entailment tests.  Source flip/complement controls
retained.  Relative to standard DPLL (1,236 nodes, 16,110 clause tests),
the typed controller used 1,452 nodes and 20,731 clause tests, with
40 failed policies, 77 recognition attempts and 93 switches.
Python source read and proof costs were additionally charged;
these are not native FLMV bounds.  \textbf{OPEN:} all-input
physical power-short ordinary SAT children, full selector/source/tape
and simultaneous peak-space accounting, and hereditary
arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contraction.  $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-2 PARITY-INDEXED-ACCESSOR268: a verified restricted child emitter and exact auxiliary-free parity guard cost (31 October 2026, 02:12 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM / PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent mathematical review pending).}
For source-authenticated original proper-3CNF, four-clause XOR chains with pairwise disjoint private variables and common boundary $B$, duplicate parity conditions may be deleted \emph{only} after complete indexed clause authentication. Keeping both contrary terminal pins yields a correctly UNSAT child. For every coherent boundary assignment $\beta$ the resulting ordinary-CNF child $G$ obeys
\[
\exists P\,F(\beta,P)\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad\exists Z\,G(\beta,Z).
\]
Every child SAT witness lifts to the signed original clauses; the same equivalence preserves source UNSAT with a typed parity/source truth-table certificate, not automatically a pure source-resolution proof. The constructed canonical DIMACS child has a checked exact symbol accessor using an explicit decimal-digit prefix formula, binary search over XOR gates and at most two chain descriptors, with $O(\log N)$ descriptor bits and charged $\mathrm{polylog}(N)$ local address arithmetic. This is \emph{not} the missing general CONSTRUCTION-STRENGTHEN17 strict22 bit accessor.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (source representation-specific).}
An auxiliary-free CNF equivalent to even parity on $b$ boundary variables needs at least $2^{b-1}$ full-width clauses: a non-tautological entailed clause cannot omit any variable, and each full-width clause excludes exactly one odd assignment. Polynomial-size existential Tseitin encodings avoid this bound. It cannot be used as SAT hardness or as an ARC space lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, fully qualified.}
The independent 26-formula original-clause audit (19 SAT, 7 UNSAT) passed 208,216 assertions, 37,504 small-source assignment slots, 36,820 original source-index pair extreme records, 3,296 boundary-profile checks, and 8,722 exact indexed character accesses. The canonical repetitive SAT source of 2,203,400 physical bits emitted a 1,016-bit ordinary child; the matched UNSAT source of 11,341,264 bits emitted a 1,984-bit child, both below $\sqrt N$, but \emph{only on the certified repetitive family}. Nine Horn/dual-Horn/2SAT/high-width/mixed controls (5 SAT, 4 UNSAT) used paid recognition and total fallback. Twelve matched small-source UP--DPLL controls searched 1,240 nodes, while the child searched the same 1,240, before extra scanning, validation, failed attempts, policy switching, output address arithmetic, and memory. The Python peak was 55,639,037 bytes and the guest consumed a materialized child. No total-work advantage or native FLMV space achievement follows.

\textbf{DUPLICATION / RECONCILIATION.}
This builds on existing LSPV-2 sparse-affine and source-authenticated mixed-interface theorems, with classical parity and knowledge-compilation techniques. The restricted affine MCSP small-bias no-go remains a scoped adversarial control. Untrusted arbitrary-CNF grouping discovery, universal source/project-join profile generation, heredity, physical CONSTRUCTION-STRENGTHEN17 indexed-bit access and the arbitrary-decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ FLMV recurrence are \OPEN. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ are \OPEN.

The complete raw JSON, producer, independently implemented verifier, PDF and SHA-256 manifest are preserved in the reproducibility archive; persistent Library upload failed because the container was not shared with the Files service.


\paragraph{LSPV-2, latest charged adaptive residual-SAT compiler ledger (31 October 2026, 05:35 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM / PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending.}
A source-indexed \emph{private Krom projection} computes the actual boundary variable set from original higher-width source clauses, eliminates only the private width-two variables by fully authenticated Davis--Putnam resolution, and physically serializes an ordinary CNF child. For every boundary assignment $\beta$, the result satisfies
\[
G(\beta)=1\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad \exists P\,F(\beta,P)=1.
\]
A source-indexed ordinary resolution DAG certifies every derived resolvent and original-source UNSAT refutation; reverse elimination plus an original-clause check reconstructs YES witnesses. Failed size gates and non-Krom residuals revert to an exact charged source solver, not a false negative. The proof avoids enumerating all boundary assignments, but quadratic-size clause tables and source/proof histories remain fully retained and paid. In the star family
$\bigwedge_i(\neg a_i\lor p)\land\bigwedge_j(\neg p\lor b_j)$,
eliminating $p$ produces $k^2$ distinct clauses $(\neg a_i\lor b_j)$, necessary for any auxiliary-free boundary-only equivalent 2-CNF. This is a representation-specific lower bound, not an arbitrary-SAT resource lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} The independent producer/checker tested 53 source formulas (31 SAT, 22 UNSAT), verified 511 original-source resolution steps and 1,700 exact boundary projections, audited 25,536 original-indexed four-way S/U clause extremes and coherent endpoint/flip controls, and rejected a deliberately corrupted resolution pivot. On two repetitive proper-source controls, physically explicit $N=142{,}688$ SAT and $142{,}696$ UNSAT bits contracted to $M=264$ and $M=272$ bits, both below $\sqrt N$; but the full selected event costs were $1{,}045{,}918$ and $1{,}041{,}961$, versus baseline UP--DPLL costs $30{,}854$ and $21{,}853$. The aggregate controller paid 2,137,340 selected events versus 68,225 baseline, despite 119 versus 120 search nodes. Host-language event proxies and 10.8 MB Python peak allocation are \emph{not} native FLMV machine time/space.

\textbf{Prior compatible components.} LSPV-2 LAZY-INTERFACE-ACCESS269 exposed expensive repeated profile regeneration; CHECKPOINT-RANK-INDEX270 reduced repeated indexed child-profile evaluations with paid $2^b$ preprocessing and cached offsets; PREFIX-CERTIFIED-COVER271 gave a disjoint source-proved blocked/accepted boundary-cylinder projection but found a parity family where incomplete-prefix acceptance and child expansion invalidate naive cheap compression. All preserve the same-source SAT/UNSAT distinction and maintain source-indexed S/U extremes as adversarial controls, not the main research target. The new Krom compiler avoids $2^b$ profile enumeration for one restricted class, not for arbitrary CNF. Historical proofs and negative controls remain intact.

\textbf{OPEN.} No uniform all-input hereditary ordinary-SAT child emitter or arbitrary correct polynomial SAT decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ has been built with all policy-discovery, switching, failed trials, source queries, physical output and guest indexed access, ancestor state and simultaneous peak writable space charged at the actual FLMV thresholds. The proposed
$K'\le\max\{11/10,3/5+K/2\}$ and
$L'\le\max\{1/2,L/2\}$ remain \textbf{CONDITIONAL}; both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-2 HORN-FRONTIER273 (31 October 2026, 06:12 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL for executable controls.}
The original-source authenticated Horn chain \(R_k\), containing private
\(q_0,\ldots,q_k\) and boundary pairs \((a_i,b_i)\), has exact projected
boundary predicate
\[
  \exists q\,R_k
  \equiv \bigwedge_{\epsilon\in\{0,1\}^k}
           \left(\bigvee_{i=1}^k\neg t_{i,\epsilon_i}\right),
  \qquad t_{i,0}=a_i,\quad t_{i,1}=b_i.
\]
Every auxiliary-free boundary CNF equivalent to this projection, even
allowing arbitrary clause widths, needs at least \(2^k\) distinct clauses.
Each displayed clause has a \(k+1\)-step original-source resolution DAG.
The matched \(U_k=R_k\land\bigwedge_i(a_i\lor b_i)\) is UNSAT with a
\(3k+1\)-step original-source resolution refutation; \(R_k\) is SAT with
a checked original-source witness.  A computed index-addressed child
character accessor runs in \(O(k^2)\) arithmetic-word operations per query
without storing all child text, but physical output has
\(\Omega(k2^k)\) bits against an \(O(k\log k)\)-bit source.  Retaining
private variables or applying direct Horn forward chaining avoids this
representation-specific blowup; no unrestricted SAT runtime lower bound
follows.
The independent verification replayed 166 proof roots with 999 resolution
steps on 20 matched and renamed source instances (10 YES, 10 NO),
2,728 boundary assignments, 4,156 original signed-clause four-way
\(SS/SU/US/UU\) compatibility checks, and 20 rejected certificate
corruptions.  Twelve additional W1/S15, Horn, dual-Horn, Krom, affine,
and high-width adversarial controls were tested.  Baseline UP--DPLL
searched 193 nodes for the 20 main sources; all tested projections
failed the \(M\le\sqrt N\) physical child-size gate.  Python peak
allocation and event proxies are not native FLMV machine-space or
time exponents.  The result refines, without superseding, earlier
KROM-PROJECTION272 and published Horn-forgetting bounds.

\paragraph{LSPV-2 HORN-KROM-FOREST-INTERFACE274 and TREEWIDTH-MINOR-GATE275 (31 October 2026, 07:12--08:12 PDT).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM / PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending.}
For each source proper signed three-CNF clause
$C_j=(\ell_{j1}\lor\ell_{j2}\lor\ell_{j3})$, the prior
LSPV-2 forest-interface map makes private signed-occurrence variables
$y_{jk}$, negative Horn triples
$(\neg y_{j1}\lor\neg y_{j2}\lor\neg y_{j3})$ and Krom
links $(\ell_{jk}\lor y_{jk})\land(\neg\ell_{jk}\lor\neg y_{jk})$.
For every coherent source assignment $x$ the $y$ extension is
unique and $\exists y\,T(F)(x,y)\Leftrightarrow F(x)$.
The original clause has a three-step source-indexed resolution
derivation from the transformed clauses, permitting complete SAT-witness
lifting and original-source UNSAT-proof replay.  Although the Horn
component has primal treewidth at most $2$ and the Krom component is a
primal forest of width at most $1$, the \emph{full} interaction graph
obeys the new restricted exact bound
\[
  w=\operatorname{tw}(P(F))
  \ \le\ \operatorname{tw}(P(T(F)))
  \ \le\ \max\{w,5\}.
\]
The lower bound follows by contracting the occurrence link edges and
obtaining $P(F)$ as a minor.  The upper bound follows by attaching a
six-vertex leaf bag to any source tree-decomposition bag containing each
three-clause clique.  Thus for $w\ge5$ the full transformed width
equals source width, even though both constituents are independently
tractable.  This is a representation/compatibility gate, \emph{not} an
unconditional SAT complexity lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The standalone, independently checked 37 original SAT/UNSAT controls
(25/12) passed 71,787 explicit assertions, including 3,899 indexed
source SS/SU/US/UU pair modes, 1,283 source signed occurrences, 189
coherent single-flip transitions and 1,350 transformed-source proof
steps; four deliberate proof mutations were rejected. The paired
$K_7$-support SAT and UNSAT sources both have full graph width $6$,
versus separate Horn/Krom widths $2/1$.
For those matched sources, physically $N=21{,}712$-bit parents
produce ordinary $M=2{,}776$ and $2{,}872$-bit extracted children,
both failing $M\le\sqrt N$.  No input among the 37 met the proposed
power-short gate.  The controller computed exact small treewidth,
charged recognition, switches, source graph reads and full fallback;
Python host counters and retained memory are not native FLMV TM
bounds. The all-TRUE high-width SAT and locally refutable eight-clause
UNSAT controls show width is not a compulsory expense for every solver.

\textbf{OPEN.}
The source-authenticated all-input hereditary ordinary-SAT
$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ compiler with polynomially cheap discovery,
strictly power-short physical children, indexed child access, source,
guest/ancestor and simultaneously retained workspace accounting on the
actual FLMV machine model is unconstructed. The proposed
$K'\le\max\{11/10,3/5+K/2\}$ and
$L'\le\max\{1/2,L/2\}$ remain
\textbf{CONDITIONAL}; both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 DIRECTIONAL-SWAP-CERT-GATE285 (31 October 2026, 16:24 PDT; restricted exact mathematics, independent peer review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted directional certificate:} For a valid original D\"ocker duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) source, coherent complete assignment $a$, and originally falsified positive/negative clauses, let $p=\rho_+(a)$ and $q=\rho_-(a)$ be their separate exact minimum hitting costs. Because each variable occurs twice per sign, $p$ and $q$ are computable by the previously proved Gallai matching-cover construction. For \emph{every} satisfying $b$, with $\Delta=|b|-|a|$, the source-name-correct lower bound is
\[
d_H(a,b)\ge p+q+|\Delta-(p-q)|.
\]
For a fixed-weight Hamming slice, each move exchanging a zero and a one costs one, and $h(a)=\max(p,q)$ is an admissible \emph{consistent} A* heuristic: one exchange changes each $\rho_\pm$ by at most one. The result is a specialized optional search policy, \emph{not} an all-input polynomial algorithm or a mandatory resource for arbitrary deciders. Its no-import verifier checked 4,736 exhaustive small-source assignments, 1,109,376 directed source-witness inequalities, 145,344 one-swap consistency relations, 606 independently enumerated SS/SU/US/UU original-clause modes, 1,716 signed original occurrence S/U/inversion targets, 858 complete flip-trail steps, 24 BFS/A* fixed-slice verdicts and 30 malformed-input rejections, totaling 1,267,478 counted checks. The source-certified U198 UNSAT (six independently checked modules and 64 boundary assignments, 3,666 proof nodes) and V198 SAT (198-variable original-source witness) have identical directional endpoint and checkerboard signatures; negative fixed-weight slices cannot establish UNSAT. On SAT CUBE12, anchor 27 of weight four has $(p,q)=(2,0)$ and nearest same-slice model distance exactly two swaps; the directional bound improves on $\lceil(p+q)/2\rceil=1$. CUBE12 weight-four BFS expands 76 states using 3,648 literal probes; matching A* expands 14 but uses 17,376 literal probes and 348 matching calls, so node reductions \emph{do not} imply charged savings. Previously verified GATE244/284 matching repair, GATE283/REFEREE251 logical XOR accounting, and LSPV-4 M22 component closure are explicitly credited and preserved. Offline logical state XOR is not native FLMV $d_{\mathrm{phys}}$; physical index/work-tape operations, guest execution, target-child re-encoding, and peak simultaneous FLMV workspace remain UNMEASURED. The full producer/independent verifier, original clauses, 69,674 source-pair records, 16 MB raw result, PDF and SHA manifest reside in the complete conversation ZIP, SHA256 \texttt{478ca6ea38314b6838bff6a87aae3c677e9c9bb869a5280a439d3b5fb2338b5e}; a GitHub compact report/code/summary mirror passed exact readback at \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-31_1624_LSPV-3_DIRECTIONAL_SWAP_CERT_GATE285_PUBLIC.tex}, \path{experiments/arc/lspv3_285/gate285_compact.py}, and \path{results/raw/arc/lspv3_285/summary285.json}. The next gate remains a uniformly discoverable, both-label-correct, hereditary target-language child compiler with full source/tape/selector/index tariffs; no numerical SAT-to-target FLMV transfer or all-input arbitrary-$A$ contraction is established. $P$ versus $NP$ and both possible directions remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 BRIDGELESS-TWOEDGE-SELECTOR-GATE281 (31 October 2026, 12:24 PDT; restricted exact theorem, independent mathematical review pending).}
\textbf{KNOWN Edmonds perfect matching polytope, PROVED/EXACT restricted source-certified SAT terminal:} for any valid D\"ocker duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) original source, construct the cubic positive original-clause multigraph $G_+$ with original variables as edge labels. A negative clause is \emph{dangerous} when its three labels are pairwise nonincident in $G_+$. If $G_+$ is bridgeless and at most two negative clauses are dangerous, choosing one forbidden source label from each and deleting those at most two edges leaves a perfect matching: the uniform vector $z_e=1/3$ satisfies all Edmonds degree and odd-cut inequalities, and any two forbidden edges have expected occupancy at most $2/3$ under a convex-combination matching distribution. The surviving perfect matching yields a globally coherent source assignment; nondangerous negative clauses cannot have all three edges in a matching, and dangerous clauses each have a selected FALSE label. The sign-reversed rule also holds. This removes GATE280's \emph{bipartiteness} hypothesis, but is only a sufficient SAT island; failure of the matching policy is \emph{not an UNSAT certificate}. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, independently checked:} 11 small valid SAT sources, 4,736 full original assignments, 456 independent two-edge deletion tests, 141,464 original-clause SS/SU/US/UU mode checks, 4,656 original indexed S/U/inverted occurrence demands, 245 source-indexed resolutions and 5,880 proof truth rows. The valid 198-variable/264-clause D\"ocker UNSAT source remains outside the terminal (both dangerous counts 126); independent six-module cofactor refutations use 3,666 DPLL proof nodes followed by 64 rejected boundary assignments. On small SAT controls the complete UP-DPLL baseline uses 58 states and 2,369 literal tests; matching selector uses 17 blossom calls, 3,186 adjacency visits and 47,875 conservative matching proxy operations, which are not native FLMV charges. The full controller rejects malformed inputs, attempts only checked SAT witnesses, and otherwise uses exact potentially exponential DPLL; it retains source-scale graph state, emits no hereditary target child, and has not transported the numerical FLMV SAT region. \textbf{OPEN:} a polynomial all-valid-target decider, target-hereditary $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$, fully charged SAT-to-target FLMV bit-access reduction and P versus NP.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 VERTEX-COVER-POLICY-GATE276 (31 October 2026, 07:24 PDT; mathematical peer review pending).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (Vertex Cover NP-completeness; classical strong-Krom clause-hitting characterization), \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} (explicit reduction), \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} (10 original-source paired controls), and \textbf{OPEN} (universal ARC). For a simple graph \(G=(V,E)\) with \(q=|V|\), replicate each positive proper clause \((x_u\lor x_v\lor z_{e,j})\) on edge \(e=\{u,v\}\) for \(j=1,\ldots,q+1\), and add a private proper three-variable guard consisting either of one positive clause or all eight sign patterns. The two sources have opposite SAT labels, each with an explicitly checked all-TRUE witness or seven-step original-clause Resolution refutation, yet their minimum static strong-Krom backdoor sizes are \emph{identical}:
\[
\beta_{\mathrm{Krom}}(F_G^+)=\beta_{\mathrm{Krom}}(F_G^-)=\tau(G)+1.
\]
Thus exact minimum strong-Krom backdoor acquisition is NP-complete \emph{even on trivial all-positive dual-Horn SAT instances}. A controller can bypass this optimization with its all-TRUE witness; a similarly authenticated fixed UNSAT guard bypasses it on the paired NO instances. No arbitrary-decider computation cost follows from the backdoor size. The independent checker passed 536,605 counted checks, including 524,800 exhaustive small assignments, 9,832 source-clause pair-extreme mode checks, 205 original local signed-occurrence extrema and inversions, 130 coherent Gray/complement states, and all five seven-step Resolution refutations. Source-authenticated selected work on the five-cycle paired sources was 513/549 operations versus 1,686/31,802 instrumented UP-first DPLL operations (SAT/UNSAT); these are not native FLMV machine comparisons. The controller constructs no physical ordinary SAT children and offers no hereditary all-input \(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) resource recurrence. The affine-small-bias theorem remains restricted to source-independent affine MCSP, and P versus NP remains \OPEN. This entry sharpens GATE268 and GATE271, preserving their verified mathematics rather than replacing them.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 HORN-AFFINE-MIXED-GATE274 (31 October 2026, 05:29 PDT; independent mathematical review pending).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM TRANSFER / PROVED/EXACT restricted / COMPUTATIONAL.}
A direct signed-occurrence reduction takes any proper 3CNF source $F$ with
$n$ variables and $m$ clauses to a single proper 3CNF $T(F)$ with
$n+6m$ variables and $13m$ original clauses.  It is the conjunction
of an individually satisfiable negative-ternary Horn component $H$
and an individually satisfiable affine/XOR component $E$, with
$\exists y,t\, T(F)(x,y,t)\Longleftrightarrow F(x)$ for every coherent
source assignment $x$, and
$\#\mathrm{SAT}(T(F))=2^{3m}\#\mathrm{SAT}(F)$.
Therefore identifying a jointly feasible assignment from separately
successful Horn and affine component solvers is NP-complete on this
authenticated image unless P=NP.  This is an explicit incarnation
of Schaefer's known constraint-language boundary, not a new
unconditional lower bound for arbitrary machines.
The independent finite checker passed eleven same-source cases
(eight SAT, three UNSAT), including W1/SWAP6 signed22 and renamed
controls, complete original-source endpoint/complement/Gray and
$SS,SU,US,UU$ adversarial matrices, and resolution proofs for source
UNSAT.  In small controls, full mixed DPLL took 117/207 nodes versus
six/seven on the recovered ordinary source children; no full
mixed baseline was measured on larger sources.  The child is merely
linear-size, descendants are not closed, and no same-machine FLMV
resource recurrence is obtained.  \textbf{OPEN:} universal all-input
both-label hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ with charged
discovery, failed trials, output, access and simultaneous workspace.
P versus NP remains \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted fixed-module control).}
The authenticated $W_1$ module has 15 variables and 20 proper strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$ clauses; an independently checked 76-row Resolution object proves
UNSAT. After replacing one private variable by two boundary names, exhaustive
source-clause evaluation gives the U relation mask $4$, while the explicit
satisfiable S module has mask $15$. Hence a cyclic U/S word is SAT exactly when
no two U modules are adjacent, and one uniform deterministic transfer program
decides both answer classes without receiving an answer bit. Independent
readback accepted the eight-module 168-row UNSAT object and the SAT witness.
This establishes a connected two-answer \emph{fixed-template} family, not
arbitrary-SAT coverage: private width is the constant 14 and no polynomial
module-acquisition theorem is known. Report:
\texttt{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-28\_2025\_LSPV-3\_W1\_RING\_TRANSFER149.tex}.
Universal polynomial discovery, a one-child $N^\lambda$ successor,
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN.

\medskip
\textbf{SOURCE-SUPPORT150 and GRID-FRONTIER-SCC151 (restricted candidate
controls; publication repair pending).}
For the fixed $CD^{k-2}U$ transfer chain, deleting the clauses of any complete
module makes the source satisfiable.  Consequently every UNSAT subset of
original source clauses touches all $k$ modules, although a two-bit transfer
still decides the family.  The complete source package is readable in Library,
but no canonical marker-valid GitHub report was found.

Separately, the grid control constructs connected strict exact-$(2,2)$ sources
whose variable primal graph has an $H\times H$ grid minor and hence every-order
syntactic frontier at least $H/16$, while one authenticated selector subfamily
has a constant-depth 2CNF/SCC decision tree.  This again separates syntactic
frontier from decision hardness.  The retrieved GitHub report body lacks both
the mandatory first-line role marker and final verification sentinel.  An
additive handoff asks LSPV-3 to republish both bodies without changing their
scientific content.
\medskip
\textbf{ONEBIT-POLARITY-CERT152 (PROVED/EXACT lift; proof discovery OPEN;
marker-safe retry pending).}
For every proper source clause $(a_i\lor b_i\lor c_i)$, the two-clause lift
$(\neg z\lor a_i\lor t_i)\land(\neg t_i\lor b_i\lor c_i)$ has exact
existential projection $\neg z\lor\exists X F$.  Thus the exposed boundary
relation has only the values $\mathsf{TOP}$ and $\neg z$.  Verifying a bare
$\mathsf{TOP}$ entry is NP-complete and verifying a bare $\neg z$ entry is
coNP-complete under the source-authentication interpretation; a complete
branch-to-2CNF UNSAT proof exists but is exponential.  The 71,137-assertion
reproducer, including the 8,192-leaf W1 control, was independently rerun.
The current GitHub report lacks both mandatory ownership markers and awaits an
owner-preserved marker-only retry.  A small table is not a polynomial proof
generator or arbitrary-SAT child constructor.
\medskip
\textbf{BUCKET-PROVENANCE153 (PROVED/RESTRICTED producer; marker-safe retry
pending).}
Given an authenticated width-$w$ elimination order, exact dynamic-programming
elimination keeps source-clause identities and emits either a SAT witness or a
source-authenticated Resolution/LRAT DAG.  The declared bounds are
$O(n3^w)$ distinct clauses and $O(n9^w)$ candidate resolution pairs, so this
is polynomial for $w=O(\log n)$ with a fixed constant.  Independent replay
passes, including the Connected Interface archive dependency.  However, two
actual ordinary-3CNF descendant levels physically grow from 1808 to 2104 to
2496 bits and from 1808 to 2024 to 2424 bits.  Thus the producer supplies a
sound restricted certificate route, not the required power-short child.

retains it as a restricted candidate pending an owner-preserved marker-only

\textbf{BALANCED-SIGNING-TWO-WITNESSES154
(PROVED/RESTRICTED candidate, independently rerun).}
For any supplied proper degree-four 3-uniform scope system, a polynomial

which every clause has both signs; consequently both $0^n$ and $1^n$
are witnesses.  This gives an easy signing of a supplied scope system
but does not preserve the answer of an arbitrary input signing and is
not a SAT reduction.  The 78,469-assertion reproducer passed.

\medskip
\textbf{EXACT22-FOUR-FIBERS155 (RESTRICTED/COMPUTATIONAL; independent referee pending).}
On one connected 15-variable, 20-clause proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
support, four balanced signings exhibit every one-variable exact projection:
$\varnothing$, $\{0\}$, $\{1\}$, and $\{0,1\}$.
Exhaustive original-clause evaluation gives respectively $(0,0)$,
$(168,0)$, $(0,168)$, and $(474,387)$ assignment counts by
the fixed boundary sign; four independently replayed, source-ID-authenticated
Resolution proofs exclude the empty fibers. This is a finite same-support
control, not an asymptotic SAT hardness theorem.
A separate four-clause balanced strict-occurrence guard $H(y,u,v)$ has
$\exists u,v\,H\equiv\top$; consequently the disconnected image $F\wedge H$
has an exact $y$-table that is full iff $F$ is SAT and empty iff
$F$ is UNSAT. Exact table recognition on that image is respectively
NP- and coNP-complete given the established strict-$(2,2)$ NP-completeness.
For connected components, a hypothetical uniform polynomial exact-table
producer would decide all strict-$(2,2)$ SAT through a nonadaptive
polynomial Turing reduction; connected prescribed-fiber many-one hardness is
\OPEN. A small table, a verified refutation, and an efficiently acquired
summary remain different properties.
See \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_0124_LSPV-3_EXACT22_FOUR_FIBERS155.tex}
and the source-authenticated reproducibility files adjacent to it.
No general answer-preserving one-child shrinker or P-vs-NP resolution follows.
P versus NP remains \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3, TSEITIN-GUARD-PORTFOLIO158 (29 October 2026, 04:54 PDT; restricted theorem, external referee pending).}
For connected cubic-graph Tseitin CNFs, a two-clause connected proper-3CNF
guard with one boundary variable has either full or empty exact boundary
projection depending on total vertex parity. Literal restrictions remove the
guard entirely, so the known exponential general-Resolution lower bound for
odd-charge cubic expanders transfers to both negative fibers and, by
polynomial certificate-to-Resolution simulation, to the precisely restricted

UNSAT certificates and SAT witnesses. Thus Resolution proof length is not
a general SAT runtime lower bound. This construction is \emph{ordinary}
connected proper 3CNF, not strict exact-(2,2); degree-preserving transport
and universal all-UNSAT certificate coverage remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3, EXACT22-RING-RESOLUTION159 (29 October 2026, 05:24 PDT; restricted theorem, external referee pending).}
Closing LSPV-2's strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ port-wire chain with
physical degree-preserving occurrence swaps gives, for each $k\ge2$,

unsigned clause scopes, with $15k$ variables, $20k$ clauses,

The latter admits an original-source ordinary Resolution refutation
with $O(k)$ derived steps and $O(k\log k)$ encoded bits, acquired

The standalone computational audit passed 135,964 assertions over

and RUP-hint checking. A formally verified external LRUP/LRAT checker

has $T=O(N^6)$ and total footprint $O(N^2)$. These are \emph{restricted

\paragraph{LSPV-3, MULTIPORT-PRODUCT163 (29 October 2026; PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED conditional on an exhaustively checked 15-variable source lemma; external referee pending).}
On an explicit connected proper strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ source $G$,
the two-negative-$x_4$ puncture profile forces $A=B=0$ whenever $x_4=0$.
Swapping outgoing and incoming negative occurrences between copies in a path
or a simple ring forces every module's $x_4$ signal to agree.
For $k$ modules and independently flipped three-bit ports $t_i$,
the \emph{entire} exact $3k$-port projection is
$\bigwedge_{i=1}^{k}(\neg a_i\vee\neg b_i\vee c_i)$ after the chosen port flips.
Each formula has $15k$ variables and $20k$ clauses, and the full relation has
$7^k$ feasible port assignments yet a $k$-clause summary.

patterns, and 960 independently checked original-physical-CNF pinned cofactors

\textbf{MULTIPORT-PRODUCT163 (PROVED/EXACT restricted product;
COMPUTATIONAL finite source audit; sharing layer OPEN).}

three exposed ports is a ternary clause. A path or ring of $k$ blocks with
disjoint exposed triples therefore has joint projection equal to the Cartesian
product of $k$ ternary clauses, with $7^k$ feasible port rows; every unpinned
member is SAT. Independent reproduction recovered the 272 source models,

checks. The exposed triples share no logical clause variables, so this is not

original specified source and splice pattern. A distinct mixed-mode

be substituted context-free after future hidden-port occurrence splices.
For the same authenticated 15-variable source, the hidden $p=x_4$
four-port relation is
$Q_f(t,p)=(t\ne3)\wedge[(t\ne1)\vee(p=f)]$, where complementing all four
occurrences of $p$ leaves the exposed ternary relation $t\ne3$ unchanged.
One matched negative-port exchange forces physical $p$ equality.
Therefore two modules pinned to exposed tuples $(1,1)$ are SAT in modes
$(0,0)$ but UNSAT in modes $(0,1)$, despite identical local three-port
projections. A connected $k$-module mixed-mode chain has exactly the
$O(k)$-size one-bit composition
$\exists p\,\bigwedge_i Q_{f_i}(t_i,p)$, with uniform polynomial
SAT-witness and relational UNSAT-certificate acquisition on this
\emph{restricted recognizable grammar}. These certificates are not
yet LRUP or original-clause 2CNF-SCC proofs.
The full original-CNF independent HiGHS audit checked 455 pinned

acquisition; independently rerun).}
For an explicitly supplied CNF whose variables are partitioned into

blocks, deterministic Davis--Putnam elimination produces either a model or an

program handles SAT and UNSAT without an answer-class bit; the supplied exact22
chain tests 356 cofactors (245 SAT, 111 UNSAT) and rejects forged proofs.
This is a genuine polynomial acquisition theorem for a path-local grammar,
not arbitrary SAT or a general one-child shrinker.  The GitHub TeX body is

\paragraph{LSPV-3, CONTEXT-VALUE-AFFINE171 (29 October 2026, 18:24 PDT; restricted affine information-value theorem, external referee pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted):} In the detached reusable-summary model for
$A_{\alpha,\beta}(X):\langle\alpha,X\rangle=\beta$ and all later pin contexts,
any fixed-length summary has $L\ge b+1$ bits; storing the original affine row
attains equality with polynomial preparation and continuation. Matrix-graph
affine sources $z=Mu$ with $b=2h$ require $L\ge h^2$, also attained up to
lower-order encoding overhead by retaining $M$. These are offline-all-context
information bounds, \emph{not} time lower bounds for a joint solver seeing
$A,B$, and not exponential in encoded source size.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED (restricted greedy policy):} A target-specific
marginal-entropy policy fails on the two XOR shares $\alpha_i,\beta$ for
context $X=e_i$: each alone has zero target information, while the pair
determines the SAT answer. Positive-marginal-only greed can choose an
arbitrarily costly exact direct-answer macro over two one-bit source probes.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (conditional ledger):} If complete-state integer rank
starts below $P$, has total upward drift at most $G$, and fully charged
intervals between strict decreases each cost at most $C$, then same-run
total time is at most $(P+G+1)C$. Pure affine $A(X,Y)$ with affine
continuation $B(X,Z)$ has a uniform Gaussian projected-summary controller,
verified SAT witness reconstruction and original-row XOR UNSAT certificates,
with conservative polynomial bit-time and space. No all-SAT acquisition
result follows; $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.
Published report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_1824_LSPV-3_CONTEXT_VALUE_AFFINE171_PUBLIC.tex};
repro and compact raw results are adjacent; full local PDF/archive not
GitHub-synced. Handoff:
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-29_1824_CONTEXT_VALUE_AFFINE171_LSPV1_LSPV5_HANDOFF.md}.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 CONTEXTUAL-TORUS-SHARES172 (restricted exact value synergy).}
On the previously verified connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ torus/AND-connector
family with $n=L^2+45$ and primal treewidth at least $L-2$, a two-bit
whole-variable sign mask yields four canonical physical templates,
two SAT and two UNSAT, with sign probes $a=r$, $b=t\oplus r$ and
SAT label $t$.  Under a declared uniform four-template prior, each
individual sign probe has zero target-information gain but the pair has
one bit.  A single deterministic source-regenerating controller charges
failed 2CNF applicability, all sign probes, source authentication,
connectedness, fixed finite connector checking, SAT witness validation,
and template-semantic UNSAT acquisition in polynomial time on this
canonical promise.  The information-value result is exact for the
specified restricted probe model; neither high treewidth nor the
two-probe floor implies a general SAT time lower bound.  Details:
\texttt{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29\_LSPV3\_CONTEXTUAL\_TORUS\_SHARES172.tex}.
P versus NP remains \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 PARITY-POLICY-SYNDROME173 (29 October 2026, 19:27 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted pre-materialized indexed parity-bit probe model; independent review pending).}
For a public menu of raw coordinate and pre-materialized triple-parity probes, an exact deterministic adaptive controller must on every successful path query rows spanning target $t$ over $\mathbb F_2$. Thus optimal worst-case \emph{data-probe count} equals the minimum number of available rows whose XOR is $t$. An independently defined uncovered-target-support potential has a necessary zero terminal value and decreases by at most three per charged primitive probe, giving $P_{\rm data}+Q_{\rm data}\ge\lceil|\operatorname{supp}(t)|/3\rceil$ within this limited menu. Finding whether an all-ones target on $3q$ coordinates admits a $q$-probe policy is NP-complete by the classical X3C incidence reduction, despite legal raw-coordinate reads. A disjoint-triple promise has a uniformly polynomial optimal planner with exact cost $\sum_j\min(k_j,4-k_j)$, handling both answer labels. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 640 X3C metadata controls, 770 exhaustive adaptive comparisons and 4680 disjoint target masks passed. Raw JSON SHA256 \texttt{b2a4335ea0f49805772238dcdd33ff4fe62c1d19ddee0dad226d4c6066e76b96}. Original-source retention, paid parity materialization, XOR synergy, duplicate redundancy, score/discovery work, failed policies and physical machine distinctions remain explicit. This is neither an ordinary SAT lower bound nor novel coding-theoretic hardness. Prior WEIGHTED-SUMMARY-SCHEDULER170 is retained. Sources:
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_1927_LSPV-3_PARITY_POLICY_SYNDROME173.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_1927_LSPV-3_PARITY_POLICY_SYNDROME173_repro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_1927_LSPV-3_PARITY_POLICY_SYNDROME173_raw.json},
and \path{research/coordination/2026-10-29_1927_PARITY_POLICY_SYNDROME173_FIVE_ROLE_HANDOFF.md}.
Next \textbf{OPEN} lemma: source-authenticated full-interface module-tree policy with polynomial acquisition and compatibility joins on recognized 2CNF-plus-bounded-affine-codimension modules. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.
