\paragraph{LSPV-3, LAMINAR-PARITY-POLICY174 (29 October 2026, 19:54 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted laminar indexed-parity-source model; independent referee pending).}
For a public laminar family of materialized XOR masks containing all coordinate
probes, with positive weights and public target $t$, a uniform deterministic
controller recognizes the laminar input, computes an exact optimal weighted
probe plan, reconstructs its queried-mask XOR certificate, and returns the
correct target parity on both answer labels.  The two-state inclusion-tree
recurrence
\[
D_i(a)=w_i(a\oplus t_i),\qquad
D_u(a)=\min_{z\in\{0,1\}}
\bigl\{zw_u+\sum_{v\in\mathrm{child}(u)}D_v(a\oplus z)\bigr\}
\]
takes $z=0$ when the root or an internal node is a synthetic unavailable
probe.  The minimum cost is $D_{\mathrm{root}}(0)$; every adaptive exact
transcript must span the target row, so data-dependent adaptation cannot
improve this declared weighted source-probe price.  This is a non-disjoint,
nested extension of the disjoint-triple policy in SYNDROME173, not a
general-SAT theorem or an unrestricted optimal-policy discovery algorithm.
The full cost charges laminar recognition $O(M^2m)$, DP/traceback, each
source probe at weight $w_S$, paid view construction if not previously
materialized, certificate verification, and actual peak workspace.  For
$w_{\max}\le N^c$ the one-controller same-run indexed-RAM time and space
are polynomial.  A nonlaminar menu falls back to charged original-coordinate
reads, without claiming optimality.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED (restricted heuristic):}
On a four-bit laminar instance, the two complementary XOR halves each have
zero initial contextual target-information value but jointly reveal the
target; a strictly positive-singleton-gain greedy policy may choose a direct
probe of price $K$ instead of the two halves of total price 2.  The
competitive ratio $K/2$ is unbounded as allowed weights grow.
The notation $p_S\mid_{v_{\Phi}}$ denotes a state-dependent score, not
probability conditioning or intrinsic datum value; discovery and acquisition
remain charged.  The prior WEIGHTED-SUMMARY-SCHEDULER170 peak-table
memory-order theorem remains a distinct valid no-recomputation baseline.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} A seed-1741029 independent Dijkstra oracle matched
the DP on 7,584 target masks across 144 generated weighted laminar families,
$m=2,\ldots,8$; 463 exact source-answer reconstructions matched, with
both possible answers observed.  Raw JSON SHA-256
\texttt{d36248f1cea3e44dc31e523481666ed3ee82c0ee36448e7f9bcc0f0a83378852}.
The source-retention, affine/Tseitin, joint-input solver and one-tape
transport controls remain easy; the model gives no arbitrary-SAT lower bound.
Verified report and code:

ledger to source-authenticated full-interface moving module trees with

Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ is proved.

\paragraph{LSPV-3, BATCH-LAMINAR-POLICY175 (29 October 2026, 20:27 PDT; restricted theorem, independent referee pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted, peer review pending):} For a publicly supplied laminar family of positively priced physically materialized XOR-source masks containing all singletons, a single deterministic polynomial-time controller computes the \emph{exact minimum total paid source-probe price} for an arbitrary batch of $k$ requested parity targets, among \emph{all} deterministic answer-dependent adaptive controllers using that fixed source-view menu.  Write each coordinate's batch signature as $c_i=W_{*,i}\in\mathbb F_2^k$, and let $\mathcal C=\{0,c_1,\ldots,c_m\}$, of size $\le m+1$ regardless of $k$.  On the laminar inclusion tree, an optimal label assignment exists entirely inside $\mathcal C$: simultaneously replacing every unpinned label outside $\mathcal C$ by $0$ never increases the positively weighted number of unequal-label edges.  Its exact tree recurrence is
\[
 D_u(a)=\min_{b\in\mathcal C}
 \left(\sum_{v\in\mathrm{child}(u)}D_v(b)+w_u\mathbf 1[b\ne a]\right),
 \qquad D_{\{i\}}(a)=w_i\mathbf1[a\ne c_i],
\]
with unavailable synthetic full-root mask pinned to label $0$.  The extracted mask set's coefficient XOR is explicitly checked against all target rows.  At each successful deterministic adaptive transcript, the queried mask span must contain the entire target row space, since otherwise a source perturbation in its orthogonal complement changes one required answer while preserving the observed transcript.  This gives a full per-source optimum price, not a SAT lower bound.

Define the independent certified contextual \emph{information-value rank} $\Phi_W(s)=\operatorname{rank}(W)-\dim(\operatorname{rowspan}(W)\cap V(s))$; marginal scores $p_S\mid_{v_\Phi}$ are $0$ or $1$, may exhibit XOR synergy, and do \emph{not} pay for useful zero-initial-value shares.  The \emph{charged execution account} $\Omega(s)=\sum_{S\in J\ {\rm unread}}w_S$ telescopes along the reconstructed fixed optimum plan; recognition, score discovery, failed nonlaminar recognition, DP, source access, verification, caching, output reconstruction, writable state and peak memory receive additional explicit polynomial charges.  The unrestricted formula-retention control remains legal.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the complete local seeded test suite compared 240 laminar instances against 80,000 exhaustive source-mask subsets and checked 720 source/batch answers, plus a 64-variable, 128-target batch using $\le65$ labels.  Full standalone LaTeX report and a separately source-verified GitHub compact reproducer and raw seed summary are at
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_2027_LSPV-3_BATCH_LAMINAR_POLICY175.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_2027_LSPV-3_BATCH_LAMINAR_POLICY175_repro.py}, and
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-29_2027_LSPV-3_BATCH_LAMINAR_POLICY175_raw.json}.
The prior single-target laminar planner174, generic batch-rank source-transcript floor211, SOURCE-RUP-CHAIN164, and WEIGHTED-SUMMARY-SCHEDULER170 remain distinct verified baselines.  Finding a polynomially discovered source-authenticated strict22 moving-interface class with these signatures is \textbf{OPEN}, as are $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.

\medskip
\textbf{OCCURRENCE-LAZY-MERGE176 (PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent referee pending).}
For every proper width-$k$ source CNF with $m$ clauses and $n$ variables,
a source-parent-authenticated streaming controller reproduces exactly the
full unit-propagation closure of \emph{all first-generation} non-tautological
opposite-occurrence resolvents, without persistently storing them.
Writing $P=\sum_xd_x^+d_x^-$, the charged indexed-RAM bounds per partial
prefix are $T=O(N+(n+1)^2mk+(n+1)Pk^2)$ and
$S=O(N+(mk+nk)\log N)$ bits, including index rebuilds, failed scans,
restarts of source UP and proof records. This is not a bound on the whole
adaptive SAT branch DAG. The connected proper width-three grid
$G_{r,s}=\bigwedge_i(x\vee a\vee u_i)\land
\bigwedge_j(\neg x\vee a\vee v_j)$ has $rs$ \emph{distinct}
non-tautological merge resolvents with additive forbidden-cube weight
$rs/8$ but exact union density
$\tfrac12(1-2^{-r})(1-2^{-s})<\tfrac12$.
Its source clause equity is exactly $Q_{\rm positive}=2^s$ and
$Q_{\rm negative}=2^r$. At the all-$u,v$-false partial prefix
one resolution infers $a=1$ while source-UP alone cannot;
full assignment union already excluded by source clauses gives zero
marginal learned-clause gain.
This falsifies additive scores \emph{even after deduplicating resolvents},
and demonstrates that a useful contextual forced-variable gain must be
charged separately from discovery and source access.
The grid is \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$, and its obvious
$a=1$ witness is a required easy control.
Independent script checks: 4063 eager/lazy partial-prefix outcomes,
720 complement identities, and eight grid controls, plus SAT/UNSAT,
Horn, 2SAT and XOR cases; selected-work and peak-space measurements
are COMPUTATIONAL, not universal lower bounds.

\paragraph{LSPV-3, PAIRED-EXTREME-CONTROLLER241 (29 October 2026, 22:28 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted paired-pivot 2CNF elimination, independent
referee pending); COMPUTATIONAL (finite hostile controls).}
Following the user's active clause-indexed $S/U$ extreme-inversion direction
(issue \#3), for every signed original occurrence $x_{i,j}$ with negative
bit $\nu_{ij}$, local all-literals-SAT requires $a_i=1\oplus\nu_{ij}$
and local all-literals-UNSAT requires $a_i=\nu_{ij}$; inversion swaps them.
For shared original clauses, $SS=UU$ is feasible iff there is no
opposite-sign shared variable and $SU=US$ is feasible iff there is no
same-sign shared variable. Incompatibility of all four extreme modes does
\emph{not} imply ordinary disjunctive formula UNSAT.
For an arbitrary proper binary source $G=\bigwedge_jD_j$ and fresh
private pivots $p_j$, the source-authenticated proper ternary construction
$F_G=\bigwedge_j[(D_j\vee p_j)\wedge(D_j\vee\neg p_j)]$
is identically equivalent to $G$; each paired source pivot resolves
to $D_j$ and forbids the entire partial cube falsifying $D_j$.
A deterministic fully charged paired recognizer and known 2SAT SCC solver
decides this restricted class polynomially. A deliberately pivot-first,
nonlearning UP-DPLL on the stated UNSAT paired controls visits exactly
$2^{M+2}-1$ recursion states, versus a \emph{core-first} DPLL with just
7 states on identical formulas: no generic DPLL or SAT lower bound follows.
On a paired SAT family fixed reflected Gray scanning takes $2^M+1$
full candidates, while one authenticated first-clause repair flip finds
a SAT witness, although the \emph{complete controller overhead can exceed}
UP-DPLL. Across 15 paired and 15 adverse controls 16{,}824 exact pair-mode
checks and 2{,}352 multi-clause checks passed; paired UNSAT $M=14$ used
65{,}535 pivot-first DPLL nodes/4{,}439{,}948 selected work against
10{,}236 controller work, whereas paired SAT $M=12$ cost 5{,}389 controller
versus 1{,}430 DPLL selected work. Strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ W1 UNSAT
also had controller overhead; this class is not the proven paired class.
Work counts are an instrumented proxy, not exact machine steps or CDCL.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 MULTI-ANCHOR246 (30 October 2026, 03:24 PDT): certified four-anchor exclusion.}
\PROVED\ (restricted, independent referee pending).
For an original-variable partial cube $\rho$, designate $V$ to be any
\emph{subset} of the free variables.  If $k$ source-authenticated certificates
each force $d_H(b|_V,a_i)\ge r_i$ for every full satisfying extension $b$
of $\rho$, a capped-distance dynamic program tests joint feasibility in
$O(A+k|V|\prod_i(r_i+1))$ indexed-RAM operations, where $A$ includes
radius-certificate acquisition, source access and checking.  Critically,
every named source witness clause for an excluded nearby $V$ assignment
must be supported entirely in $\operatorname{dom}(\rho)\cup V$; otherwise
one may not disregard other free variables.  If the all-threshold
distance state is unreachable, the \emph{entire} original-variable cube
$\rho$ is forbidden.  Four radius-two anchors $111,100,010,001$
have empty joint feasibility despite every proper subfamily feasible.
The original proper-3CNF source
$G^+(h;x,y,z)\land G^-(h;u,v,w)$ with twelve guarded clauses per
module supplies two authenticated whole cubes $h=0$ and $h=1$
and an exact restricted UNSAT certificate.  It is not signed exact22:
an ordinary pinned 2SAT-SCC solver finds this contradiction
more cheaply, so neither SAT complexity breakthrough nor novel
post-2SAT inference is claimed.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (matched input; source-indexed controls).
Eleven SAT/UNSAT sources passed 6,968 pair-extreme compatibility
checks, 992 triple-mode checks, coherent endpoints and complements,
original-occurrence $S/U$ targets and inversions, intermediate flip
trails, signed $N_0,\ldots,N_3$, source resolvent-cube replay and
independent witness/UNSAT proof-tree verification.
Guarded UNSAT selected costs:
UP-first DPLL 35 nodes/1935 events,
lean four-anchor 1536 events,
mandatory full clause-extreme diagnostic 15600,
and 2SAT SCC 310.  W1 strict22 UNSAT instead cost baseline
11857, lean 11909, full diagnostic 22029, without DPLL improvement.
These are instrumented heterogeneous logical events rather than
physical random-access machine steps or TM head-crossing counts.

\textbf{SIGNED-NAE-EULER247 (30 October, 04:51 PDT): PROVED/EXACT,
restricted; independent mathematical referee pending.}
For a proper signed 3CNF consisting of pairs $(D_j,\overline D_j)$
and with every original variable occurring in exactly two distinct
base clauses, a deterministic signed Euler-trail construction produces
and source-verifies a full SAT assignment.  Its original-variable dual
multigraph has degree three at clause vertices; adding a hub edge per
vertex gives an Eulerian even graph.  Removing hub edges yields variable
trails.  Assign successive variable bits so two paired signed literal
truths at each base clause are opposite.  Every base clause and
complement then have one TRUE and one FALSE literal.
The fully charged recognizer, adjacency and Euler construction,
assignment, source reads, and final witness replay take
$O(M+n)$ indexed word-RAM work and words with normalized integer IDs
and stable bounded-key radix sorting (or $O(M\log M)$ comparison sorting).
No hidden SAT answer or witness is supplied.  The statement covers
signed frustrated incidences but not arbitrary strict exact-$(2,2)$ CNF.
The known monotone NAE special case is not claimed as novel.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT complement-invariant extension; FALSIFIED/RETIRED
fixed two-witness policy.}
For SAT complement-invariant $H$, any one nonempty added clause $C$
remains satisfiable by choosing a witness of $H$ or its full complement.
Two added clauses can reject a particular chosen witness/complement pair
even while the extended formula is SAT (explicit paired $K_4$ control).
This does not establish UNSAT or solve the general case.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ 13 clause-indexed SAT/UNSAT hostile controls and all
4096 signed $K_4$ incidence assignments passed independent exhaustive
checks.  Checks include original-occurrence local $S_j/U_j$ targets,
both inversions, indexed $SS/SU/US/UU$ pair matrices, 504 signed
occurrences, 4976 four-mode pair records, 133712 coherent pair trials,
2045824 complement literal-count checks, 189076 source-resolvent
forbidden-cube extensions and 103696 complete formula masks.
K4 signed SAT baseline: UP-first DPLL 6 nodes/256 instrumented events;
lean Euler controller 141; mandatory complete clause-extreme audit
14792.  W1 strict22 UNSAT: 151 nodes/11935 baseline events versus
12016 lean and 105645 full audit.  All failed detectors and fallback
work are charged; explicit quadratic pair-matrix output is not
part of the linear witness algorithm.  These event counts do not
measure native RAM/TM time or industrial CDCL.  The full local
reproducibility package (producer SHA256
\texttt{0427120316da65512b80b53e4f9d01d0874e3fc979c7948cf088f675c02db332},
raw SHA256
\texttt{d33235c7a81202f1b062975115da73e0d2b937260dd896c3d72c286bc1d776a2})
is available as a conversation attachment; persistent Library upload
failed and the full raw data are not asserted to be on GitHub.

\paragraph{LSPV-3, ADAPTIVE-SELECTOR-GATE255 (30 October 2026, 10:43 PDT; opaque-policy theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only within an explicit opaque finite-policy interface:} if $m$ certified silent methods are indistinguishable through their first $B-1$ charged steps, determining whether any answers by $B$ needs $mB$ candidate steps in the all-silent worst case. A source-reading or code-inspecting SAT solver may bypass this interface, so this is neither general SAT hardness nor an arbitrary-algorithm lower bound. \textbf{KNOWN bounded restart scheduling, charged restricted upper bound:} geometric $1,2,4,\ldots$ time slices find a certified method finishing in $t$ steps using fewer than $4mt$ candidate steps plus generator, checking, replay, and declared space-cap overhead. The complete DPLL fallback guarantees both SAT and UNSAT correctness but not polynomial worst-case time or FLMV sublinear space. \textbf{CONDITIONAL, previously present in CONTRACT1:} under the still-unconstructed arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ ARC recursion with selector multiplier $N^\eta$, the formal fixed time is $6/5+2\eta$; at actual FLMV $c=241/200$, $d=11642479/23232400$, strict interior needs $\eta<1/400$. This arithmetic does not supply a compiler. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the 11-method source-verified portfolio was slower in selected work than UP-DPLL on W1 UNSAT (65,725 vs. 11,555), SWAP6 SAT (32,577 vs. 5,925) and signed-renamed easy strict22 orbit (13,374 vs. 1,606), but beat UP-DPLL on a canonical source-known easy exact22 orbit (556 vs. 1,897). Same-source YES/NO, Horn, Krom, affine, source S/U and complement checks passed independent finite verification; no CDCL or native FLMV bit-space comparison was run. Forward/reverse memo counts and M22 parameters are not mandatory resources for arbitrary solvers. The all-input hereditary source-authenticated ARC successor and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \OPEN. \subsection*{LSPV-3 exact acquisition countercontrol: TSEITIN-SELECTOR-GATE255 (2026-10-30 11:29)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted canonical-source theorem, independent review pending.}
For every connected simple cubic graph with $v$ vertices and an arbitrary vector of charge bits $b$, the exact four-clause-per-vertex 3CNF Tseitin source has SAT iff $\bigoplus_t b_t=0$; every proper subset of complete charge blocks is SAT; the same condition equals even $N_0(0^e)$ \emph{only after an exact source XOR-grammar verification}. A deterministic exact decider for this canonical fixed-graph promise must inspect one charge-dependent source bit in every one of its $v=\Omega(N/\log N)$ source blocks on every input, by a one-charge-flip indistinguishability adversary. The input-read bound is tight and does not force high workspace, arbitrary SAT runtime or mandatory per-child re-reading: a shared one-bit parity summary suffices for repeated decision queries. Under the expressly \emph{no-sharing} architecture in which each of $N^{3/5}$ child operations must separately decide complete global parity, accumulated parent source probes are $N^{8/5-o(1)}$, outside the proposed FLMV $c=241/200$ box. That is a \emph{restricted architecture falsifier}, not an ARC obstruction for arbitrary children. The family is signed exact-$(4+,4-)$, not strict22.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} one uniform charged Krom/Horn/dual-Horn/XOR-certified/fallback DPLL controller passed both SAT and UNSAT cases, 10,872 proper-subset rank tests, 64 exhaustively enumerated small charge vectors, 384 single-charge toggle controls, 32 polarity-and-variable renamings, and 240 random mixed-CNF truth-table controls. On odd-charge six-vertex source, 400 selected controller events versus 3,137 ordinary DPLL events; no CDCL or FLMV-machine measurement is claimed. Original signed22 W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT both have $N_0(0^{15})=1$, \textbf{falsifying} unguarded endpoint parity; their source does not match the cubic XOR grammar and falls back. Source-query acquisition, recognition storage and witness reconstruction are charged; this implementation has $O(N)$ writable indexing and does \emph{not} achieve the proposed ARC space bound.
\textbf{OPEN:} hereditary all-input arbitrary-$A$ SAT-to-ordinary-SAT power-shrinking compiler with fully charged source access and simultaneous FLMV time/space. Full role report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255.tex}; checked compact source: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255_check.py}; summary data: \path{results/raw/clause_extremes/2026-10-30_1129_LSPV-3_TSEITIN_SELECTOR_GATE255_summary.json}.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 SAT-ONLY-SELECTOR-FIREWALL256 (30 October 2026, 12:24 PDT; independent review pending).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, elementary self-reducibility.}
A uniform polynomial-time total selector that, on every ordinary SAT
residual, chooses at least one consistent original-variable assignment
preserving SAT whenever the source is SAT (with no selection promise on
UNSAT inputs) exists exactly if SAT is in P. Iterating the selector and
checking the complete original-source assignment gives a total both-label
polynomial decider; conversely a polynomial SAT decider can test the
zero cofactor before choosing the safe side. This does \emph{not}
supply a selector or show P=NP.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted architecture, review pending.}
A purported arbitrary-$A$ ARC stage that invokes a correct guest
polynomial SAT decider $A$ on a direct cofactor of physical size
$\Theta(N)$ cannot uniformly meet
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\}$ for all polynomial guest
exponents $K$ when $\lambda<1$: legal DELAY wrappers of the
guest incur $\Theta(N^K)$ on this full-parent call. This rules out
that particular parent-sized guest policy, not arbitrary adaptive
source-aware choices. The strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ direct
one-pin cofactor retains at least $m-4$ untouched source clauses,
as already proved by LSPV-2 COFACTOR-VOLUME-ARC252.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent exhaustive checks of all 256
proper signed three-variable clause subsets validate the safe-selector
logic; 15 SAT cases invalidate blind first-zero selection. Authentic
strict22 W1 (UNSAT) and SWAP6 (SAT, 54 models), signed renamings,
Horn, Krom, XOR, and original-clause $S/U$ extremal controls were
tested. Same-engine selected work on W1 was 15,451 standard DPLL
versus 14,343 charged guest-selector events; on SWAP6 it was 7,518
versus 15,575, with full source access and guest outputs paid.
These are not native FLMV time--space measurements.
The complete four-page PDF, source, experiment implementation and
raw data are persistently stored in Library
\texttt{libfile\_a5b1e65258988191b2c78f2fb370609f}.

\[
 K'\le\max\{\alpha_0,\gamma+\lambda+\delta_s,\gamma+\delta_e+\lambda K\},
 \qquad L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda L\}.
\]
With parameters $(\alpha_0,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, formal fixed point is

The guest-step margin was known in CONTRACT1; the additive tariff is a new explicit accounting distinction, not an ARC compiler or arbitrary-machine lower bound. If scans reread the full parent or guest bit access repeatedly pays parent-scale work, the small-child premise fails. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 58 source-authenticated SAT/UNSAT controls (52/6), including known heterogeneous Horn/XOR one-variable backdoors, strict22 W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT, signed variants, finite GF(2)/Horn/Krom and exhaustive random 3CNF; an independent checker validated original source clause extremes, witnesses and exact rational FLMV tail. \paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-SIGNED-OVERLAP-CHILD-GATE258 (2026-10-30).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted direct-cofactor original-source accounting; independent review pending.}
For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, a coherent partial assignment of $t$ variables has exactly $2t$ assigned-TRUE source occurrences. Writing $s_j$ for their multiplicity in original clause $C_j$, $D=\#\{j:s_j>0\}$ for deleted clauses, and $\Omega=\sum_j(s_j-1)_+$ for repeated coverage, one has the exact state-dependent identity
$D=2t-\Omega$.  A further pin $x=b$ changes the deleted-clause count by $2-h_x(b;\rho)$, where $h_x$ counts the already deleted source clauses among the two satisfying occurrences of $x=b$; both updates use authenticated original occurrences and must charge acquisition.  Combining the previously verified LSPV-4 signed22 $D\le2t$ bound with the previously verified LSPV-1 explicit prefix-cylinder Kraft theorem gives a physically serialized direct-cofactor child-size requirement and corresponding source-certified NO-cube mass bound. This is \emph{not} a lower bound on arbitrary SAT algorithms, semantic compilers, or cached DAGs.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, exact-source finite controls.}
On the $15$-variable W1 UNSAT/SWAP6 SAT signed22 twins (20 clauses, 349-bit declared source encoding, 18-bit child cap, 33-guest cap), a source-conflict-aware degree policy produced a complete W1 refutation with $413$ original-clause NO cubes and no guest, and produced $16$ verified short ordinary SAT residual children for SWAP6, with $412$ source-NO cubes. Their fully charged \emph{instrumented} controller totals were 81,850 and 86,368 selected events versus UP-first DPLL 15,193 and 7,633. The alternate first-variable SWAP6 policy yielded $36>33$ children. A family of easy connected strict22 formulas with a polynomial-time source-known witness remains a hostile counterexample to treating large direct-cofactor guest counts as SAT hardness. Independent finite checker passed 184,300 checks; original $S/U$ occurrence targets, source-indexed four-mode pair matrices, coherent endpoints and partial flips remain adversarial controls. Full raw data and checker: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1424_LSPV-3_ARC_SIGNED_OVERLAP_CHILD_GATE258_summary.json}; complete archive in persistent Library \path{/P-vs-NP/2026-10-30_1424_LSPV-3_ARC_SIGNED_OVERLAP_CHILD_GATE258_COMPLETE.zip}.
\textbf{OPEN:} The uniform hereditary both-label arbitrary-decider $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$, genuine power-short ordinary SAT child construction on \emph{all} inputs, charged policy discovery, source/random-access guest simulation and simultaneous FLMV time-space contraction. No $P=NP$ or $P\ne NP$ conclusion follows.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 FRONTIER-TWO-SWEEP259 (2026-10-30).}
\textbf{KNOWN DP SPECIALIZATION / EXACT source-proof, independent review pending.}
For a paid original-clause order $\pi$, the cut boundary
$B_i=\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(\leq i)})
\cap\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(>i)})$ and local union
$U_i=B_{i-1}\cup\operatorname{Var}(C_{\pi(i)})$
support exact forward and reverse Boolean satisfiability messages.
Joining those messages with the actual signed original $C_{\pi(i)}$
determines in one bidirectional computation every globally supported
source-clause variable pattern and every backbone literal.
Unrealizable patterns give source-authenticated forbidden partial cubes,
including on satisfiable formulas.  Work is
$O(N+D_\pi+\sum_i2^{|U_i|}\operatorname{poly}(|U_i|+\log N))$,
where clause-order discovery, scanning, proof replay, and source access
are charged; retaining all tables has generally
$\Omega(\sum_i2^{|B_i|})$ states.  This specializes the established
project--join/full-interface DP method, not a novel all-input SAT algorithm.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL / negative uniform-cost gate.}
An independently replayed 27-formula experiment contained 22 SAT and
five UNSAT cases; all 25 implemented DP runs matched exhaustive model
enumeration, with 17,856 independently replayed table rows.  On the
15-variable strict22 SWAP6 SAT twin with 54 models, two sweeps certify
$x_2=x_{15}=1$ and 50 nontrivial original-clause-scope forbidden
patterns; 46 survive original UP and have satisfiable residual binary-only
2SAT.  In particular the SAT-satisfied cube
$(x_2,x_8,x_9)=(0,0,0)$ is globally forbidden, proving
$F\models(x_2\vee x_8\vee x_9)$, but its acquisition is not free.
Measured Python median milliseconds with policy selection and proof
checking were 122.962 adaptive versus 5.919 baseline for SWAP6,
and 113.405 versus 13.561 for W1.  The easy source-known signed22
orbit exhibits width 13,15,17 with only 21,27,33 baseline DPLL
nodes, falsifying width as mandatory arbitrary-solver hardness.
Two dense hostile controls triggered paid greedy discovery and DPLL
fallback.  Original clause $S/U$ targets, their inversions, indexed
SS/SU/US/UU modes, coherent endpoints and complement/flip trails
remain adversarial checks only.

the persistent Library as

\paragraph{LSPV-3 source-frontier recursive replay (RECURSIVE-FRONTIER-REPLAY260).}
\textbf{KNOWN METHOD SPECIALIZATION, exact restricted proof, independent review pending.}
For the source-authenticated original-clause order, let $B_i$ be the prefix/suffix shared variables, $U_i=B_{i-1}\cup{\rm Var}(C_i)$, and $w=\max_i|U_i|$.
The previously established exact Boolean forward/reverse frontier-support identities admit a Hirschberg-style divide-and-conquer replay: on interval $(a,b]$, compute global midpoint prefix and suffix messages and recurse on both sides. At each singleton join the original clause with its actual global boundary messages; this emits exactly all $H_i(u)$, so unsupported original-source local patterns are formally forbidden partial cubes for SAT and UNSAT instances alike. A \emph{streaming} implementation retains $O(2^w(w+\log N)\log(m+1)+S_B+\log^2(N+2))$ simultaneous writable bits while paying $O(N+\log(m+1)\sum_i[d_{i-1}+d_i+2^{|U_i|}{\rm poly}(|U_i|+k+\log N)]+mk2^k)$ indexed-word time; here $d_i$ and $S_B$ explicitly charge source-boundary discovery and access, and every streamed output symbol is billed. Retaining all output in RAM removes the low-space claim. The recognizer for the numeric source-band grammar $|v-i|\le b$ charges the initial source scan and $O(b^2k)$ source-occurrence work per boundary, but low numeric band is not invariant under variable renaming and not arbitrary hardness.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, adverse fully charged same-source controls.}
Independent full-table/global-mask replay and small exhaustive source-oracle checks agree on 23 original formulas and 9,958 checked records, with two corrupted outputs rejected. W1 strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ is UNSAT and SWAP6 is SAT with 54 models; SWAP6 has 70 globally unsupported local patterns of 160. Band128 SAT/UNSAT recursive replay uses 1,917,887/1,915,434 selected host operations versus full DP 839,166/837,844 and UP-first DPLL 61,057/17,566. W1/SWAP6 replay uses 1,604,689/1,602,849 versus DPLL 19,962/9,548. Band128 SAT replay has a conservative 111 live-table-entry envelope versus 2,115 full retained entries, but W1's loose replay envelope is \emph{worse} than retaining its tables. Original clause-indexed signed $S/U$ targets, inversions, extreme compatibility and coherent complement flips were checked as adverse controls, not used as semantic oracle decisions. These instrumented events are not native FLMV machine costs. The full report, executable producer and independent verifier, raw output tables and hashes are archived in persistent Library \path{/P-vs-NP/2026-10-30_1624_LSPV-3_RECURSIVE_FRONTIER_REPLAY260_COMPLETE.zip}; the compact public LaTeX proof record is \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1624_LSPV-3_RECURSIVE_FRONTIER_REPLAY260_PUBLIC.tex}.
\textbf{OPEN:} this is not an ordinary SAT child of power-short physical length, and an all-input hereditary arbitrary-$A$ ARC contractor with fully charged source/output/guest access and simultaneous FLMV space remains unconstructed; both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-BRANCH-MASS-GATE261 (30 October 2026, 17:35 PDT; restricted theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-preserving cofactor-tree accounting.}
For strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ source $F$ on $n$ variables with $m=4n/3$ original clauses, a partial assignment fixing $t$ variables eliminates exactly $D=2t-\Omega$ clauses, where $\Omega=\sum_j\max(0,s_j-1)$ and $s_j$ counts fixed TRUE literals in the original $C_j$. In a complete binary source-authenticated cofactor partition with rejected cubes $P$, fully satisfied leaves $S$ and exact residual children $E$ containing at most $r$ original clauses, $t_*=\lceil(m-r)/2\rceil$ and
\[
\sum_{P}2^{-|\rho|}+\sum_{S}2^{-|\rho|}
+\sum_{E}2^{-|\rho|}=1,\qquad
\sum_{P}2^{-|\rho|}+\sum_{S}2^{-|\rho|}\geq1-|E|2^{-t_*}.
\]
Every inferred unit is paired with its \emph{source-justified} contradictory binary sibling, rather than charged as a free disappearance of assignment mass. This is a combination of the classical binary-tree Kraft equality and earlier signed-overlap accounting, not a novel SAT lower bound. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} same-source W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT (0 and 54 models) satisfy the independently replayed coverage; at the 8-original-clause cutoff, max-occurrence selection gives respectively 3 and 11 physical residual children, but their encodings remain 86--117 bits versus the proposed 19-bit child limit. The SAT orbit21 signed22 family has an explicit witness despite local nonshrinkage. All source scans, policy tests, failed rules, proof checks, guest DPLL calls, physical child writes and live records are charged in the declared instrumented word-event model; this is not native FLMV work/space. Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261.tex}; code and verifiers: \path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261.py} and associated \texttt{verify} scripts; summary: \path{results/summaries/arc/2026-10-30_1735_LSPV-3_ARC_BRANCH_MASS_GATE261_summary.json}. The hereditary arbitrary-$A$ SAT successor and simultaneous FLMV contraction remain \OPEN; $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 RUP-CORE-REUSE262 (30 October 2026, 18:24 PDT; restricted source-authenticated theorem, independent review pending).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted fixed-CNF proof invariant:} If unit propagation under a current branch-decision set $D$ conflicts relative to the original source and earlier RUP-validated learned clauses, the assumption-negation clause
$L_D=\bigvee_{\ell\in D}\neg\ell$ is a globally implied, RUP-checkable source consequence, safe to retain after backtracking.  A deletion-based UP check can produce an inclusion-minimal contradictory assumption set; its acquisition, unsuccessful deletions and replay are charged.  This is classical clause learning, not a new SAT method.  A per-leaf naive indexed-word bound is $O((d+2)nL)$ for depth $d$ and current total literal count $L$; the number of leaves remains potentially exponential.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} An independently replayed 60-formula both-label control (50 SAT, 10 UNSAT) checked 1,952 learned RUP clauses, 3,574 conflict leaves and 7,829 proof-tree nodes.  A specially ordered but easily recognized Krom UNSAT family at $k=9$ decreased from 2,047 baseline DPLL nodes and 622,845 selected operations to 21 nodes and 28,107 operations under full assumption-core minimization; ordinary 2SAT SCC instead decides the same easy family in only 356 selected events.  W1 strict22 UNSAT decreased from 143 to 45 nodes but cost rose from 20,186 to 68,010 events; SWAP6 strict22 SAT decreased from 78 to 32 nodes while cost rose from 10,483 to 37,555 events.  These are complete source-checking word-level instrumentation and Python memory observations, \emph{not} native FLMV machine complexity or industrial CDCL comparisons.  Original signed clause $S/U$ extremes, flips and polarity renamings remain adversarial controls.  More compact learned certificates do not by themselves create smaller ordinary SAT children or a uniform adaptive policy.

\textbf{ARC bridge OPEN.}  The result permits source-authenticated RUP cache reuse across backtracking in a fixed formula but supplies neither a hereditary, both-label, arbitrary-$A$ SAT-to-shorter-SAT contractor nor the same-machine $(241/200,11642479/23232400)$ forbidden FLMV pair. \paragraph{LSPV-3 ARC-PARITY-MOMENT-CAMOUFLAGE264 (2026-10-30 20:24 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted known parity/linear-algebra specialization, independent mathematical review pending.} For a connected cubic graph on even $q$ vertices, four original proper 3CNF clauses per vertex encode signed XOR charges on $n=3q/2$ edge variables. Changing the overall charge parity flips SAT to UNSAT while keeping the unsigned source-support hypergraph identical and preserving every raw and factorial moment of the globally coherent false-original-clause count $N_0$ through degree $q-1$; the $q$th factorial moments differ by $q!/2^{q-1}$. The SAT source has $2^{n-q+1}$ models; the UNSAT source has none. This is the familiar affine parity syndrome property specialized into an explicit arbitrary-order falsifier for a \emph{moment-only} SAT policy; it is not a lower bound on arbitrary algorithms. Source-authenticated XOR recognition and paid Gaussian elimination decide both labels. As a strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ adverse pair, the W1 UNSAT and SWAP6 SAT twins have identical unsigned clause supports and equal first two $N_0$ moments but different cubic moments and 0 versus 54 models.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} The independent checker exhaustively replayed 664624 complete assignments over 16 original sources (8 SAT, 8 UNSAT), 23136 source-indexed $SS/SU/US/UU$ extreme modes, 1038 signed occurrences, 83095 complement checks, 72 authenticated parity blocks and four source-derived odd-charge UNSAT XOR proofs, rejecting three corrupt records. For prism $q=6,8,10,12$, UP-DPLL SAT/UNSAT selected events were 448/2865, 736/9065, 1095/26113, 1525/70825; a paid XOR-aware controller used 220/137, 286/177, 352/217, 418/257. These are explicit Python work proxies, not native FLMV bit-machine transitions. Complete source $S_j/U_j$ target inversions, coherent endpoints and Gray flips were retained as adversarial controls; both endpoints fail on all eight parity formulas. The controller creates no ordinary power-short child, and its all-input fallback is not polynomially bounded.
\textbf{ARC OPEN.} The only load-bearing arrow remains a uniform hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ controller with exact both-label source-relative cover and physically smaller ordinary SAT children, satisfying simultaneous native-resource bounds $K'\le\max\{11/10,3/5+K/2\}$, $L'\le\max\{1/2,L/2\}$ on the actual FLMV machine. Low-order moment camouflage does not establish hardness of SAT; the earlier affine MCSP theorem remains restricted. P versus NP remains OPEN. Compact report and independent checker:

\paragraph{LSPV-3 CUT-ACQUISITION266 (30 October 2026, 22:24 PDT).}

not a lower bound for arbitrary SAT algorithms or native TM

$\bigoplus_{e\in\delta S}x_e=\bigoplus_{v\in S}b_v$
certifies forbidden partial cubes on the cut edges; it is a

\noindent\textbf{LSPV-3, NONMONOTONE-GUARDED-CACHE267 (30 October 2026,
23:24 PDT; KNOWN THEORY and PROVED/EXACT restricted, external review
pending; COMPUTATIONAL both-label controls).}
For a fixed original-source CNF, a source-indexed unit-propagation trace
under decision cube $D$ supplies transitive decision-support guard
$G(\ell)$ and source-reason provenance $P(\ell)$ for every propagated
literal. The source entails
$\bigvee_{d\in G(\ell)}\neg d\vee\ell$ and, on conflict, the
corresponding guarded blocker. Both are source-RUP and therefore
remain valid after arbitrary backtracking to any coherent cube containing
their precise guard, even if the old and new decision cubes are incomparable.
The proof may survive source deletions only with its authentic reason
clauses still present or with an independently verified replacement
proof. Unconditional cache carryover is unsound. This is a classical
unit-resolution/learned-clause consequence, not a new SAT lower bound.

Fifteen complete original sources (10 SAT, 5 UNSAT), including W1/SWAP6
strict signed $(2+,2-)$, signed renamings, Horn/Krom, XOR and random
3CNF controls, were tested and independently checked with 16,177
finite assertions, three deliberate corruptions rejected. The controller
mined 328 original-source RUP clauses and found 476 sound incomparable
cross-branch reuse opportunities. It reduced DPLL search nodes
$220\to176$ but \emph{increased fully selected Python operations}
$68{,}500\to382{,}597$, paying policy/source scanning, failed probes,
proof replay, verification and retained caches. Clause-indexed local
S/U targets, inversions, pair compatibility, coherent endpoints and
flip trails remain adverse tests, not general classification oracles.
These finite events/diagnostic bit footprints are not native FLMV indexed
random-access Turing machine resources. There are no power-short
ordinary SAT children and no arbitrary-guest ARC contraction.

\noindent\textbf{LSPV-3 HORN-GUARD-SET-COVER-GATE268 (31 October 2026, 00:24 PDT; PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL both-label checks).}
For an explicitly source-indexed definite Horn CNF $H$ of width at most three,
binary set-selection implications $(\neg d_i\lor e_u)$, and a ternary
conjunction chain for the target $t$, all positive selector assumptions $D$
satisfy $H\wedge D\models t$. Finding a minimum subset $G\subseteq D$
sufficient for $H\wedge G\models t$ is NP-complete by an exact
SET COVER equivalence: $G$ entails $t$ precisely when its selected sets
cover the universe. This is a \emph{restricted source-authenticated optimization
problem}, not hardness of Horn SAT or a mandatory cost for an arbitrary
SAT algorithm; it overlaps the earlier LSPV-3 SOURCE-COVER-OPTGAP146
theme but uses a distinct assumption-support / Horn UP grammar. Horn
closure itself is polynomial and does not require computing a minimum
support. A six-element control has optimal guard size two but a greedy
guard of size three. The comparison invalidates free \emph{optimal}
certificate selection without implying that every adequate selector
needs optimization.

An independent verifier checked $18{,}492$ assertions,
$230{,}400$ exhaustive complete assignments, $131{,}232$ additional
control assignments and rejected three deliberate corruptions.
Four paired source-Horn SAT/UNSAT systems and ten original-source
adversarial controls include W1 strict signed-$(2+,2-)$ UNSAT,
SWAP6 SAT, signed renamings, Krom, Horn, and XOR. Indexed local S/U
occurrence targets, inversions, SS/SU/US/UU pair modes, globally coherent
zero/one endpoints, complement and Gray-code flip trails were retained.
Selected host-language operation proxies for eight paired Horn systems
were 10,187 UP-DPLL versus 5,575 certified-stage operations, before
the separately recorded expensive minimum-core search. These are
\emph{not} native FLMV indexed-machine time or simultaneous writable
space measurements. The stage selector uses a complete original-source
UP-DPLL fallback and a real SCC/GF(2) detector. No arbitrary guest
$A$, physically power-short ordinary SAT child, or hereditary resource
contraction was constructed.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 SOURCE-DELTA-REVALIDATION-GATE270 (31 October 2026, 01:48 PDT; restricted exact theorem pending external review).}

post-edit \emph{validation of that particular stale witness} requires $m$
indexed clause inspections even if at most one clause is replaced by its
all-negative counterpart. Both source labels remain SAT: this is a
\emph{certificate-validation query lower bound}, emphatically not a SAT
decision or arbitrary-algorithm lower bound. An authenticated edit log
permits changed-clause checks only after its source discovery, update and

through source versions if every proof-used indexed signed reason remains
unchanged and the new assumption cube extends the old one. A changed
proof-used reason requires revalidation, not automatic semantic failure.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 STRICT22-BACKDOOR-SELECTOR-GATE271 (31 October 2026, 02:19 PDT; restricted exact theorem, external referee pending).}
For a proper original CNF, a static \emph{strong syntactic} Krom,
Horn, or dual-Horn backdoor $B$ is characterized, respectively, by
$|B\cap\operatorname{var}(C)|\ge |C|-2$,
$|B\cap\operatorname{pos}(C)|\ge |\operatorname{pos}(C)|-1$,
or its negative-literal analogue for every original clause $C$.
For proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$-3CNF, each of the three minimum
backdoor sizes is at least $\lceil n/3\rceil$. On the previously
constructed connected orbit family with $n=3k$ (odd $k\ge7$), their
\emph{exact} sizes are $(k,2k,2k)$, even though a fixed canonical
original-source grammar is checkable in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$
time, $O(\log N)$ auxiliary space and yields a verified SAT witness.
The signed-renamed orbit remains polynomial-time recognizable by the
prior bounded-degree graph-isomorphism result, with no small-space claim.
Thus large static strong-backdoor size is \emph{not} a lower bound on
arbitrary adaptive SAT solvers or on depth backdoors.

An architecture explicitly emitting every static cofactor needs at least
$2^{n/3}$ children, whereas retaining a literal four-mode original
SS/SU/US/UU record for every pair costs $2m(m-1)$ simultaneous bits.
Both are \emph{architecture-conditional} barriers; source-local streaming,
inference caching, different policy acquisition, and early witnesses
remain permitted and must be charged. A nineteen-source independent
finite verifier checked 16,908 clause-pair modes, 978 signed occurrence
extremes, 132,660 coherent exhaustive assignments, 83,781 smaller
backdoor subsets, 83 UNSAT proof leaves, and three rejected corruptions.
Selected Python event proxies: W1 UNSAT $10{,}940\to11{,}241$,
SWAP6 SAT $3{,}699\to4{,}060$, canonical orbit $k=7$
$2{,}150\to504$, affine prism UNSAT $5{,}695\to298$.
The exact-backdoor MILP was used only off-policy; it is \emph{not}
a free controller selector. The full original-clause diagnostics are
offline controls, not native FLMV machine resources. The full raw,
producer, verifier, negative tests, sources and report were checked
separately. No power-short ordinary SAT children, all-input
hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contraction, or matched FLMV
time-space violation is obtained. P versus NP remains \textbf{OPEN}.
Source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-31_0219_LSPV-3_STRICT22_BACKDOOR_SELECTOR_GATE271.tex}.

\medskip
\textbf{INTERFACE-SOURCE-SELECTOR-GATE272 (PROVED/EXACT restricted oracle theorem; external mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL checks).}
For a verified two-module SAT split $F=G(B,X)\wedge H(B,Y)$,
the complete signed interface $B$ must be glued coherently:
$\operatorname{SAT}(F)$ iff $P_G\cap P_H\ne\varnothing$.
When $P_G=\{0,1\}^{b}$, a deterministic \emph{membership-only}
selector distinguishing $P_H=\varnothing$ from an unknown singleton
must make $2^b$ membership probes on the empty relation;
a randomized selector of worst-case error $\epsilon<1/2$
requires expected at least $(1-2\epsilon)2^b$ such probes
on the empty case. This is a restricted interface-oracle tariff,
not a SAT runtime lower bound. The earlier
SPARSE-BOUNDARY-SUPPORT83 and ARC-MEMBERSHIP-JOIN162
already established source-typed interface joins; no novelty
is claimed for their exactness.
A source-authenticated proper-3CNF PIN grammar decides both SAT
and UNSAT by scanning original clauses and producing, respectively,
a verified full witness or an original-source Resolution refutation.
The source-visible bypass falsifies any claim that the membership
tariff is mandatory for arbitrary CNF solvers.
The independent finite verification covered 25 original formulas
(13 SAT, 12 UNSAT), 4,751 source-indexed SS/SU/US/UU pair
modes, 232 globally coherent Gray/complement states, 49
Resolution proof steps and four rejected certificate corruptions;
W1/SWAP6, signed renamings, Horn, Krom and affine GF(2) were
hostile controls. At $b=5$, baseline UP-first DPLL visited
2,607 SAT and 4,095 UNSAT nodes; the authenticated restricted
controller used 595 and 569 selected Python operation events,
respectively, with unlike costs and no native FLMV conclusion.
No power-short ordinary SAT child, universal arbitrary-$A$
$\Phi(A)$ hereditary contractor, or same-machine simultaneous
FLMV forbidden-region implication was obtained.
P versus NP remains \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 IMPLICIT-LOGSPACE-SELECTOR-GATE279 (31 October 2026, 09:24 PDT; KNOWN theorem transfer, PROVED/EXACT restricted pending external review; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls).}
Reingold's undirected connectivity in deterministic logarithmic space
provides an \emph{implicit source-graph} articulation recognizer and
source-scanning arbitrary-cofactor-bit emitter in $O(\log N)$ writable
space and unspecified polynomial time for any indexed proper CNF.
The fully source-authenticated one-variable separator identity preserves
both SAT and UNSAT labels with separately solved ordinary CNF cofactors.
Thus the parent-scale explicit primal graph allocated in GATE278 is
\emph{not necessary} for this particular policy, including under variable
renaming. This does not eliminate the polynomial source-query tariff or
produce an all-input balanced separator. An unbalanced easy SAT control
has a 207-bit child from a 230-bit source; a no-articulation easy Horn
control has an immediate witness.
The independent original-source experiment passed 4,706 checks on
17 formulas (10 SAT, 7 UNSAT), including signed S/U targets,
all four clause-pair extreme modes, coherent complements and flips,
and independently decided every cofactor. The scan-based reference
selector used 3,259,362 source-clause scans, with an additional
1,983,634 source-clause scans for 220 sampled virtual bit probes;
baseline DPLL visited 93 nodes and the cofactor DPLL 149 nodes
(measures of different units, not an equal-cost ratio).
The Python BFS reference uses $O(n)$ auxiliary graph space and
does \emph{not} implement Reingold or establish a native FLMV bound.
The entire universal hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$
contraction remains \textbf{OPEN}; P versus NP remains \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 M22-BIPARTITE-FACTOR-GATE280 (31 October 2026, 11:24 PDT; PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL same-source controls).}
The corrected scientific target is \emph{every valid input} of D\"ocker's
NP-complete duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-$(2,2)$ language,
with malformed strings rejected; prior broader signed exact22 work is
retained as hostile controls, not conflated with the monotone target.
For every valid source, its positive and negative clause incidences
form two loopless cubic multigraphs with the \emph{same} variable-labelled
edges. A full assignment satisfies the source if and only if its TRUE
edges cover the positive clause vertices and its complementary FALSE
edges cover the negative clause vertices, preserving both labels
pointwise. On bipartite positive incidence graphs, three disjoint
perfect matchings can be acquired by standard polynomial matching.
If at most two negative original clauses have three pairwise
nonincident positive edges, at least one entire factor assignment
satisfies every original clause. This is a sufficient SAT-terminal
policy, not a universal decision upper bound.
A valid nine-variable $K_{3,3}$ positive source with all six perfect
matching triples as negative clauses has 18 exhaustively checked
satisfying assignments but \emph{no} safe perfect matching; failed
factor selection cannot certify UNSAT.
The independent small-source audit tested seven valid sources, 2,688
complete assignments, 1,544 source-indexed S/U pair-extreme modes,
42 variable-renaming/clause-order/global-sign controls, malformed
encodings and three corrupted records. The original published
198-variable/264-clause monotone exact22 UNSAT source was
independently refuted using six original-source module cofactors
(3,666 exhaustive DPLL nodes) and all 64 boundary valuations,
without relying on a timeout or an imported label.
The complete JSON-language controller rejects malformed sources,
tries a charged matching policy, and otherwise uses uncapped
iterative UP-first DPLL, so both labels are decided at possibly
exponential cost. It retains parent-scale graph/source memory,
does not generate hereditary smaller target inputs, and supplies
no same-machine SAT-to-target length/index/space transport.
NP-completeness alone does not transfer the numerical FLMV SAT
forbidden region. The arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contraction,
a target-wide polynomial bound or superpolynomial lower bound,
and P versus NP all remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 GATE282: globally extendible danger, restricted SAT certificate (2026-10-31 13:24 PDT).}
On every valid duplicate-free proper monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) source, call a negative triple dangerous if its three positive cubic-dual edge labels are pairwise vertex-disjoint. A dangerous triple is \emph{extendible} iff deletion of its six endpoints leaves a perfect matching. Let $q_{\rm ext}$ count extendible dangerous original clauses. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, external review pending:} if the positive cubic dual is bridgeless and $q_{\rm ext}\le2$, one polynomially discovered perfect matching avoids a chosen edge from each extendible danger by Edmonds' uniform $1/3$ edge-marginal decomposition, yielding a complete original-clause-verified SAT witness. Sign reversal gives the negative-dual counterpart. Excluding nonextendible triples sharpens prior GATE281 $q\le2$ and the independent LSPV-1 H22-DANGEROUS-HITSET282 $\tau\le2$ theorem. A valid 12-variable cube source has $q=3$, pairwise-disjoint dangerous triples $\tau=3$, but $q_{\rm ext}=2$; its TRUE set $\{1,7,8,9\}$ satisfies all 16 source clauses (268 models, exhaustively checked). The exact matching-witness decision cost is at most $q+3^{q_{\rm ext}}$ general perfect-matching calls, not an arbitrary-SAT complexity lower bound. An independent checker tested 15 valid sources including a source-refuted 198-variable UNSAT control, 143336 original clause-pair compatibility modes, and 10 malformed strings. Full source graph and repeated matching calls are charged; 4 matching calls on the strict example exceeded an eight-node tiny DPLL baseline in overhead. No hereditary in-target ordinary SAT children, low-space FLMV transfer, or arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contraction follows. \textbf{P versus NP OPEN.} Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-31_1324_LSPV-3_EXTENDIBLE_DANGER_SELECTOR_GATE282.tex}; compact reproducer \path{experiments/arc/lspv3_282/strict282.py}; independently checked archive preserved with a documented full-data Library container-session failure.

\paragraph{LSPV-3 GATE283 M22-XOR-ORDER-INVARIANCE (31 October 2026, 14:24 PDT; PROVED/EXACT restricted logical-state identity, independent mathematical referee pending).}
For every proper clausewise-monotone width-three source, on \emph{every} one-pass $0^n\to1^n$ variable-flip ordering, the complete source-indexed aligned assignment, two-bit true-literal count, and one-bit SAT-indicator vector has cumulative adjacent logical XOR $D_{\rm bin}=n+5m$ and endpoint XOR $E_{\rm bin}=n+3m$ using ordinary binary count encoding; reflected Gray count encoding gives $D_{\rm gray}=n+4m$, $E_{\rm gray}=n+2m$. This \emph{logical} order-invariance is not a physical machine lower bound, and discovery/head movement costs differ substantially. The legal two-occurrence same-polarity source-switch U198 UNSAT/V198 SAT preserves every count-vector observation on the equality subcube $x_{196}=x_{197}$, while independent source-derived UNSAT certification and SAT witness checking establish opposite original labels; Y6 SAT has exactly 20 models. Separately implemented verification checked 277,840 clause-pair compatibility cells, all signed S/U targets, malformed input rejection, U198 proof and V198 witness. UP-first DPLL solved Y6 in four nodes and 115 literal tests; both U/V were UNRESOLVED at 1,200-node cap, without relabeling capped searches as proofs. Greedy immediate-XOR changes no one-pass cumulative delta yet requires 19,701 candidate evaluations, so policy acquisition must not be free. The previous LSPV-1 FLMV indexed-tape amortized recharge bound is distinct and properly credited. Full code, raw, independent checker, 6-page PDF, SHA-verified ZIP and persistent Library readback are preserved at \texttt{/P-vs-NP/LSPV-3/2026-10-31\_1424\_GATE283/GATE283\_COMPLETE.zip}; public GitHub \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-31_1424_LSPV-3_M22_XOR_ORDER_INVARIANCE_GATE283_PUBLIC.tex}. Universal hereditary target-language ARC, charged SAT-to-target FLMV transport and $P$ versus $NP$ remain \OPEN.


\paragraph{LSPV-3 GATE284 CONSISTENT-MATCHING-RADIUS (31 October 2026, 15:24 PDT; elementary exact theorem, independent mathematical review pending).}
For every CNF source, let $\rho_F(a)$ be the minimum number of original source variables required to hit all original clauses false at complete anchor $a$. Every SAT witness $b$ satisfies $\rho_F(a)\le d_H(a,b)$, and any single coordinate flip obeys $|\rho_F(a)-\rho_F(a\oplus e_i)|\le1$, because any newly false source clause contains the flipped variable. This makes $\rho$ an admissible and consistent one-bit-move lower bound but does not imply polynomial global decision. On canonical duplicate-free proper monotone exact-(2,2), its polynomial computation as $\rho=|U|-\nu(G_U)$ by maximum matching is \emph{KNOWN} from Gallai and previously proved in LSPV-3 GATE244; no priority claim is made. For Hamming ball radius $R$ and partial prefix with $h$ changed reference variables, $h+\rho_{\rm free}>R$ is a sound certificate that no SAT witness in that \emph{ball} completes the prefix, not an UNSAT proof. Finite independent verification checked 4,736 complete small-source assignments, 24 radius-constrained decisions, 706 independent brute-force cover minima, 5,976 one-bit consistency edges, 72,704 complement source rows, and 18 malformed inputs; the prior GATE283 independent U198 UNSAT refutation (3,666 nodes) and V198 SAT witness were rerun. U198 UNSAT and V198 SAT have exactly the same endpoint values $|U|=132$, $\nu=64$, $\rho=68$. On Y6 at radius two, matching saves 4 search nodes but raises counted source probes from 258 to 291 and makes 9 matching calls; its total-work advantage is not established. Offline complete logical XOR and fixed-mask proxy XOR are \emph{not} native FLMV work/index tape $d_{\rm phys}$. The existing GATE244 ordinary UP-dominance theorem remains intact. Compact GitHub report \path{research/agents/lspv-3/2026-10-31_1524_LSPV-3_CONSISTENT_MATCHING_RADIUS_GATE284_PUBLIC.tex}, compact executable \path{experiments/arc/lspv3_284/gate284_compact.py}, and summary \path{results/raw/arc/lspv3_284/summary284.json} have verified source publication; full ZIP is retained locally with documented Library upload failure. No hereditary target child, fully charged arbitrary-decider contraction or numerical SAT-to-target FLMV transport is obtained. $P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-PAYLOAD-JUMP-TRADEOFF283 (31 October 2026, 15:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted indexed-access policy; COMPUTATIONAL independently
replayed same-target SAT/UNSAT controls; CONDITIONAL FLMV transport; P versus NP OPEN.}
For a fixed FLMV indexed-input machine which performs a \emph{separate binary absolute indexed
jump for each requested source bit} at address $a_j$, the jump atomically erases its
input-index tape, but the source index head does not automatically reset.
If the tape begins blank and $q$ is the fixed direct zero-to-one index edits per
step, then end-to-end index recharge requires
\[
 W_{\rm index}\ge\sum_j\operatorname{popcount}(a_j),\qquad
 T\ge q^{-1}\sum_j\operatorname{popcount}(a_j).
\]
This uses the known native index-erasure accounting of ARC282 and ARC283
but measures the \emph{requested absolute address} for each independently charged jump.
For all $P$ contiguous payload bits after $H=O(\log P)$ header bits, the
sum is $\Theta(P\log P)$ for \emph{this policy only}. A sequential source
scan is $O(P)$ and does not incur the indexed address-writing cost, so no
unrestricted SAT time lower bound follows.
At separated physical write/jump/read checkpoints, physical index-cell
XOR totals twice the address one-bit writes, but a blank-to-blank
checkpoint enclosing a whole completed read has zero endpoint physical
content XOR despite real index writes, head movements, erasures and reads.
The original residual logical state is unchanged by read-only probing;
the source-coverage mask is an OFFLINE diagnostic, not a free internal oracle.

For canonical proper unique monotone 3-SAT-(2,2), original Y6 SAT,
U198 certified UNSAT and V198 witness-certified SAT, the independent verifier
passed $3{,}647{,}073$ assertions across original $S/U$ clause extrema,
pair compatibility, the full 64-boundary UNSAT certificate, and complete
fixed-location input index-tape traces.
Y6 has $P=80$, 254 index one-bit writes, 508 adjacent physical XOR changes
and 1184 abstract native operations; U198 and V198 each have $P=6600$,
40850 writes, 81700 adjacent XOR changes and 196390 operations.
All runs' physical tape-content endpoint XOR is zero. This run's V198
uses the specific first-literal swap at zero-based clauses 1 and 7,
\emph{not} the earlier 2/44 V198 variant.
Matching 1200-node UP-DPLL remained UNKNOWN on both U/V; independent
original certificates establish their labels.
These are indexed event trace counts, \emph{not} end-to-end native FLMV SAT times.

The exact Theorem~5.7 envelope remains
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, uniquely maximized at $k=2$
with strict $k\ge4$ tail exclusion.
The universal, all-valid-target, malformed-rejecting hereditary
$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contraction and numerical same-machine
SAT-to-monotone-target FLMV transfer remain \textbf{OPEN}.
This restricted policy lower bound must never be substituted for either.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-XOR-INDEX-RESET281 (31 October 2026, 13:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted native machine accounting; COMPUTATIONAL source-backed controls; CONDITIONAL FLMV transport; P versus NP OPEN.}
The complete physical tape Hamming inequality $T\ge \sum_j d_{\rm phys,j}/k$
holds for fixed-map \emph{ordinary one-bit-write tape} checkpoints when each
transition changes at most $k$ such bits. It is \emph{not valid for the
full indexed FLMV work/index-tape configuration}: FLMV Section~2.1 permits
one indexed jump to erase \emph{all} live cells of the corresponding
index tape in one native step. With $q$ ordinary write ports, $q_W$
non-index ordinary work ports, and $E$ index cells atomically erased
over disjoint checkpoint intervals, the exact model-matched bounds are
\[
 D_{\rm all}\le qT+E,\qquad
 T\ge \max\{0,(D_{\rm all}-E)/q\},\qquad
 D_{\rm ordinary\ work}\le q_W T.
\]
A 16-one indexed-input address produces $d_{\rm phys}=16$ in one
jump on a fixed-two-tape machine; index preparation writes and head
movement remain fully charged. LSPV-5's earlier \emph{ordinary fixed
verbatim work buffer} lower bound is unaffected. This is an
operation-model correction, not an arbitrary-SAT algorithm lower bound.

For every valid D\"ocker monotone 3-SAT-$(2,2)$ source and every variable,
the fixed \emph{logical} source-indexed state consisting of assignment
bits, two-bit clause truth counts and per-clause satisfied flags has
exact Hamming distance seven from either coherent extreme after a single
flip. A lazy physical assignment bit may change once, with independently
charged reads required to reconstruct semantic counts. Matched canonical
Y6 SAT, U198 source-certified UNSAT and V198 witness-certified SAT
controls passed 161,465 independent checks; all six closed binary/Gray
tours have endpoint XOR zero but positive cumulative changes. The
published condensed report and code are
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-31_1336_LSPV-4_ARC_XOR_INDEX_RESET281_PUBLIC.tex}
and \path{experiments/arc/lspv4_281/arc_xor_reset281_compact.py};
readback-verified numerical summary is
\path{results/summaries/arc/2026-10-31_1336_LSPV4_ARC_XOR_INDEX_RESET281_summary.json}.
Full raw/independent checker remain in the separately supplied
reproducibility ZIP because Library container-path upload failed.
Exact FLMV boundary $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$ are unchanged.
The all-valid-target hereditary $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ compiler and

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-TARGET-SHANNON-ROUNDING280 (31 October 2026, 12:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted quota and full-Shannon barrier;
COMPUTATIONAL independently audited original SAT/UNSAT sources;
CONDITIONAL FLMV target transfer; P versus NP OPEN.}
The target is D\"ocker's valid duplicate-free proper monotone
3-SAT-$(2,2)$, not an all-positive subfamily. Each valid nonempty target
has $3\mid n$, $n\ge6$, and $m=4n/3$ clauses. A valid re-encoding of
an $r$-pivot cofactor that preserves all $n-r$ unassigned source
variables as distinct active target variables must satisfy
\[
n'\ge\max\{6,3\lceil(n-r)/3\rceil\},\qquad
m'\ge4\max\{2,\lceil(n-r)/3\rceil\}.
\]
In particular, for $r=1,2$ there is no clause-count shrink; under
declared canonical explicit DIMACS, there is no physical bit-length shrink.
If all $2^r$ Shannon branches are separately re-encoded without proof
pruning or aggregation, a polynomial child-count bound forces
$r=O(\log N)$, hence every identity-preserving target child has
physical length $(1-o(1))N$. This excludes simultaneous
$q\le N^{3/5+o(1)}$ and $M\le N^{1/2+o(1)}$
\emph{only for that exhaustive, survivor-preserving policy}.
It is not a lower bound against arbitrary SAT algorithms,
variable-eliminating compilers, or certified adaptive pruning.

The original-source controls were valid Y6 SAT (20 models),
D\"ocker U198 UNSAT (independent six-block source proof with a checked
89-node local refutation), and V198 SAT (checked 198-bit witness).
Original indexed S/U targets, inversions, compatible-pair modes,
coherent endpoint/complement statuses, intermediate flips, cofactor
profiles, and 89,096 independent raw checks passed. The 1200-node
matched UP-DPLL budget was UNRESOLVED for U/V but not used to infer
their labels. Exact FLMV Theorem~5.7 audits retain
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, with unique $k=2$ and
the complete $k\ge4$ tail strictly excluded. NP-completeness alone
does not transfer the numerical SAT boundary to the target.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-TARGET-COFACTOR-TRANSFER279 (31 October 2026, 11:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted cofactor nonclosure; KNOWN NP-completeness
of D\"ocker's monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ proper duplicate-free 3-SAT;
COMPUTATIONAL matched originals; CONDITIONAL FLMV transfer; OPEN ARC.}
The revised scientific domain is \emph{all valid} inputs of this one
established NP-complete language, rejecting malformed strings, not all
ordinary SAT formulas. The larger signed strict-$(2^+,2^-)$ language is
a comparison superset. On every connected nonempty valid target formula,
any nontrivial raw cofactor has an assigned/unassigned boundary clause
that is either deleted (losing an unfixed variable's signed occurrence)
or shortened (violating required ternary width). Thus raw hereditary
target membership fails.
Each one-variable cofactor retains $m-2$ clauses ($m-4$ ternaries and
two binaries) and $3m-8$ literal occurrences; its SAT/UNSAT OR/AND
cofactor semantics remains exact. Sign-monochromatic clauses cannot
share both equal and opposite literal polarity, so the old
four-incompatible-extremes pair case is absent in the target language.

The original D\"ocker Appendix C connected $U_{198,264}$ is UNSAT by
an independently source-checked 89-node DPLL refutation of its exact
32-private-variable module, six block-normalizations and complete
64-row contradiction of the terminal core. Two positive-occurrence
swaps yield connected valid SAT $V_{198,264}$ with a checked 198-bit
witness and the same 29,392-bit physical length. Three valid target
sources and original-clause S/U controls were recorded, with malformed
strings rejected. Standard UP-DPLL without the specialized source
certificate was UNRESOLVED on both large inputs under declared
three-second budgets.

The actual FLMV SAT forbidden envelope remains
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, unique maximizing $k=2$
with exact infinite-tail exclusion. The \emph{conditional}, fully
charged same-machine SAT-to-target reduction with length exponent $a$,
prepass exponents $(r,s)$, indexed target-bit cost exponent $\beta$ and
target guest exponents $(K,L)$ gives source exponents
$\max\{r,aK+\beta\}$ and $\max\{s,aL\}$ only with a proved
total both-label virtual input simulator. At the unconstructed
target ARC point $(6/5,1/2)$, strict FLMV transfer at $c=241/200$
requires $r<c$, $s<d_*(c)$,
$6a/5+\beta<c$ and $a/2<d_*(c)$.
Even $\beta=0$ requires
$a<11642479/11616200\approx1.0022623$.
NP-completeness does not supply this numerical transfer.
The source-authenticated, hereditary, target-preserving arbitrary-$A$
contraction or a direct applicable target lower bound remains
\textbf{OPEN}. P versus NP remains OPEN.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-BOUNDARY-JOIN-STREAM-INDEX278 (31 October 2026, 10:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent mathematical review pending;
COMPUTATIONAL same-original-source controls; KNOWN FLMV; OPEN universal ARC.}
The new canonical proper-3CNF family $F_{S,T}$ has two independently
satisfiable modules sharing a $b$-variable interface, with boundary
projections exactly $S,T\subseteq\{0,1\}^b$.  Its full original source
has $2\cdot 2^b(b+3)$ proper ternary clauses and fixed-width physical
length $N=\Theta(2^b b^2)$, matched for all SAT and UNSAT variants.
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(F_{S,T})\iff S\cap T\ne\varnothing,\qquad
 \operatorname{UNSAT}(F_{S,T})\iff S\cap T=\varnothing.
\]
A deterministic one-way projection-stream decider with all $m=2^b$
first-vector bits preceding the second needs at least $m$ persistent
bits, by the exact singleton-suffix distinguishability argument.
\emph{This is not an indexed-input FLMV workspace lower bound}:
an authenticated fixed-width read-only source permits a charged
$O(\log N)$-writable-space, $N\operatorname{polylog}N$-time direct
recognizer using indexed sign reads and no retained $m$-bit vector.
At the UNSAT $S=\{0\}$, $T=\{1,\dots,m-1\}$ source, each of the $m-1$
absent first-module pins must be source-bit probed by a deterministic
exact indexed source reader, as toggling any one flips to SAT, but this
proves only a restricted \emph{input-read} lower bound.

Six matched original-indexed 3CNFs (four $b=3$, two $b=4$; three
SAT and three UNSAT) were generated; independent verifier passed
$80{,}805$ checks including every original signed occurrence
$S/U$ extreme/inversion, $SS/SU/US/UU$ matrix, complete assignments,
flip trails, and original-source resolution provenance.  Source-verified
pin-preprocessed UP--DPLL nodes were $3,7,7,7,85,15$ on the six
sources, with $48$ or $96$ original resolution preprocessing steps
charged separately.  This is not a native FLMV-machine or industrial
CDCL benchmark.  No power-short child was generated.

The independently verified full FLMV Theorem~5.7 envelope retains
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, unique $k=2$ with
the entire $k\ge4$ tail excluded by exact $c^{-4}$ inequalities.

\[
 O\!\left(M+S_A(M)+u\log^2(M+T+2)+R+\log(M+T+2)\right)

bits, including all materialized child, guest and retained controller

LSPV-5's TOTAL-VIRTUAL-INPUT226.  A fully charged native machine

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-FAR-BLANK-GUARD274 (31 October 2026, 06:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, external mathematical review pending;}
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} finite same-source original-clause and guest trace controls;
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} actual FLMV; \textbf{OPEN} universal ARC.
The previously verified BOUNDED-GUEST-INPUT273 host simulation required both
the guest input-index \emph{head extent} $h=O(\log M)$ and an extra
$O(M)$ guest input-head \emph{coordinate} restriction. The latter
restriction is unnecessary for one faithfully simulated guest run on
a genuine $M$-character ordinary-SAT child held on a \emph{counted}
indexed host work tape. With guest time $T=T_A(M)$ and guest work/index
space $S_A(M)$, a charged binary virtual input coordinate,
guest-index-tape erasure with preserved index-head position, an exact
blank guard, and all child-buffer index writes give
\[
 T_{\rm host}=O\bigl(M+T[h+\log(M+T+2)+1]\bigr),\qquad
 S_{\rm host}=O\bigl(M+S_A(M)+h+\log(M+T+2)\bigr).
\]
Thus $h=O(\log M)$ and polynomial $T$ suffice for
$O(M+T\log M)$ time and $O(M+S_A+\log M)$ space,
even when the guest reads a blank at address $\Omega(M^2)$.
This does \emph{not} handle unrestricted polynomial-time guests:
a correctness-preserving guest may walk its input-index head
$\Omega(M)$ cells, as already demonstrated by BOUNDED273.
EPOCH-INDEX-LIFT265 earlier used a far-blank concept; this
reconciles and strengthens the precise bounded-index contract rather
than asserting a new unrestricted capability.

A complete physical reset can add $S_A(M_i)$ time per guest call

For even $m\ge4$ and hidden marker $h\in[m]$, the proper original seven-variable one-hot 3CNF $F_{m,h}$ has 16 witnesses if $h$ is even and a source-indexed nine-step resolution refutation if $h$ is odd. This corrects the declared byte offset without retiring the scoped cold-source indistinguishability lemma.

When the original source or an authenticated update log is replayable,

\textbf{AFFINE-FULLPORT-LEDGER217 (PROVED/EXACT restricted algebra;
COMPUTATIONAL audit; mathematical referee review pending).}
For an authenticated affine equation system, eliminate discarded variables
over $\mathbf F_2$ and retain the \emph{joint} affine projected relation
on the $w$ surviving variables, represented by at most $w$ independent
XOR equations.  An explicit proper-3CNF parity-chain compiler has
$O(w^2\log(w+2))$ output bits, including auxiliary variables, complete
DIMACS headers, and all clauses.  Within this already tractable grammar,
choosing $w=\lfloor n^{1/4}\rfloor$ yields
$N_{\rm child}=O(N^{1/2}\log N)\le N^{3/4}$ for sufficiently large
canonical parents.  Physical coefficient tests show that attempting a
second direct power-short contraction on a small child can fail; the
uniform restricted decider uses a separately charged finite-size
base/fallback instead of asserting a nonexistent size inequality.
The independent reproducer passed 110,276 assertions (320 source
cases, 4,686 exact projection and 4,686 compiled fixed-boundary checks).
The source Library static-boundary audit was rerun byte-for-byte.
Singleton boundary marginals \emph{do not} preserve correlation:
$\{00,11\}$ and $\{01,10\}$ have identical individual supports.
Pure affine projection cannot capture an arbitrary 3-clause (seven
satisfying assignments), and mixed 2CNF-plus-XOR is NP-complete; no
unrestricted semantic contraction follows.  A deliberately conservative
fixed-$k$ implementation has $T_k=O(N^5)$ and $S_k=O(N^2)$,
while its source-inclusive one-tape simulator has
$T_1=O(N^7)$ and $P_1=O(N^2)$, without any RAM or FLMV
lower-bound transfer.  The next target is exact full-correlation
interface discovery for \emph{arbitrary} signed SAT at charged
polynomial total cost.  Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN.
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_0147_LSPV-4_AFFINE_FULLPORT_LEDGER217.tex}.

\textbf{MIXED-AFFINE-PORT-FIREWALL218
(PROVED/EXACT restricted; independently rerun).}
For $r$ recognized Boolean guards, first-true case expansion yields at most

$(7/4)^r$ affine pieces in that representation. This is an
architecture-specific representation lower bound: the same function has a
linear CNF description and is trivially satisfiable. Hence it neither proves
general SAT hardness nor supplies arbitrary-instance affine decomposition.
The 2,587-check audit passes; unrestricted acquisition remains \OPEN.

\textbf{CONTEXT-PORT-ACQUISITION-FIREWALL219
(PROVED/EXACT under restricted proper-3CNF hypotheses; independently rerun;
marker-safe owner retry pending).}
For arbitrary proper 3CNF $F$, the connected proper always-satisfiable guarded
source
\[
G_F=\bigwedge_i(\neg y\vee\ell_{i1}\vee u_i)
              (\neg u_i\vee\ell_{i2}\vee\ell_{i3})
\]

decision-only compiler may output a fixed YES base. The guard is not strict

\textbf{MIXED-XOR-2SAT-FANO-BARRIER221
(PROVED/EXACT finite counterexample; restricted FPT positive control;

A seven-variable Fano instance has satisfiable affine and binary subsystems,

\textbf{PROJECTED-EXCESS-SCC222 (PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
For an explicitly encoded system $F=E\land B$ with affine GF$(2)$
equations $E$ and signed width-at-most-two clauses $B$, parse and
Gaussian-eliminate the affine-incidence components, including singletons.
If $x_{C_i}=a_i+L_i z_i$, let $P_i$ contain \emph{all} $B$-mentioned
variables in $C_i$, including internal binary clauses, and define
$r_i=\operatorname{rank}(\pi_{P_i}L_i)$ and
$e_i=\max(r_i-1,0)$. Partition the graph of binary interactions between
affine components into connected clusters $D_j$ and put
$K_j=\sum_{i\in D_j}e_i$. A uniform source-authenticated solver
branches on $r_i-1$ exposed coordinates in each component and leaves
at most one Boolean quotient bit per component. Every residual $B$
is then 2CNF, so paid SCC decisions and witness reconstruction give
$\operatorname{poly}(N)(1+\sum_j 2^{K_j})$ time and polynomial
peak workspace. This strictly improves the \emph{bound for this
explicit algorithm} over branching on full affine component nullity,
and complements the LSPV-3 projected-rank/treewidth table bounds.
A dense complete-interaction family with $q$ components and
$h=q^2$ private pairs per component has old excess $q^3$,
exposed excess zero, and a directly materialized ordinary 2CNF child
of physical length $O(N^{2/3}\operatorname{polylog}N)
\le N^{3/4}$ eventually, with a closed restricted-base decision.
The same family is not strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF, and the
construction does not contract arbitrary SAT. An internal-clause
counterexample shows that ignoring binary constraints inside affine
components is unsound. Conservatively, for
$R=1+\sum_j2^{K_j}$, fixed two-tape sequential-input
construction time is $O(RN^6)$ with $O(N^2)$ workspace;
one-tape simulation gives $O(RN^8)$ time and $O(N^2)$
source-inclusive footprint, with no exact fixed-UTM exponent claimed.
The independent Python audit passed 6,659 assertions; the
original connected-interface audit reproduced byte-for-byte.
Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ follows. LSPV-1/LSPV-5
handoff was published and verified.
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_0607_LSPV-4_PROJECTED_EXCESS_SCC222.tex}.

\medskip
\textbf{FOUR-PIN-ONLINE-CONTEXT226 (PROVED/EXACT restricted online
information theorem; independently refereed).}
For connected proper 3CNFs $F_{t,s}$, four pinned variables define queries
$Q_i$ with $\operatorname{SAT}(F_{t,s}\land Q_i)=s_i$.  A deterministic
source-free state answering a later adversarial $Q_i$ therefore needs at least
$t$ bits on some source.  If the source remains readable, a scan uses only
logarithmic auxiliary space; writable-cell consequences require fixed heads,
finite alphabet and polynomial time.  All unaugmented sources are SAT, so this
is not a decision-only contraction or runtime lower bound.
