\subsection{LSPV-4 HYBRID-BAD-MESSAGE228: exact exceptional-union interfaces (October 29, 2026)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on an authenticated restricted module-tree grammar, independent referee pending; unrestricted SAT OPEN.}
Extending LSPV-1 ARC229's exact bijunctive-message closure, at each node $t$ retain non-bijunctive child relations as full explicit separator tables, absorb each exactly certified 2CNF-definable child message into the local binary clauses, and enumerate only
\[
 E_t=S_t\cup\bigcup_{\text{non-bijunctive child }u}S_u,\qquad h_t=|E_t|,
\]
where $S_t$ is the \emph{parent-edge separator}, not the complete full shared-variable interface.
For polynomially authenticated running-intersection trees with once-only source constraint ownership and local affine coefficient rank $e_t$, deterministic exact bottom-up SAT/UNSAT decision and SAT witness reconstruction use conservatively
\[
 D(N)+\operatorname{poly}(N)\sum_t\left[
 2^{h_t}(N_t+1)^{O(e_t+1)}+2^{|S_t|}\operatorname{poly}(|S_t|+1)
 \right]
\]
indexed-RAM bit time, with all failed local routes and full tables charged. Uniformly fixed $e_t$, logarithmic $h_t$ and $|S_t|$, polynomial decomposition discovery and polynomial node count imply polynomial total computation on this \emph{restricted} verified class even when full interfaces are linear. A rank-one star of $q$ equality leaves and $h$ odd-parity leaves has full centre interface $2q+3h$ but exceptional union $3h$; its SAT value is the centre target parity $\beta=h\bmod2$. A two-odd-parity-child overlap has sum of separator sizes six but exceptional union three. When $h$ grows, this particular controller has a potentially exponential branch-mass bound; no algorithm-independent hardness follows. The original fixed-$e$ local algorithm is \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (Arvind--Guruswami IPEC 2021); no new $e=2$ claim is made. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 259 randomized small-tree differential controls, 24 SAT/UNSAT stars, four source-validation failures correctly rejected, 278 exhaustive arity-at-most-three relation recognitions, and overlap controls passed. The local reproducibility bundle contains the extended Python source and raw results; a compact independent JavaScript check and raw results are on GitHub. The four-pin online lower bound remains source-free and contextual, not decision-only SAT hardness. No ARC/FLMV forbidden-region transition or strict exact-$(2,2)$ universal decomposition coverage has been proved.
Report: \texttt{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29\_1336\_LSPV-4\_HYBRID\_BAD\_MESSAGE228.tex}.
Next: prove uniform polynomial discovery and strict22 coverage with bounded $e_t$, $|S_t|=O(\log N)$ and $h_t=O(\log N)$, or exhibit a restricted structural counterfamily.

\paragraph{LSPV-4, HEAD-WINDOW-INFO-VALUE236 (29 October 2026, 18:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independently reviewed here.}
For the declared one-read-only-sequential-head source model, a source-independent
prepared head position, \(L\) complete retained noninput bits and \(t\)
independent sign fields spaced by \(g\), the exact optimum worst future
singleton-context head movement is
\[
 H_{\min}=\max\{0,\lceil g(t-L-1)/2\rceil\}.
\]
The proof injects each source into retained state plus the radius-\(H\) source
window; storing \(L\) signs and centering the head on the remainder attains the
bound.  A source-dependent initial head position must have its address charged.
Indexed RAM access and writable-source re-encoding escape the theorem, so it is
not a total SAT-time or RAM lower bound.  Report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_1836_LSPV-4_HEAD_WINDOW_INFO_VALUE236.tex}.


\paragraph{LSPV-4, BATCH-SWEEP-VALUE237 (29 October 2026, 19:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; independent referee pending.}
For a public read-only serial source with $t$ independent sign bits at spacing $g$,
one fixed source-independent prepared head position, and a complete $L$-bit
persistent state, a deterministic non-resetting \emph{batch} continuation
reconstructing every four-pin INDEX-style SAT-context answer has exact worst-case
post-head-travel frontier
$H_{\mathrm{post}}^\star=g\max(0,t-L-1)$.
The same fully charged prefix strategy attains this bound while every exact
preparation-plus-continuation execution has total input-head movement at least
$g(t-1)$, also attained. This batch theorem is distinct from the radius-half
single-query bound in HEAD-WINDOW236 and does not transfer to unrestricted
SAT decision time, source-rewriting machines, or indexed RAM.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} A claim that reading one bit decreases
$\Phi_W(K)=\log_2|\pi_W K|$ by at most one \emph{pointwise}.
The Horn 2CNF $\bigwedge_{j=2}^{t}(x_1\lor\neg x_j)$ has
$2^{t-1}+1$ models; the rare observed $(x_1=0)\mid_{v}$ lowers
logarithmic support by $\log_2(2^{t-1}+1)>t-1$, whereas expected
improvement under the uniform model prior is binary entropy $\le1$.
These are contextual knowledge scores, not general lower bounds on steps.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 3068 exhaustive prefix-trajectory checks,
48 frontier rows, and 12 Horn-skew rows.
\textbf{OPEN:} immediate-response adaptive sequence frontier with cache/write
costs, general SAT, and P versus NP.

\textbf{ONEWAY-RANK-WINDOW238 (PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-present
single-query interface; independent referee pending).}
Let $a\in\mathbb F_2^t$ be encoded as independent signs spaced $g$
physical source cells; let a future selected parity query belong to a
public family $W$.  Before the query, an arbitrary deterministic preprocessor
leaves a complete $L$-bit source-dependent state and a source-independent
initial head position.  With one unchanged read-only source head moving
only right by at most $H$, put $r=\min(t,1+\lfloor H/g\rfloor)$.
The exact minimum summary length, including nonlinear preparations, is
\[
 L^*_W(H)=\min_{0\le u\le t-r}
 \operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb F_2}
 (W_{[t]\setminus\{u,\ldots,u+r-1\}}).
\]
Necessity is the $2^{\operatorname{rank}}$-response count on source
vectors agreeing inside the reachable window; sufficiency stores a basis
of projected parity masks and performs a charged one-way source sweep.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} target answer rank alone determines
physical continuation cost.  Rank-one sparse and dense parity contexts
with $L=0$ require respectively $0$ and $g(t-1)$ head movements.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 150 seeded mask families, 54,856 exhaustive
source/query answers, 256 nonlinear one-bit summary maps, and writable
source-compaction controls.  Source retention, public query-description
length, Gaussian elimination, input-head repositioning, peak writable
state and construction time are charged. The theorem is not an
unrestricted SAT lower bound, does not transfer to RAM or writable
source, and leaves P versus NP \OPEN.
Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-29_2036_LSPV-4_ONEWAY_RANK_WINDOW238.tex}.

their naive summed weight is $r/8$.

guarantees marginal unit-propagation work savings.  Every derived

the source has explicit polynomial-time SAT witness $u_i=1,v_i=0$.
For any full assignment to strict22, $N_2-N_1=-3D$,

is $(E-|D|)/2$.  These are diagnostics, not an answer oracle.

seeded partial states, SAT/UNSAT controls, and same-run

\paragraph{LSPV-4 SPARSE-EXTREME-SLACK244 (30 October 2026, 01:40 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; general complexity OPEN.}
For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, exactly two same-polarity and four opposite-polarity source-occurrence pair events arise per original variable. Therefore only at most $6n$ original clause pairs have a nondefault local $SS,SU,US,UU$ extremal compatibility mode. A sorted sparse source-index table encodes the complete four-mode relation in $O(n\log m)$ bits and can be compiled in $O(N+n\log n)$ indexed word-RAM operations, in contrast to an \emph{optional} $4\binom m2$-bit explicit dense matrix. Actual connected strict22 $H_{200}$ has $n=1200,m=1600$, exactly 5200 nondefault pairs; dense-mode bitpacked payload 5,116,800 bits versus a conservative sparse record payload 124,800 bits. These are implementation-specific representation costs, not all-algorithm SAT resource lower bounds.

For coherent original-variable pinning $\rho$ fixing $k$ coordinates, at a nonconflicting original-source unit-propagation fixed point define $Z$ uncovered original clauses, $H_2,H_3$ original clauses with respectively exactly two and three pinned-TRUE occurrences, $w$ uncovered residual ternary clauses, and $e$ free occurrence count in already-satisfied clauses. The exact \emph{signed-occurrence slack} $d=m/2-H_2-H_3$ satisfies $\boxed{2d=w+e}$. Selecting one free variable per uncovered ternary clause yields a certified strong 2SAT backdoor of size $h\le w\le 2d$, with polynomial acquisition and charged $2^h$ leaf search, no universal polynomial bound. On the previously verified connected SAT family $H_r$, pinning the two original variables $x_{j,5}=x_{j,6}=0$ in every block gives a source-UP-saturated UNSAT partial cube with $d=4r$, $w=0$, $e=8r$. Nevertheless a non-tautological original-parent resolution DAG with at most five steps proves the width-at-most-four source clause
$R_j=x_{j,5}\lor x_{j,6}\lor x_{j-1,5}\lor x_{j-1,6}$; its falsification excludes this entire original-variable cube. Thus linear signed-occurrence slack does not force large proof or search work, even on connected strict22 SAT sources. This is an explicit restricted falsification of any such intrinsic-cost proposal.

Full original-source-indexed local SAT/UNSAT targets and inversions, globally coherent endpoints and flips, proof provenance, W1/S15/CUBE9/XOR/Horn controls, and simultaneous same-source baseline/prepass ledgers are reported in
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-30_0140_LSPV-4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_PUBLIC.tex},
with independent compact checker
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/LSPV4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_independent_check.js}
and readback-verified machine-readable results
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/LSPV4_SPARSE_EXTREME_SLACK244_public_results.json}.
Finite selected-operation comparisons did not demonstrate a net time or peak-space advantage, and proof assistant/referee review is pending. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\textbf{LSPV-4 NAE-ENERGY-TWIN246 (2026-10-30 02:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary NAE/complement-phase identity; COMPUTATIONAL connected strict22 SAT/UNSAT twin; independently checked, P versus NP OPEN.}
For every proper original 3CNF, \(E_F(a)=N_0(a)+N_3(a)\) counts original clauses violating the signed not-all-equal predicate. Writing \(s_i=2a_i-1\), it has the exact quadratic signed-pair form
\[
E_F(a)=m/4+(1/4)\sum_{i<k}W_{ik}s_i s_k,\qquad
W_{ik}=\sum_{j:\,i,k\in C_j}\sigma_{ij}\sigma_{kj}.
\]
Negating \emph{all three literals} in any selected original source clauses leaves this entire function unchanged for every coherent full assignment; \(E_F(a)\le1\) certifies that at least one of \(a,\bar a\) satisfies the original CNF, but \(E_F(a)\ge2\) gives no UNSAT conclusion.

A verified connected proper duplicate-free strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ UNSAT source W1 (\(n=15,m=20\)) and its SAT signing \(G\), obtained by inverting all literals in zero-indexed original clauses \(T=\{0,1,2,4,7,9,12,14,16,17\}\), retain identical original unsigned clause supports and two positive/two negative occurrences per variable. Within the selected clauses every original variable has one positive and one negative occurrence. Exhaustive coherent assignment checks show \(\#\mathrm{SAT}(W1)=0\), \(\#\mathrm{SAT}(G)=270\), with verified \(G\) witness mask 26; nevertheless \(E_{W1}(a)=E_G(a)\) for \emph{every} one of the \(32768\) assignments and \(\min_a E(a)=2\). Their aggregate four-mode pair histograms coincide, but the \emph{full original-clause indexed} SS/SU/US/UU matrices differ on \(26/190\) pairs, preventing any full-information indistinguishability claim. A source-parent resolvent from \(G\) original clauses \(C_0,C_2\) proves the forbidden global partial cube \(x_1=0,x_3=1,x_{10}=1,x_{13}=0\) by incompatible forced values of \(x_{14}\).

Same-input charged UP-DPLL versus endpoint-\(E\) prepass/fallback versus strictly \(E\)-decreasing pair-indexed walk/fallback takes selected events \(11782/11966/12239\) on W1 UNSAT and \(989/1173/1446\) on \(G\) SAT. The independent W1 76-leaf refutation replay adds 6303 checker events for every complete UNSAT method. Separate same-run Python peak-memory values and all fourteen hostile controls are in the complete raw artifact; selected events are not native machine instructions. This \emph{falsifies E-only SAT/UNSAT decision power} on connected strict22 sources but does not imply any all-algorithm SAT lower bound or P-versus-NP resolution.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 BUCKET-FLIP247 (30 October 2026, 03:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent mathematical referee pending.}
On proper original strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF, keep the original
signed occurrence index and the exact per-clause truth counts
$t_j\in\{0,1,2,3\}$.
The global-variable flip gain $g_i=R_i-B_i$ is the number of FALSE original
clauses repaired minus uniquely-TRUE clauses broken.
Each variable has four original source occurrences, hence
$g_i\in[-4,4]$, so nine intrusive gain buckets select a strictly
improving flip in constant worst-case indexed word-RAM time after
$O(N)$ source preprocessing.
Each flip updates four original clauses, at most 24 gain-contribution
arithmetic events and nine variable bucket entries.
Since each strict flip reduces $N_0$ by at least one, at most $m$ flips
occur, giving $O(N+m)$ \emph{prepass} work; failure returns
UNRESOLVED, never UNSAT.
The full explicit $m$-field truth vector at all $q+1$ assignments costs
$\Omega(m(q+1))$ trace writes, whereas a source-indexed initial vector
and at most four changed clause records per flip use $O(m+q)$ retained
records; re-expansion pays the output cost.
The theoretical constant-time step assumes an indexed word-array assignment;
Python's full arbitrary-precision integer masks are not constant-time native
operations, and the experimental ``delta'' objects retain masks for checks.
The optimal $O(m+q)$ trace omits repeated full masks, recording only the
initial assignment, flipped variable and changed source-clause counts.
No Turing/native time or space bound is inferred from the Python event ledger.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 13 indexed SAT/UNSAT controls,
1,744 independently checked pair extreme modes, 65,664 coherent
complement checks, 768 independent gain checks, and a 7-size product
scaling.  On $r$ independent signed paired-$K_4$ blocks, the
untraced strict-descent controller uses exactly $278r$ selected
source/bookkeeping actions for $2r$ successful flips, while an
expanded source-indexed truth-count log grows quadratically.
The matched $W_1$ strict22 UNSAT baseline/fallback work is
$16{,}238$ versus $16{,}876$ selected events,
with additional source-authenticated contradiction replay common to both.
On planted dense SAT the prepass plus fallback costs $1{,}296$ versus
$2{,}274$ for the declared baseline.
These are selected logical operations, not native/Turing instructions,
and no uniform speedup is proved.  Full source-indexed local $S_j/U_j$
targets and inversions, four-mode $SS,SU,US,UU$ matrices,
$0^n/1^n$, complements, multi-flips, resolvent forbidden cubes,
and proof replay are contained in the persistent reproducibility bundle.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 COMPLEMENT-GRAY248 (30 October 2026, 04:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted explicit-enumeration resource theorem,
independent mathematical referee pending; COMPUTATIONAL full 14-source
audit; P versus NP OPEN.}
For every original proper ternary clause $C_j$ and signed occurrence
$x_{i,j}$, the independently audited local all-literals-TRUE target
$S_j$ and all-literals-FALSE target $U_j$ invert one another; the
four original source-pair compatibility modes $SS,SU,US,UU$ retain
all shared variable identities.  The raw original-source data include
coherent $0^n,1^n$, intermediate one/multiple-variable Gray flips,
per-clause $t_j=0,1,2,3$, complementary four-bucket counts,
source-parent resolution proofs and forbidden partial cubes.
An original proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $n$
variables has exactly $2^{n-1}$ complete assignment/complement orbits.
Fixing $x_n=0$ and using the standard reflected $(n-1)$-bit Gray
sequence visits one representative from each orbit with precisely
$2^{n-1}-1$ coherent global flips, updating exactly four original
clause truth counters per step.  The full explicit UNSAT traversal
therefore has exactly $4(2^{n-1}-1)$ affected counter writes, with
$O(N+2^{n-1})$ indexed word-RAM selected work and $O(N+n+m)$
untraced words.  Among explicit one-bit paths visiting all orbits
and explicitly maintaining the per-clause counter array, this number
of flips and writes is minimal; it is \emph{not} a lower bound on
arbitrary SAT solvers, which may reason symbolically or prune.
The complete $m$-counter output vector on each representative
costs $\Omega(m2^{n-1})$ output fields even when the maintained
working state is small.  On authenticated strict22 W1 UNSAT
($n=15,m=20$), the quotient traversal visits 16,384 orbits,
uses 16,383 variable flips and 65,532 source-counter updates,
versus 131,068 counter updates on the full unpaired Gray path;
materializing all quotient vectors costs 327,680 fields.
Fourteen coherent source-controlled cases passed 5,988 original
pair-mode checks, 536 triple-mode checks, 336,688
assignment/complement validations and 92 independently replayed
UNSAT UP--DPLL contradiction leaves.  The same-source W1 baseline
DPLL used 151 nodes/15,431 selected solver events, while a fixed
eight-resolvent prepass plus DPLL used 81 nodes/15,170
\emph{fully charged} selected events (including source-mode
construction and resolvent acquisition); independent proof replay
added 11,283 and 8,494 events, respectively.  On H1 SAT the
same prepass reduced nodes 7 to 5 but worsened charged work
288 to 1,400; no uniform speedup is established.  Python
\texttt{tracemalloc} on W1 showed baseline 60,536 allocated peak
bytes, minimal quotient 13,916, quotient retaining the dense
source audit 151,266 and resolution retaining it 214,237.
These are Python allocation snapshots, not Turing-machine space
bounds.  This result complements earlier BUCKET-FLIP247 and
SPARSE-EXTREME-SLACK244 without claiming Gray codes or source
resolution as novel SAT methods.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 NEAR-FULL-TRUE-FPT249 (30 October 2026, 05:00 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted source-indexed FPT corollary; independent mathematical review pending.}
For every proper original signed 3CNF $F$, define $G^-(F)$ on ORIGINAL clause indices,
with edge $(j,h)$ when local source-target mode SS is incompatible
because one ORIGINAL global variable occurs in opposite polarity.
Every ordinary SAT assignment has its middle-state clause set
$M(a)=\{j:t_j(a)\in\{1,2\}\}$ as a vertex cover of $G^-(F)$.
Source-indexed branching on an uncovered opposite-sign edge,
forcing all nonexceptional original clauses into their all-literals-TRUE
local S mode, and enumerating at most $3k$ remaining original variables,
decides SAT with $|M(a)|\le k$ in $O(N^2+2^kN+k16^k)$
deterministic indexed word-RAM time and $O(N+m^2+k)$ streamed working words.
This is a known Vertex-Cover FPT construction applied to the
known Kullmann clause conflict graph, not unrestricted SAT.
A greedy matching of size $k+1$ gives a source-authenticated budget-NO
certificate; it never alone certifies ordinary UNSAT.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT sharp strict22 firewall:} for every SAT assignment
of a proper signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ ternary source,
$N_0=0$, $\sum_jt_j=3m/2$, and $N_2+2N_3=m/2$, hence
$\boxed{|M(a)|=m-N_3\ge3m/4}$.
Connected strict22 SAT $H_1$ attains equality ($m=8$, four models
with $N=(0,6,0,2)$).
Therefore $k=o(m)$ excludes even SAT witnesses on ALL growing strict22 SAT families,
and failed bounded-middle search is \textbf{NOT} an unrestricted UNSAT proof.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 14 SAT/UNSAT adverse controls;
4,128 original source-clause SS/SU/US/UU modes,
472 exact triple modes, 70,536 coherent assignment/complement checks,
438 signed original occurrences, 57 independently matched budget decisions,
108 source proof checks; all passed, including W1 strict22 UNSAT
and signed-neighbor SAT (168 models).
Selected total work for $k=2$ prepass plus identical UP--DPLL fallback
versus baseline: all-positive SAT $43$ vs $94$;
one-sign-conflict SAT $49$ vs $100$;
$H_1$ SAT $322$ vs $236$;
W1 UNSAT $12290$ vs $12075$;
signed-neighbor SAT $1885$ vs $1670$;
odd-charge $K_4$ Tseitin UNSAT $1196$ vs $952$.
W1 separate source-UP UNSAT proof replay costs $6206$
additional declared checker events on either complete path.
No across-family total-work advantage or unrestricted SAT conclusion.
The mandatory per-occurrence local S/U targets, both inversions,
four indexed pair modes, all coherent $0^n/1^n$ and multi-flip trails,
$N_0,\ldots,N_3$, non-tautological source resolutions, forbidden cubes,
full SAT witnesses and DPLL UNSAT proof leaves were independently checked.
The 343KB GitHub compact raw retains all indexed records;
the larger raw JSON, PDF and code archive are in the persistent Library.

\paragraph{LSPV-4: FLMV-CHILD-GATE251, 30 October 2026, 10:36 PDT.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (FLMV 2005):} The exact Theorem~5.7 forbidden envelope
is $d_*(c)=\max_{k\ge1}\min\{(1/f_k-c)/k,c^{-k}\}$,
where $f_0=1$ and $f_{k+1}=cf_k/(1+f_k)$.  The exact, infinite-tail-checked
value at $c=241/200$ is $d_*=11642479/23232400$ (attained at $k=2$);
$d_*-1/2=26279/23232400>0$.  The SAT time--space transfer
requires \emph{strictly} $K<c$ and $L<d_*$ on the \emph{same}
uniform deterministic indexed-input random-access Turing machine.
The proposed conditional fixed point $(6/5,1/2)$ is inside this forbidden
region, but no all-input transformer realizing it is known.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted physical-cofactor firewall (independent review
pending).} For a proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $n$ variables,
$m=4n/3$ original clauses and a declared fixed-slot record of
$B=\Theta(\log n)$ bits, pinning $t$ globally coherent variables
can delete at most $2t$ source clauses.  Thus an ordinary plain-cofactor
child has $N_\rho\ge N-2tB$ bits and obtaining $N_\rho\le N^\lambda$ for
fixed $\lambda<1$ by this mechanism requires
$t\ge m/2-O(N^\lambda/\log N)=\Omega(n)$.
This is a source-specific \emph{compiler} obstruction, not an arbitrary
SAT time or space lower bound.  The earlier short virtual child descriptor
still describes a full-scale ordinary SAT input unless its physical
encoded length actually shrinks.

\textbf{CONDITIONAL resource type-check:} With $N^\gamma$ serial ordinary
children of length $N^\lambda$, $N^\alpha$ charged source/discovery work,
$N^\delta$ \emph{additive} per-child setup, $N^\varepsilon$
\emph{multiplicative} per-guest-step accessor slowdown, and retained-cache
space exponent $\rho$, the justified abstract upper profile is
$K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\delta,\gamma+\varepsilon+\lambda K\}$ and
$L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda,\lambda L,\rho\}$, assuming all other sources
of work and live tapes satisfy the same accounting.  For
$(\alpha,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, a setup
$\delta=3/5$ preserves formal time fixed point $6/5$, whereas a
per-step exponent $\varepsilon$ raises it to $6/5+2\varepsilon$.
At $c=241/200$ it is necessary for this sufficient recurrence that
$\varepsilon<1/400$; equality is not enough.  Retaining
$N^{3/5}$ disjoint full-sized $N^{1/2}$ children simultaneously would
require $N^{11/10}$ writable bits and is outside the budget.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Nine original-source SAT/UNSAT hostile controls
were checked against coherent clause-indexed $S/U$ target inversions,
four pair modes, $0^n,1^n$, complements, exact partial cofactors
and independently replayed DPLL contradictions.
Matched strict22 W1 UNSAT and its signed-swap SAT control have
$n=15,m=20$, each with 426 encoded source bits and 386-bit
one-pinned-variable children; exact model counts are 0 and 168.
Fully charged selected operations did not improve against DPLL on
these matched sources (W1 11555 versus 12058, signed swap
1505 versus 9642).  These operations are not physical FLMV
machine instructions.  The AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2
finding remains a \emph{restricted source-independent affine} semantic
no-go, never an unrestricted SAT hardness statement.

\textbf{OPEN:} Uniform hereditary arbitrary-decider
$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ producing source-authenticated physically power-short
ordinary SAT children on \emph{all} YES/NO inputs while paying
policy discovery, failed attempts, source access, guest simulation,
re-entry, ancestor retention and peak simultaneous space.
$P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\textbf{ARC-SELECT-INDEX252 (PROVED/EXACT, restricted checkpoint
directory; independent review pending).}
For a virtual child assembled by retaining original source-clause slots
in source order, a single-level block-prefix directory on an arbitrary
retention mask with $m$ slots, block length $h$ and
$w=\lceil\log_2(m+1)\rceil$ requires
$S_{\rm idx}=\lceil m/h\rceil w$ stored bits and, in the worst case,
$Q\ge h-1$ read-only mask-bit probes to answer one exact
$\operatorname{select}$ query. Hence
$S_{\rm idx}(Q+1)\ge mw$. With source size
$N=\Theta(m\log m)$, this restricted architecture cannot satisfy
both $S_{\rm idx}=O(N^{s+o(1)})$ and
$Q=O(N^{\varepsilon+o(1)})$ when $s+\varepsilon<1$.
The current hypothetical FLMV/ARC budget $s=1/2$,
$\varepsilon<1/400$ fails \emph{for this index alone};
no bound on all succinct rank/select indexes or SAT algorithms follows.
A periodic retention mask has zero-probe arithmetic addressing and
falsifies such an overgeneralization.
The original FLMV Theorem 5.7 infinite tail was independently
rechecked with exact fractions: at $c=241/200$, optimal $k=2$
gives $d_*(c)=11642479/23232400$ and a strict
space margin above $1/2$ of $26279/23232400$.
Both-label finite controls preserve original W1 UNSAT (zero
models), signed-swapped strict22 SAT (168 models),
H1 SAT, Horn and XOR SAT/UNSAT; 126 original-source
checkpoint configurations and 987 independently replayed select
queries passed. No physical FLMV machine is implemented; the
Python host materializes bitmap controls and charges are not
native TM step or space measurements.
The still-missing arrow is a \emph{uniform, hereditary,
source-authenticated} arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ with genuinely
power-short ordinary SAT children, paid indexed output-bit
access, selector discovery, failed trials, both-label decisions
and peak writable ancestor space. $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain
\textbf{OPEN}. Report:

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} the actual Fortnow--Lipton--van Melkebeek--Viglas
Theorem 5.7 time--space envelope, with a certified infinite tail.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted algebraic consequence, independent referee
pending:} for every $6/5\le c\le c_0$, where
$c_0^3+c_0^2-c_0-2=0$ and $c_0\approx1.20556943040059$,
the full original FLMV envelope is
\[
d_*(c)=d_2(c)=\frac{2+c-c^3}{2c^2};
\]
all $k\ne2$ have $d_k(c)<1/2$ throughout this interval, and
$d_k(c)<1/2$ for all $k$ if $c>c_0$.
For a \emph{hypothetically constructed}, uniformly correct, all-input,
hereditary $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ satisfying the \emph{fully charged,
same-machine} recurrence
\[
K'\le\max\left\{\tfrac{11}{10}+\pi,\tfrac35+\delta+\tfrac K2\right\},
\quad
L'\le\max\left\{\tfrac12+\eta,\tfrac L2\right\},
\]
with $\pi,\delta,\eta\ge0$ for additive local work, repeated guest-step
slowdown and simultaneous counted scratch, respectively, its fixed point
lies \emph{strictly} in some actual FLMV forbidden region precisely when
\[
\eta<\tfrac1{90},\qquad
\delta<\frac{c_\eta-6/5}{2},\qquad
\pi<c_\eta-\tfrac{11}{10},\quad
c_\eta^3+(1+2\eta)c_\eta^2-c_\eta-2=0.
\]
\textbf{CONDITIONAL:} this is an algebraic budget, \emph{not} a constructed
compiler or a $P$ versus $NP$ proof.  At $c=241/200$,
$d_*(c)=11642479/23232400$ with optimal $k=2$, and simultaneous
boundary equality fails entry. A full $N$-bit original-source rescan
for each of $N^{3/5}$ children costs $\Omega(N^{8/5})$ and exceeds
this additive-stage budget.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-HEAD-SHADOW-GATE256 (30 October 2026, 14:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted abstract virtual-input head state, independent mathematical review pending:}
for a physical nonempty child ending in a nonblank input bit, the $r+1$
right-of-end input-head coordinates $m,m+1,\ldots,m+r$ are pairwise
distinguishable by at most $r$ ordinary left moves and one read. A post-jump
state-only abstraction that cannot reconstruct discarded addresses requires
$\lceil\log_2(r+1)\rceil$ bits for these coordinates; a saturated coordinate
with $O(\log(m+r))$ abstract bits is exact for all ordinary-move continuations
of length at most $r$ before a new indexed jump.
If the arbitrary guest's input-index tape uses at most $s$ binary positions
and it takes at most $T$ steps, the input-head coordinate has magnitude
at most $2^s+T+1$ and an exact binary shadow has
$O(s+\log T)$ bits. This closes only the \emph{abstract information-space}
accounting, with $s$ already charged to the guest's index tape;
native head-counter update time, arbitrary indexed jumps/erasure and all-tape
same-realization simulation remain \OPEN.
A naive scanner that rereads the entire $s$-bit address each guest ordinary
move can spend $\Theta(Ts)$ selected operations on a far-address alternating
walk while the original guest spends $O(T+s)$; this is a
\emph{restricted implementation falsifier}, not a machine-independent
simulation lower bound.
\textbf{KNOWN FLMV:}
exact rational Theorem~5.7 envelope and certified infinite tail
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, both optimal at $k=2$.
At $(c,d)=(241/200,11642479/23232400)$ the conditional
$(6/5,1/2)$ fixed point has \emph{strict} margins $1/200$ and
$26279/23232400$; a repeated guest-step multiplier needs
$\delta<1/400$ if the remaining hypothetical ARC resource coefficients hold.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
eight original signed-CNF SAT/UNSAT controls retain per-occurrence local
S/U extreme targets and inversions, original SS/SU/US/UU modes, true-literal
histograms, coherent endpoint/complement and multi-flip checks. Authentic
strict22 W1 UNSAT has $0/32768$ models, same-unsigned-support signed-swap
SAT $168/32768$. Main source-indexed checker passed $82{,}653$ assertions;
independent Python checker passed $132{,}796$ bounded-left/right-path and

head wrapper on W1 is $14826/15583$ (151 DPLL nodes unchanged), on W1' SAT
$1737/2494$ (19 nodes unchanged). Additional complete original-source

to either method; these selected-operation counts are \emph{not native

\paragraph{ARC-SOURCE-PROBE-TRANSCRIPT257: restricted inverse-index tariff (30 October 2026, 15:33 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted interface; mathematical referee pending).}
For an original read-only source permutation $\pi\in S_m$, a deterministic
preprocessor retaining $s$ source-dependent bits and an online stage answering
the fixed $t$ inverse-position queries $\pi^{-1}(1),\ldots,\pi^{-1}(t)$
using at most $Q$ adaptive full-word source probes obey
\[
  2^s m^Q\ge \frac{m!}{(m-t)!}.
\]
The exact observation-tree counting proof includes source-derived head
positions in the retained state. For $Q=0,t=m$, a complete inverse index
needs $\lceil\log_2(m!)\rceil=\Theta(m\log m)$ retained bits. Separately,
\emph{an implementation that rereads all $N$ original source bits for each
of $N^{3/5+o(1)}$ purported ARC children} uses
$\Omega(N^{8/5-o(1)})$ time, outside the hypothesized $N^{11/10}$
additive stage and the strict FLMV time slice $c=241/200$.
These are interface/algorithm restrictions, \emph{not} mandatory costs of
arbitrary SAT algorithms; a selective probe or structured accessor might
avoid either route. LSPV-1 already established the $Q=0$ count, and
the broader permutation-index literature is credited.

\par\noindent\textbf{LSPV-4 ARC-RESPONSE-WIDTH258 (30 October 2026, 16:36 PDT): exact restricted child-response-width firewall, independent mathematical review pending.}
A deterministic one-pass, label-only decoder of $q$ exact child SAT/UNSAT outcomes in a \emph{fixed} prescribed order requires at least
$\lceil\log_2 w_D(k)\rceil$ counted mutable bits at cut $k$, where
$w_D(k)$ is the number of different residual functions induced by the $k$-bit child-label prefixes.
This is the established ordered-BDD residual-function theorem, not new general SAT hardness.
Blocked equality on $q=2h$ independently variable labels has $w_D(h)=2^h$ and demands $h$ bits;
paired equality permits a two-bit stream, while AND/OR/XOR need one bit.
Consequently, storing or irrevocably decoding $N^{3/5}$ independent labels that require $\Omega(N^{3/5})$ state
cannot fit the actual simultaneous FLMV work/index space exponent
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400\approx0.501131$, assuming that restricted interface.
A different order, replay or source reread may avoid this particular obstruction but must be fully charged.
The source-faithful finite original-clause S/U controls, both-label DPLL proof checks and selected-operation ledger
are COMPUTATIONAL only and not native FLMV timings.
The exact FLMV full-envelope optimum is $k=2$ at $6/5$ and $241/200$, with certified $k\ge4$ tail.
The purported all-input arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$, physically power-short ordinary-SAT child,
fully paid decoder-acquisition/guest source/time/index/ancestor space and hereditary closure remain OPEN.
P versus NP remains OPEN. Report:

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-ASSIGNMENT-STATE-FIREWALL259 (30 October 2026, 17:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted internal state-query interface; mathematical referee pending.}
An exact stable ternary partial-assignment interface with $t$ distinct variable pins among
$n$ original variables, preserving arbitrary later variable-state queries without a free
history, must distinguish $\binom nt2^t$ states. For $t=\Theta(n)$ on a fixed
polynomial-time finite-tape FLMV machine, this demands $\Omega(n)$ counted space,
including ordinary head/index-coordinate information in the configuration audit.
On proper strict signed exact-$(2+,2-)$ 3CNF with $N=\Theta(n\log n)$, this is
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ and cannot certify the conditional ARC $N^{1/2}$ writable-space
target. This is NOT a lower bound on arbitrary SAT algorithms, on regenerable
state, or on source-aware decision procedures that never maintain this exact
interface. Each strict22 variable overlaps at most six original unordered
clause pairs, so at most $6n$ of $\binom m2$ original S/U four-mode records
are nontrivial; this sparse combinatorial representation need not be retained
in counted space. An eager full pair matrix and nine-source extreme audit did not
reduce UP-DPLL nodes and increased conservative charged selected work.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} W1 UNSAT has 0 models, same-support signed-swap W1 SAT
has 168; original clause complements, local S/U inversions, four pair extreme

$k\ge4$ tail certified. The same-machine, strict-box,

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-COUPLED-CHILD-BUDGET260 (30 October 2026, 18:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted conditional recurrence algebra, independent referee pending; KNOWN FLMV; COMPUTATIONAL nine matched original-source SAT/UNSAT controls.}
For a hypothesized \emph{uniform, hereditary, all-input, both-label-correct}
ordinary-SAT contractor using at most $N^{3/5+\theta}$ serial
\emph{physical} ordinary children of at most $N^{1/2+\xi}$ bits,
with parent local cost $N^{11/10+\pi}$, independent per-child
policy cost $N^\tau$, per-guest-step slowdown $N^\nu$, nonchild
counted scratch $N^{1/2+\eta}$, and a counted child input buffer,
the \emph{conditional} simultaneous native-FLMV exponent recurrences are
\[
K'\le\max\{11/10+\pi,\,3/5+\theta+\tau,\,
              3/5+\theta+\nu+(1/2+\xi)K\},\quad
L'\le\max\{1/2+\eta,\,1/2+\xi,\,(1/2+\xi)L\}.
\]
Writing $\zeta=\max\{\eta,\xi\}$, this \emph{recurrence certificate}
enters some \emph{strict} true FLMV Theorem~5.7 SAT forbidden box
if and only if
\[
\zeta<1/90,\quad 11/10+\pi<c_\zeta,\quad
3/5+\theta+\tau<c_\zeta,\quad
\theta+\nu+c_\zeta\xi<(c_\zeta-6/5)/2,
\quad c_\zeta^3+(1+2\zeta)c_\zeta^2-c_\zeta-2=0.
\]
The exact entire infinite envelope equals
$d_2(c)=(2+c-c^3)/(2c^2)$ on $6/5\le c\le1.20556943\ldots$;
the child-only materialization inflation threshold is the unique
$\xi_*\approx0.0019122778182$ with
$5c_*^3+10c_*^2-11c_*-10=0$ and
$\xi_*=1/2-(3/5)/c_*$. Thus $N^{0.502}$ physical children
\emph{fail this particular recurrence certificate}, even with other
perturbations zero, while $N^{0.501}$ passes. This is not a theorem
against all possible compilers. Full source re-read per
$N^{3/5}$ children costs $\Omega(N^{8/5})$ source-bit accesses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independently replayed $1{,}313{,}201$
checks across nine original-source SAT/UNSAT formulas, including strict22
W1 UNSAT (0 models) versus its same-support signed-swap SAT (168 models),
original occurrence $S/U$ inversions, $SS/SU/US/UU$ modes, coherent
endpoints, complement true-literal counts, multi-flip trails and an
H1 resolution-certified prohibited cube. Declared selected-logical-event
baseline/two-child UP--DPLL work W1 $10403/10430$, signed-swap
$1315/1298$, H1 $183/175$, XOR3-UNSAT $143/158$; these
are \emph{not native FLMV tape costs}. No physical power-short children
or all-input ARC successor were constructed. Earlier affine-MCSP
and direct-cofactor results remain \emph{restricted}, not universal
SAT hardness. \textbf{OPEN:} the all-input arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$
compiler and $P$ versus $NP$. Source:

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-COVER-GATE-SCOPE262 (30 October 2026, 20:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted many-one cover-gate reduction, independent referee pending.}
For any proper 3CNF $H=\bigwedge_j(a_j\vee b_j\vee c_j)$, set
$F_H=\bigwedge_j[(y\vee a_j\vee z_j)\wedge(\neg z_j\vee b_j\vee c_j)]$.
Every $F_H$ is SAT by $y=1,z_j=0$, while
$F_H\models y$ if and only if $H$ is UNSAT. Hence even a
single-cube \emph{source-relative semantic cover} test is coNP-complete
on parents promised SAT. This is the known backbone/entailment
phenomenon applied to the LSPV-5 residual-cover gate, not an
unrestricted SAT lower bound. Exact SAT child answers cannot certify an
UNSAT parent without a source-relative cover proof, a complete
syntactic partition, or an exact uncovered-region fallback; witness
termination on the SAT side does not require cover.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent 10-source Python checker passed
135,508 groups; matched original W1/S15 strict22 sources have
$0/861$ models, but both transformed parents are SAT, and their
$y=1$ cover judgments are YES/NO respectively.
A separately executable compact checker passed 2,621,824 local
projection assertions and 7,280 four-way pair-mode checks.
The native FLMV bit-machine transition benchmark remains pending.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM/attribution scope:}
FLMV Theorem 3.4 is a specific linear-nondeterministic-time
multitape-to-SAT encoding, not a statement of the project's
generic external total-child-accessor head-shadow simulator.
The latter needs independent same-machine proof and attribution;
FLMV Theorem 5.7 itself is unaffected.
The exact $k=2$ infinite-tail audit again gives
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL/OPEN:} no uniform hereditary
arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ ordinary SAT contraction exists yet.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN.}

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-EXPONENT-SELECTOR263 (30 October 2026, 21:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted conditional computability reduction; independent mathematical referee pending.}
Assuming a fixed correct polynomial SAT decider $A$ of exponent $K$ exists,
fix $D>K+2$. For each two-counter program $M$, an effectively compiled
correct polynomial SAT decider $B_M$ reads unchanged source length $N$,
simulates $M$ for $N$ steps, executes a charged $N^D$ dummy delay if
$M$ has not halted, and then calls $A$ on the exact original source.
This uses only $O(\log N)$ additional counted FLMV work/index space.
If $M$ halts, $B_M$ eventually runs in $O(N^K)$ time; if not,
every input takes $\Omega(N^D)$ transitions. Consequently a total
program-code selector deciding $O(N^{D-1})$ worst-case time for
\emph{promised} correct polynomial SAT deciders would decide
two-counter halting. This is the known halting reduction specialized
to guest exponent introspection, \emph{conditional on SAT in P},
not an unrestricted SAT lower bound. A $K$-oblivious ARC transformation
is not ruled out. Full-parent $A(F)$ calls still pay parent-scale time.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} original signed-occurrence all-$S$/all-$U$
and inversion controls, all four pair modes, coherent full-assignment
complements, flip trails, resolution-derived forbidden cubes, and UP/DPLL
checks passed on six matched sources. W1 UNSAT versus S15 SAT:
$0/861$ models, baseline $151/9$ DPLL nodes, $11555/784$ selected work;
a full pair/endpoint diagnostic raised work to $13385/2614$
without pruning nodes. The independent Python checker passed
$1,312,240$ comparison groups on the authenticated W1/S15 pair.
Finite simulated wrapper profiles are not native FLMV timings.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} certified full FLMV 5.7 envelope has
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$, $d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$ at $k=2$,
with an exact strict infinite-tail bound.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL/OPEN:} a uniform all-input, both-label-correct
hereditary ordinary-SAT power-short $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ compiler with paid
selector, failed probes, output accessor, source traffic, guest simulation
and peak combined workspace remains unconstructed.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN.}

\paragraph{ARC-ALL-LEAF-OUTPUT-TARIFF264: charged fully unfolded cofactor outputs (30 October 2026, 22:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary restricted architectural theorem; independent mathematical referee pending.}
For any complete unpruned full binary tree of $q$ logical leaves, with every
leaf separately emitting a raw original-source cofactor of an explicit
proper ternary CNF having $m=\Theta(n)$ original clauses, maximum source
variable incidence $\Delta=O(1)$, and $h_n=\Theta(\log n)$ physical bits
per unchanged original clause, each leaf pins at most $q-1$ variables.
Consequently the \emph{total physically emitted child bits} satisfy
\[
 B_{\rm out}\ge qh_n\max\{0,m-\Delta(q-1)\}.
\]
For $q\le N^\gamma$ with fixed $\gamma<1$ and $N=\Theta(n\log n)$,
each leaf retains $(1-o(1))N$ bits; if $q=N^{3/5+o(1)}$ the obligatory
output alone costs $N^{8/5-o(1)}$ transitions, incompatible with the
hypothesized $N^{11/10+o(1)}$ ARC parent stage for \emph{this
materializing unpruned architecture}.
This is a corollary of classical binary-tree counting and the existing
LSPV-1 Kraft252/LSPV-2 Cofactor252/LSPV-3 Branch-Mass261 restrictions,
\emph{not} unrestricted SAT hardness. Certified P/S terminals, source
proofs, shared physical guest calls, proof DAGs, structural recognition,
streaming child-bit access, and nonlocal re-encodings are not excluded.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent source-indexed same-input SAT/UNSAT
controls passed 1,310,720 complement comparisons, 1,520 original-clause
four-mode checks, 327,680 exact source-cofactor valuations, and
2,500 seeded Kraft-tree checks. Opposite-label equal-unsigned-support
W1/S15 have zero versus 861 models, with each source's
full-cofactor OR/AND correctness verified. Complete balanced guest
partitions with $q=1,4,16,32$ emitted 300,880,2340,3600 physical
child bits per original source, with guest DPLL and output events charged.
These are selected logical operations, \emph{not} native FLMV
deterministic indexed-TM transition or space measurements.

\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} exact FLMV 5.7 infinite-tail audit again
certifies $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$ at $k=2$, with strict
same-machine time/space requirements. \textbf{OPEN:} a hereditary
both-label-correct, arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ ordinary-SAT contractor
with charged selectors, failed trials, source/child indexed access,

\paragraph{ARC-EPOCH-INDEX-LIFT265: bounded-horizon virtual input-index simulation (30 October 2026, 23:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for a restricted supplied-$T$ guest-index simulator; independent mathematical and native-machine referee pending.}
On the same FLMV deterministic indexed-input model, assume an already authentic
ordinary-SAT child of physical length $m$, a reentrant source-aware accessor
of fully charged time $b$ per valid bit query and scratch $s_B$, a fixed guest
decider of at most a \emph{supplied valid} $T$ steps, guest work/index space
$S_A$, virtual input-index tape head high-water $H_A$ and concurrently
retained parent state $R$. Epoch-tagged physical work blocks implement logical
one-step index-tape erasure without naive full-tape rescanning, while an
$O(\log(m+T+2))$-bit low-address register, a high-index-bit counter and
a bounded-horizon FAR state preserve all guest input symbols. One finite-tape
implementation has
\[
T_{\rm sim}=O\bigl(T[b+\log^2(m+T+2)]\bigr),\qquad
S_{\rm sim}=O\bigl(S_A+H_A\log(m+T+2)+s_B+R+\log(m+T+2)\bigr).
\]
This does \emph{not} supply a $K$-oblivious horizon for arbitrary polynomial
SAT deciders, or a uniformly cheap source-authenticated power-short ordinary
child, or proof of ARC. These essential premises remain \textbf{OPEN}.
For a hypothetical source-bit accessor costing $N^\beta$ per guest read,
the conditional ARC fixed-time exponent becomes $6/5+2\beta$; at
$c=241/200$ the strict fixed-box condition requires $\beta<1/400$,
if all other unconstructed assumptions hold.

\paragraph{ARC-QUIESCENT-DFS-LEDGER266: serial-child peak-space and indexed guest access (31 October 2026, 00:39 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted conditional architecture lemma, external referee pending:}
Given an \emph{authenticated} exact OR cover by at most $N^\gamma$
genuine ordinary-SAT children of \emph{physical} lengths $N^\lambda$,
parent setup $N^\alpha$, per-child policy/source acquisition $N^\delta$,
fully charged child-bit/guest-step adapter $N^\beta$, guest
$T_A(m)\le m^{K+o(1)}$, $S_A(m)\le m^{L+o(1)}$, and paid
reset $m^{L+o(1)}$, a \emph{quiescent sequential} implementation
has exponent upper ledgers
\[
K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\delta,\gamma+\lambda,
\gamma+\beta+\lambda K,\gamma+\lambda L\},\qquad
L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda,\lambda L\}.
\]
One OR result bit suffices for both SAT and UNSAT when the source-relative
cover is proved; no $q$ sibling-label or sibling-guest factor is required
in \emph{simultaneous} peak space. Geometric hereditary shrink gives
$N^{\sigma+o(1)}$ total retained ancestor frames under the stated positive
frame exponent and fixed base case, but no universal shrinker is thereby
supplied. Every source reread and missed selector is still time-charged.
Repeated entire-parent scans with $\delta=1$ and $\gamma=3/5$ cause an
$N^{8/5+o(1)}$ time contribution; physically writing genuinely short
$N^{1/2}$ children would instead cost $N^{11/10}$, provided the missing
all-input constructor actually exists.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted single-head buffer counterexample:}
$q$ alternating reads of endpoints of an $m$-byte serialized child on one
sequential work tape cost $(q-1)(m-1)$ head moves. The \emph{label-independent}
guest wrapper writes $O(q\log m)$ bits of genuine FLMV input indices and
then calls an unchanged exact SAT decider, so it preserves both labels.
Therefore serializing a child on a work tape is not, by itself, a
constant-time FLMV guest input-accessor theorem. This particular sweep
can be defeated by caching or a different indexed representation; no
all-machine lower bound is inferred.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent enumerative checker passed 16,823
assertions over six SAT/UNSAT sources, with original W1/S15 model counts
0/861; source-indexed all-$S$/all-$U$ and inversions, SS/SU/US/UU
compatibility, coherent endpoints/flips and sound original resolvents
were replayed. The matched W1/S15 streamed child-buffer peaks were

dummy indexed reads, the W1 first child had 1,600 binary address writes
versus 39,601 \emph{naive sequential} buffer-head moves; S15 had
1,592 versus 39,204. These are instrumented exact selected events,
\emph{not} native FLMV runtime/space benchmarks.
\textbf{KNOWN FLMV:} $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$ with $k=2$ and certified exact
infinite tails, with strict same-machine entry only. The 265

\subsection*{2026-10-31 01:36 PDT: LSPV-4 Joint ARC Overhead Gate267}
\noindent\textbf{CONDITIONAL same-machine budget; exact algebra and independently
checked controls, universal constructor OPEN.}
The currently unconstructed hereditary arbitrary-decider ARC recurrence, with
$N^{\gamma+\theta}$ physically available child calls of length
$N^{\lambda+\xi}$, additional guest-index accessor exponent $\beta$,
and per-child source/policy/output cost exponent $\rho$, has the
\emph{conditional} joint resource bounds
\[
K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\theta+\rho,\gamma+\theta+\beta+
(\lambda+\xi)K\},\qquad
L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda+\xi,(\lambda+\xi)L\}.
\]
On the proposed $(\alpha,\gamma,\lambda,\sigma)=
(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$, with a counted child work-tape buffer and genuine
quiescent sequential calls, the strict FLMV box at
$c=241/200$, $d=11642479/23232400$ is certified by these
\emph{upper recurrences} precisely when
\[
\theta+\beta+\frac{241}{200}\xi<\frac1{400},\quad
\xi<\frac{26279}{23232400},\quad
\frac35+\theta+\rho<\frac{241}{200},
\qquad\rho\ge\frac12+\xi.
\]
These are not necessary conditions for all SAT algorithms, since the
recurrence itself is a hypothetical contractor.  Two individually small
$\theta=\beta=1/600$ exceed the \emph{joint} $1/400$ time slack; a physical
child growth $\xi=1/800$ passes the time test but fails counted space.
A separate original-source rescan per child gives stage exponent $8/5$,
not the required $<241/200$.  The exact FLMV infinite-tail check again
gives $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and $d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, both at $k=2$.

\emph{source-authentication acquisition tariff}, not a SAT runtime floor.
For the proper 3CNF source consisting of $q$ repeated parity-chain
components, all pinned to zero, the original source is SAT; flipping
\emph{one} terminal pin to one yields an UNSAT source of the same unsigned
support. In a balanced native ASCII-DIMACS control, each component's
four-clause terminal pin is paired with an independent opposite-sign
four-clause private pin, eliminating the original unpadded ASCII end-marker
length side channel. The entire signed source remains physically equal
length when any single component is inverted, and only bits within that
component differ. Thus any deterministic exact bit-probe policy which
classifies the SAT source versus each of the $q$ single-odd UNSAT sources
must inspect at least one changed original source bit in every component
on the all-even SAT source, by a transcript indistinguishability proof.
For constant boundary width, $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ distinct probes,
\emph{restricted to this recognizable promise}. One authenticated
$O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ scan, $O(\log N)$ position counters and two
pin-presence bits avoid redoing the scan per child, so the acquisition
barrier is \emph{not} a lower bound for arbitrary SAT and requires no
$\Omega(q)$ retained workspace. The direct balanced checker passed
18,534 tests and separately matched opposite-label DPLL controls;
the unbalanced peer-grammar diagnostic passed 54,283 checks.
At $q=1500$ the balanced SAT source uses $3{,}095{,}632$ ASCII bits
and the canonical ordinary child $1{,}016$ bits; at $q=7001$ a
balanced UNSAT source uses $16{,}122{,}008$ bits and a
$1{,}984$-bit contradictory child, with exact output bytes checked
by a second canonical serializer. These restricted genuine short
children neither supply a uniform all-input hereditary $A\mapsto\Phi(A)$
nor establish matched native FLMV guest simulation. The exact FLMV
Theorem 5.7 values remain $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$ (both $k=2$, complete certified
tail $k\ge4$); strict simultaneous time and workspace apply to one
deterministic indexed input Turing machine. \textbf{P versus NP OPEN.}

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-LAMBDA-SPACE-PARETO269 (CONDITIONAL exact joint frontier; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
The FLMV Theorem~5.7 infinite-index envelope is independently certified:
$d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and
$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, each uniquely at $k=2$
with an exact tail exclusion. Reconciliation of earlier JOINT-SLOPE-FRONTIER255
($\lambda=1/2$), COUPLED-CHILD-BUDGET260 ($\xi$-only),
and JOINT-SLACK-GATE267 (one fixed $c$) yields the optimized
\emph{conditional} physical-child recurrence fixed point
\[
K_*=\max\left\{\tfrac{11}{10},\tfrac35+\theta+\rho,
\frac{3/5+\theta+\beta}{1/2-\xi}\right\},
\quad L_*=\tfrac12+\max(\xi,\eta).
\]
With $z=\max(\xi,\eta)<1/90$ and $c_z$ the positive root
$c_z^3+(1+2z)c_z^2-c_z-2=0$ in $(6/5,1.20557)$, a
\emph{hypothesized} compiler's certified upper bounds enter some
strict FLMV box exactly when
$\tfrac35+\theta+\rho<c_z$ and
$\theta+\beta<(\tfrac12-\xi)c_z-\tfrac35$.
The physical output term $\rho\ge\tfrac12+\xi$ applies when
$\Theta(N^{3/5+\theta})$ children actually attain the length exponent
and are fully emitted; virtual/fewer/smaller children need separate
source-access and peak-space accounting. The exact rational witness
$(\xi,c)=(3/2000,301/250)$ passes strict bounds while failing the
previous fixed $c=241/200$ space gate. The $\xi$-only crossover
$0.0019122778182\ldots$ and baseline guest tariff
$0.0027847152003\ldots$ reproduce predecessor results, not new
unrestricted SAT bounds.

Six original-source controls (W1 strict22 UNSAT, its signed-swap SAT,

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-LABELCOUPLED-ONEHOT270 (PROVED/EXACT restricted source-probe lemma; independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
A matched proper source 3CNF with seven original variables and $m$ indexed
clauses uses one marked sign position $h$ plus eight source-authenticated
forced-literal clauses.  For all even $m\ge4$, every fixed-$m$ source has
the \emph{same physical binary length}, and the family is SAT exactly
when $h$ is even; the odd-mark variant is UNSAT.  The signed source
cofactor $x=1$ has a genuine proper-3CNF 10-clause child $C_h$,
of constant encoded length and preserving both source labels.  In the
declared deterministic source-bit-probe promise model, a \emph{cold} query
for the first child's signed literal bit (which is also its SAT label
on this recognizable family) requires at least $m/2=\Omega(N)$
charged original-source sign probes, including all pre-query source
observations. This follows from an odd/even uninspected-marker
transcript indistinguishability argument. The exact claim is
\emph{restricted}; the family is globally easy and a paid once-only

cold-query tariff. Earlier ONEHOT253, PIN268, MARKER-PROBE-FUSION114,
and BATCH-RANK-AMORTIZATION-REFEREE211 contain related precursor
techniques and are not silently superseded.

To prevent an invalid parent-length query count on a constant-size
child, a \emph{separate} proper-3CNF control appends

clauses, producing a genuine $M=N^{1/2+o(1)}$ child on both labels.
A fixed guest dummy-read wrapper then makes $\lceil M^{6/5}\rceil$
indexed reads before invoking its supplied correct SAT decider.
A fully reset cold per-request source-bit accessor has the
architecture-specific $\Omega(N^{8/5-o(1)})$ source-probe tariff;
a charged cache defeats it. Neither is an unrestricted SAT lower bound
or an achieved ARC contractor.

The producer passed $34{,}752$ original-indexed complement/pair and
same-source SAT/UNSAT assertions; a separately implemented executable
checker passed $16{,}488$ checks, including exact finite minimax

These are \emph{not native FLMV transitions}; CDCL and native-machine

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC source/guest reentry reconciliation, STRIDE271, MULTIPIN272 and BOUNDED-GUEST273 (31 October 2026, 06:06 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted; external mathematical review pending.}
STRIDE-VIRTUAL271 produces an explicitly encoded $M=\Theta(\sqrt N)$ ordinary-SAT child on a recognizable original signed-3CNF grammar, with a charged logarithmic-state exact child-byte accessor after source validation.
Its first source-dependent child character is in clause nine, offset $H+8D$, not in the first clause.
MULTIPIN-WARM-CACHE272 extends this to $q=N^{3/5-o(1)}$ independent signed source pins and $q$ actual ordinary children of length $M=\Theta(\sqrt N)$, satisfying
$\mathrm{SAT}(F)=\bigvee_j\mathrm{SAT}(C_j)$ and
$\mathrm{UNSAT}(F)=\bigwedge_j\mathrm{UNSAT}(C_j)$ on both labels.
A fully quiescent no-source-revisit arbitrary-label summary requires $q$ bits by exact INDEX injectivity; a paid per-child indexed source read and one current-sign cache avoid that joint retention on the actual FLMV machine.

BOUNDED-GUEST-INPUT273 proves a \emph{restricted} guest indexed-input simulation on the actual multitape index-erasure semantics when the guest input-index head stays within $h=O(\log M)$ and its input-head addresses remain $O(M)$:
with the true $M$-byte child \emph{stored on a counted host work tape}, $T_{\mathrm{sim}}=O(M+T_A(M)\log M)$ and
$S_{\mathrm{sim}}=O(M+S_A(M)+\log M)$.
Sequential guest workspace clearing additionally costs $O(M+S_A(M))$ per call, not zero, while the live peak does not multiply by $q$.
The FLMV output tape is write-only and cannot serve as a free readable child buffer.
A concrete two-jump SAT/UNSAT control falsifies an emulator that erases index contents \emph{and resets the index head}.
A correctness-preserving $M$-step input-index sweep followed by a blank read at address $2^M$ falsifies the inference that every correct polynomial SAT guest has logarithmic input-index head excursion.
This is a wrapper adversary, \emph{not} a universal-machine hardness theorem.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls.}
For 12 matched original sources, 93 true child labels and 24,246 abstract guest input-read events passed independent original-source checks;
61,440 source assignments for $q\le4$ and all 35,058 original clause pairs (140,232 SS/SU/US/UU modes) were verified, together with all 2,151 original signed occurrences, $S/U$ inversions, coherent endpoints, intermediate flip trails, and 729 source-indexed forbidden cubes with direct axiom or non-tautological resolvent provenance.
A separate true SAT/UNSAT eight-clause pair exposes index-head reset and far-blank-address failures.
At $q=16$ a 1,973-byte source has 16 children of 264 bytes; parent UP-DPLL 27 UNSAT nodes and 12 last-pin SAT nodes compare with 1,328 and 1,271 child nodes, respectively, before full machine tariffs.
These host-language counts are not native FLMV transitions or CDCL benchmarks.

Status: PROVED/EXACT restricted local-feasibility/physical-output tariff; COMPUTATIONAL controls; CONDITIONAL FLMV contractor; universal \(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) OPEN.
For original proper 3CNF clauses, \(P_{ij}\) means same-polarity and \(Q_{ij}\) opposite-polarity shared variables.
The exact local all-literals TRUE/FALSE compatibility vector is
\((SS,SU,US,UU)=(\neg Q_{ij},\neg P_{ij},\neg P_{ij},\neg Q_{ij})\).
Selected local S/U demands are jointly realizable iff pairwise compatible, since all reduce to unary variable equalities; this is neither a formula SAT nor UNSAT certificate.
A fully emitted dense table contains exactly \(4\binom m2\) bits, costing \(\Omega(m^2)\) output time and, if retained, counted workspace.
A sparse incidence index tracks at most \(R\le\sum_x\binom{d_x}{2}\) distinct shared pairs at \(O(m+\sum_x d_x^2)\) expected word work and may require \(\Theta((m+R)\log m)\) explicit retained bits.
A selected pair instead admits \(O(\operatorname{polylog} N)\) charged indexed source work and \(O(\log N)\) scratch under a previously authenticated fixed-width original source grammar, but query multiplicity, heads, discovery, policy switches and guests must be paid.

For \(64\) disjoint W1 copies (\(m=1280\)), dense four-mode output is \(3{,}274{,}240\) bits, while \(4{,}672\) distinct sparse pairs consume \(112{,}128\) explicit record bits.
Producer performed \(10{,}668\) pair/multiclause checks; independent verifier passed \(15{,}325\) finite checks, without native FLMV timing or industrial CDCL benchmarking.
Exact rational checks confirm the full FLMV Theorem~5.7 envelope \(d_*(6/5)=23/45\), \(d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400\), each uniquely attained at \(k=2\) with the \(k\ge4\) infinite tail excluded.
The conditional fixed point \((6/5,1/2)\) fits the strict FLMV box only if a fully charged same-machine, all-input, hereditary, two-label-correct arbitrary-\(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) compiler is built. That OPEN construction does not follow from prior FAR-BLANK274, ANCHORED-INDEX275, affine MCSP, strict22 normalization or CP22LIFT1.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-AMORTIZED-INDEX282 (31 October 2026, 14:36 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted FLMV physical-bit accounting; independent external review pending.}
For one fixed finite-tape indexed-input machine, let $D$ be the sum of
fixed-cell physical Hamming distances across disjoint checkpoint intervals
inside the \emph{entire} $T$-step run, $Q$ its direct non-erasure bit flips,
$E$ its atomically erased live index bits, $B_0$ its initially live index bits,
and $q$ the fixed bound on direct bit flips per transition.  The potential
of live index bits proves
\[
 D\le Q+E\le 2Q+B_0\le2qT+B_0.
\]
Thus FLMV bulk index erasure invalidates a \emph{local} per-reset $D/q$
claim, but a global $D/(2q)$ bound survives from a blank initial index.
This does not count semantic clause XOR, head movement, source reads or
output as physical writes.  Separate proof corrects the canonical
duplicate-free monotone-(2,2) DIMACS byte count to
$B(n)=H(n)+5m+2n+4\sum_{i=1}^{n}\operatorname{digits}_{10}(i)$:
$B(6)=86$, $B(198)=3674$.  Earlier ARC280 omitted one zero-token byte per
clause; the asymptotic exponent is unchanged.

$d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, unique maximizing $k=2$ at
both points and strictly bounded entire $k\ge4$ tail.

\paragraph{LSPV-4 ARC-TARGET-QUOTA-BATCH284 (31 October 2026, 16:36 PDT; PROVED/EXACT restricted architecture, COMPUTATIONAL same-target controls, general ARC/FLMV OPEN).}
For the complete NP-complete D\"ocker duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) target, define only the \emph{restricted} quota-checking policy that scans all $P=m(1+3w)$ original binary clause payload bits once for each block of at most $b$ variables, retaining two saturating two-bit counters per variable and no inter-block source cache. For $n$ variables and $m=4n/3$ original clauses its exact ledger is $p=\lceil n/b\rceil$ scans, $R=Pp$ source-bit reads, $S_{\rm counter}=4b$ fixed counter-tape bits, and
\[
R\,S_{\rm counter}=4Pb\lceil n/b\rceil\ge4Pn=\Theta(N^2/\log N).
\]
Consequently this \emph{particular} method with $S_{\rm counter}\le N^{1/2+o(1)}$ requires $R\ge N^{3/2-o(1)}$ input-bit reads, beyond the hypothetical ARC $N^{11/10}$ local work allowance. It is \emph{not} an all-algorithm target grammar, SAT, or FLMV lower bound. For an actually materialized fixed binary counter tape, with counters $00\to01\to10$, per-bit writes and explicit between-batch clearing, cumulative \emph{counter-subtape} physical XOR is exactly $D_{\rm counter}=6n+2(n-\ell)$ with $\ell=n-(p-1)b$, endpoint counter XOR $2\ell$. The logical full-state XOR is not a compulsory writable-tape trace; original source scans, index tape resets/head motion, parser scratch, selection, failed routes, child/guest work and ancestor retention remain charged independently. Quota-correct malformed counterexamples with duplicate clauses, mixed signs and repeated literals show additional grammar validation is compulsory.

The no-import independent verifier completed 73,928 checks on the authentic Y6 SAT (20 models), U198 UNSAT (six separately verified 179-node private refutations plus all 64 boundary rows), and V198 SAT (198-bit full witness; original-clause first-literal swap zero-based 1/7). All original signed occurrence $S_j/U_j$ targets/inversions, indexed SS/SU/US/UU compatibilities, coherent endpoints, flip/complement trails and matched UP-DPLL baselines were retained as adverse controls. U198/V198 both have $P=6600$; at $b=1$, $R=1{,}306{,}800$ with $D_{\rm counter}=1582$, versus $R=6600$ and $D_{\rm counter}=1188$ at $b=198$. U/V baseline DPLL remains UNKNOWN at 1200 nodes and was not used for labels. Independently checked exact FLMV Theorem 5.7 envelope remains $d_*(6/5)=23/45$ and $d_*(241/200)=11642479/23232400$, each uniquely at $k=2$ with strict entire $k\ge4$ tail; numeric transport to this target, fully charged arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ heredity, and P versus NP remain \OPEN. Source review includes REDIRECT1, CONTRACT1, AFFINE-GATE2, CONSTRUCTION-STRENGTHEN17, SATURATION1, LSPV1 payload sensitivity, LSPV5 XOR activity identity, original FLMV/D\"ocker/Munro--Paterson/Beame literature and project/openai GitHub searches. \paragraph{XOR-ACTIVITY-IDENTITY-REFEREE251 (31 October 2026, 15:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT fixed-representation activity identity; COMPUTATIONAL
same-target controls; XOR as an independent hardness resource
FALSIFIED/RETIRED; universal ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
For the aligned state
\[
 S_e(F,a)=a\ \Vert\ \bigl(e(t_j(a)),\mathbf1[t_j(a)>0]\bigr)_{j=1}^{m},
\]
one flip of variable \(x_i\) in any CNF without repeated variables inside a
clause has
\[
 d_{\rm logic}^{\rm Gray}=1+d_i+B_i,\qquad
 d_{\rm logic}^{\rm bin}=1+d_i+B_i+H_i,
\]
where \(d_i\) is source occurrence degree, \(B_i\) counts incident
\(0\leftrightarrow1\) true-count transitions, and \(H_i\) counts
\(1\leftrightarrow2\) transitions.  Gray adjacent count codewords differ in
one bit; binary uses two bits only on the middle edge.  Every valid D\"ocker
target has \(d_i=4\), giving \(5+B_i\) and \(5+B_i+H_i\), both between five
and nine.  Summing proves the arbitrary, possibly backtracking trace identities
\[
 \sum_t d_{{\rm logic},t}^{\rm Gray}=5T+\sum_t B_t,\qquad
 \sum_t d_{{\rm logic},t}^{\rm bin}=5T+\sum_t(B_t+H_t).
\]
Thus the declared logical XOR is a local transition ledger, independent of the
SAT/UNSAT label.

Exact controls covered all 384 single flips of all 64 \(Y_6\) assignments,
13,068 \(U_{198}\) flips and 13,266 \(V_{198}\) flips, for 239,310 target
identity assertions.  A common 1,000-flip trace gave UNSAT/SAT Gray totals
6008/6009 and binary totals 7930/7928.  The prior 59,504-node same-source UNSAT
certificate and complete SAT witness were replayed; capped UP-DPLL left both
large instances UNKNOWN at 301 nodes.  All 792 signed occurrences, 34,716
clause pairs and five malformed-input rejections remain controls.  Horn,
2-SAT, easy XOR and bounded-treewidth checks show the identity is cheap on
tractable families.  DELAY, rereads, lazy flags and restored bits block
physical transfer.

Discovery, source/index access, policy choice, switching, failed trials,
children, output, checking and peak writable space remain charged.  No child
or quantitative FLMV transport is constructed.  See

\paragraph{XOR-ORDER-SCOPE-REFEREE250 (31 October 2026, 14:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT comparable-endpoint logical XOR formula; COMPUTATIONAL
same-target controls; unrestricted order/runtime inference FALSIFIED/RETIRED;
universal ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
For any clausewise-monotone CNF with distinct variables within each clause,
fix the aligned logical state
\[
 S(a)=a\ \Vert\ \big\Vert_j(e(t_j(a)),[t_j(a)>0]).
\]
If \(a\le b\) or \(a\ge b\) coordinatewise, every path that flips each
differing variable exactly once toward \(b\) has cumulative adjacent Hamming
distance
\[
 d_H(a,b)+\sum_j\sum_{r=\min(t_j(a),t_j(b))}^{\max(t_j(a),t_j(b))-1}
 \bigl(d_H(e(r),e(r+1))+\mathbf1_{\{r=0\}}\bigr).
\]
Each clause count is monotone and traverses each intervening integer exactly
once, proving order independence.  Full \(0^n\)-to-\(1^n\) sweeps therefore
give binary cumulative/endpoint totals \(n+5m,n+3m\) and Gray totals
\(n+4m,n+2m\).

The hypothesis is sharp.  For one clause \((x_1\vee x_2\vee x_3)\), binary
start/end \(001\to110\) gives cumulative totals \(7\) and \(9\) under two
orders; Gray \(001\to010\) gives \(4\) and \(6\).  A restored-bit detour with
the same \(000\to110\) endpoints doubles the binary direct total from \(6\)
to \(12\) (Gray \(5\) to \(11\)).  All \(720\) full \(Y_6\) sweep orders have
logical totals binary \(46\), Gray \(38\), while one declared eager
single-tape realization ranges over \(295\)--\(319\) and \(287\)--\(311\)
native steps.  Thus logical XOR does not determine physical time.

Exact controls include 10,592 comparable-endpoint paths and 63,552 assertions
on \(Y_6\), full witness replay for SAT \(V_{198}\), the empty module-boundary
certificate for UNSAT \(U_{198}\), 34,716 signed S/U clause-pair tests, five
malformed-input rejections, and independent whole-run index-recharge checks.
A fresh capped solver run returned \(V_{198}\) SAT and honestly left
\(U_{198}\) unresolved; no timeout was treated as UNSAT.  Discovery, source
and index access, switching, failed trials, children, output, checking and
peak writable space remain charged.  See

\medskip
\paragraph{XOR-STATE-DIFFERENCE-REFEREE249 (31 October 2026, 13:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT fixed-tape interval write bound; COMPUTATIONAL canonical
controls; logical-XOR universal runtime inference FALSIFIED/RETIRED; universal
ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
For one execution on \(k\) fixed binary writable tapes and increasing
checkpoints, every differing endpoint cell changes at least once in its
interval, hence
\[
 T\ge\sum_i\left\lceil
 \operatorname{popcount}(W_i\oplus W_{i+1})/k\right\rceil .
\]
The intervals are disjoint and endpoint distance is at most the adjacent sum.
The statement is one-sided and applies only to the same declared physical
cells.  It does not include read-only source cells, implicit logical residuals,
or free tracing.

An exhaustive two-tape/four-cell audit covered 16,105 traces and 105,415
assertions.  Complete \(Y_6\) binary/Gray cycles gave aligned logical totals
648/512, eager physical totals 648/512, and lazy assignment-only totals 126/64;
both lazy policies still used 1,536 literal reads.  The U198/V198 ten-variable
cycles had identical ledgers despite opposite exact labels.  DELAY to 10,000
steps, restored scratch, same-value writes, binary/Gray counter encodings and
unaligned packed records falsified any equality between XOR and work.  A fresh
HiGHS trial found V198 SAT in one node/0.084 seconds, while U198 was correctly
reported UNRESOLVED at the charged eight-second cap; its exact UNSAT label comes
from the independently replayed empty module-boundary certificate.

Logical clause-status XOR is therefore an offline diagnostic, not an
arbitrary-machine lower-bound invariant.  Acquisition, checkpoint placement,
source/index/head activity, policy selection, switching, failed trials,
children, guests, output, proof checking and peak writable space remain
charged.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-31_1348_MAIN_XOR-STATE-DIFFERENCE-REFEREE249.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/lspv5_249/}, and
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-31_1348_LSPV5_XOR_STATE_DIFFERENCE_REFEREE249_raw.json}.

\paragraph{COMPONENT-CLOSURE-REFEREE248 (31 October 2026, 12:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT source-faithful component closure; universal ARC and
P versus NP OPEN.}
For every valid duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) formula \(F\), a
nonempty set of unchanged original clauses induces another valid target
instance iff it is a union of whole connected components of the bipartite
clause--variable incidence graph.  Hence component labels combine by exact
conjunction for both SAT and UNSAT, but a connected valid input has no proper
source-faithful target child.  Independent exhaustive enumeration checked all
65,790 nonempty subsets of connected \(Y_6\) and \(Y_6\sqcup Y_6\) with
zero theorem mismatches.  The same-source 198-variable controls are both
connected: \(U_{198}\) is re-certified UNSAT by the empty 64-row module
intersection and \(V_{198}\) is SAT by full witness replay.  A fresh HiGHS
run used 46,035 versus 1 branch-and-bound nodes.

Explicit linear-time component discovery retains parent-scale state; Reingold
gives logarithmic space and polynomial time but not the strict numerical time
bound or ranked dense child-bit access.  Repeated connectivity queries avoid
the stored map only at an unproved polynomial-time tariff.  Thus the theorem
is an adopted restricted hereditary rule, not an arbitrary-algorithm lower
bound or all-input power-short ARC compiler.  Validation, malformed rejection,
policy discovery, failed trials, switching, source/index/output access,
children, verifier, and retained state remain charged.  Report and reproducible

\paragraph{FIXED-SLOT-ACCESSOR-REFEREE246 (31 October 2026, 10:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted accessor composition; KNOWN virtual-input
transfer; COMPUTATIONAL controls; universal ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
On the checked fixed-width contiguous-block promise of LSPV-1
ORDERED-SEPARATOR-STAGE276, each simplified source clause can be replaced by
exactly eight ordinary proper-3CNF slots.  Widths \(3,2,1,0\) use respectively
\(1,2,4,8\) active clauses, while satisfied records and unused slots receive
existentially harmless positive clauses on private variables.  Thus the
requested logical child bit determines its source record and slot by arithmetic
rather than a prefix scan.  Exact length, all in-range symbols, and standard
out-of-range blanks are computed in \(\log^{O(1)}N\) time and \(O(\log N)\)
scratch.  Composing with the complete-total virtual-input theorem closes the
native arbitrary-guest input bridge on this promise and yields
\[
 K'\leq\max\{11/10,3/5+K/2\},\qquad
 L'\leq\max\{1/2,L/2\}.
\]
The construction is not all-input or hereditary: nonmembers lack a certified
power-short successor, and the padded child is not proved to re-enter the
ordered promise.  Four matched original sources (two SAT, two UNSAT, including
renaming) passed 51240 physical/virtual/far-blank bit requests, full
occurrence-indexed S/U and four-mode controls, exact joins, witnesses,
resolution provenance, and an independent 796-check verifier rejecting eight
corruptions.  The finite padded children expand to 848 bits from 338/440-bit
parents and the guest route used 5292 DPLL nodes versus 22 direct nodes, so no
finite speedup is claimed.  Raw SHA-256 is

\paragraph{BALANCED-ROUTING-REFEREE245 (31 October 2026, 09:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT volume aggregation and explicit routing ledgers;
universal ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
For any physical child lengths \(M_i\), \(V=\sum_iM_i\),
\(M=\max_iM_i\), and \(K\geq1\),
\[
 \sum_i M_i^K\leq VM^{K-1}.
\]
Consequently, a balanced articulation split with total encoded child volume
\(N^{1+o(1)}\) and maximum child \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) charges all serial calls to
the same arbitrary guest by \(N^{1/2+K/2+o(1)}\) time and
\(N^{L/2+o(1)}\) peak guest workspace.  Fixed-width indexed clause lists give
only constant duplication across the two pivot labels, and the exact join is
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(F)\iff
 \bigvee_{b\in\{0,1\}}\left(B_x(b)\wedge
 \bigwedge_i\operatorname{SAT}(F_{i,b})\right).
\]
This independently removes child multiplicity as the obstruction on the
verified balanced family.  It does not supply the router.  On the matched
square-root STAR controls, one parent scan per child costs
\(N^{3/2+o(1)}\) time, retaining all physical children costs
\(N^{1+o(1)}\) space, and retaining linear state for all child guests also
costs \(N^{1+o(1)}\) space.  These are exact failures of three implementations,
not an arbitrary-algorithm lower bound.  The independent SAT/UNSAT finite
control has ten records, 366844 assertion units, full original-occurrence
S/U extremes and complement checks, and an import-independent verifier with
2808 checks and ten rejected corruptions.  Report, producer, verifier, raw
data, and handoff are at

\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-31_0948_LSPV5_BALANCED_ROUTING_REFEREE245_raw.json},
and

\paragraph{RENAME-INSTABILITY-REFEREE244 (31 October 2026, 08:48 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted counterfamily and ordered-promise audit;
universal ARC and P versus NP OPEN.}
For every sufficiently large \(n=s^2-1\), a SAT formula with one binary
coupling clause and private units, and an UNSAT mate with an opposite unit
core, satisfy GATE277's contiguous-bin promise in canonical variable order.
Swapping \(1\leftrightarrow n\) and
\(2\leftrightarrow\lfloor n/2\rfloor\) moves the coupling across two private
bins for every tested logarithmic prefix boundary.  The renamed formulas have
exactly the same native DIMACS length, label, model count and clauses, yet the
recognizer rejects them.  Thus favorable ordered recognition is a
representation property, not a semantic residual-SAT parameter.

The positive restricted result survives: under the explicit ordered promise,
agreement of all block projections on their common boundary is equivalent to
a source model, and the reported upper tariffs
\(D=N^{1+\delta+o(1)}\), \(H=N^{1/2+o(1)}\), and
\(M=N^{\delta+o(1)}\) for \(\delta<1/10\) are valid promise-class bounds.
They do not give an all-input hereditary recurrence.  The next obligation is a
uniform exact fallback or a fully charged permutation-invariant interface
finder, including discovery, policy selection, failed trials, source/index
access, verification, children, retained state and output.  No lower bound on
all such finders, no SAT runtime lower bound, and no FLMV contradiction follows.

Finite replay covered 16 principal SAT/UNSAT rows, 512 fixed-seed random
renamings, H7/H8 original-clause \(S/U\) extremes and inversions, all pair
\(SS,SU,US,UU\) modes, endpoints, complements, flip trails, an excluded cube
and an explicit source resolution.  The independent checker passed 134,588
units and rejected four corruptions.  The affine theorem remains a restricted
source-independent affine MCSP no-go and cannot be promoted to a renaming-
invariant SAT selector.
