\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a conditional computability theorem.}
Assume one fixed correct polynomial-time ordinary-SAT decider \(B\) exists,
with finite joint time/space exponents.  For every integer \(d\) above those
exponents, there is no total computable code analyzer which, even under the
promise that every supplied program is a correct polynomial-time SAT decider,
always distinguishes
\[
  T_A,S_A=O(N^d)
  \qquad\hbox{from}\qquad
  T_A,S_A=\Omega(N^{d+1}).
\]
Given a machine \(M\) and word \(w\), the wrapper simulates \(M(w)\) for
\(N\) steps, sequentially fills \(N^{d+1}\) writable cells iff a halt is
seen, and then returns \(B\)'s exact answer.  A classifier would decide the
halting problem.  Therefore the universal residual controller may not receive
a free exact/tight \(K,L\) threshold bit from arbitrary guest code.  This does
not refute an exponent-oblivious \(\Phi\), and it does not prevent an external
proof from quantifying over the unknown actual exponents.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT finite-prefix firewall.}
For every finite benchmark cutoff, a late-trigger wrapper is identical to a
benign wrapper on every tested input length but eventually has exponent
\(d+1\).  Finite timing or regression data cannot certify the ARC exponent.
The safe repair is a structural exponent-oblivious interface bound or a
separately verified syntactic resource certificate whose restriction is made
explicit.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The source-indexed producer passed 460 assertions and the independent checker
passed 52 groups on three SAT and one UNSAT width-three sources, retaining all
original-clause \(S/U\) targets, inversions, pair modes, coherent endpoints
and complement identities.  Symbolic fill counts were not physically
executed.  Report, code, raw SHA-256 and handoff are readback-verified at
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1248_MAIN_ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1248_ARC_EXPONENT_DISCOVERY_FIREWALL224_repro.py},
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_1248_ARC_EXPONENT_DISCOVERY_FIREWALL224_manifest.md},
and
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-30_1248_LSPV-5_ARC-EXPONENT-DISCOVERY-FIREWALL224_HANDOFF.md}.

The integrated peer state is retained: LSPV-1's completed-call spectrum,
LSPV-2's fixed-\(B\) policy-entry frontier, LSPV-3's SAT-only selector
equivalence, and LSPV-4's far-address/materialization guard are restricted
necessary conditions or firewalls, not a constructed all-input contractor.
The single weakest arrow remains a hereditary both-label ordinary-SAT stage
with physical \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) children and a complete exponent-oblivious
same-machine simulation/selection/space ledger.  The affine-small-bias result
remains restricted MCSP semantics.  P versus NP remains OPEN.



\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-GUEST-REENTRY-FIREWALL225}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted arbitrary-guest simulation falsifier.}
Let \(B\) be any total exact ordinary-SAT decider.  A fixed wrapper \(A_B\)
finds the physical input length \(m\), indexes its read-only input head to
\(m+1\), moves left twice and records the symbol at \(m-1\), then indexes
directly to \(m-1\) and records that symbol again.  It returns \(B\)'s answer
when the symbols agree and its complement otherwise.  On every nonempty
physical input they agree, so \(A_B\) is correct on SAT and UNSAT and adds
only \(O(m)\) time and \(O(\log m)\) writable space.

A virtual-child simulator that handles the far indexed jump by setting a
sticky out-of-bounds state and returns blank after subsequent ordinary head
moves until another indexed jump is incorrect: the first recorded symbol is
blank, the later valid jump reads the actual last bit, and on an encoding
ending in \(1\) the wrapper reverses \(B\).  Thus the existing far-address
comparison is necessary but not sufficient.  A complete simulator must also
reproduce ordinary moves, boundary re-entry, index erasure, guest work/index
tapes and exact blank semantics inside the charged multiplicative access
tariff.  This is a restricted compiler counterexample, not a SAT lower bound
and not a contradiction to FLMV's complete on-demand Cook encoding.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
One fixed identity emitter and wrapper were checked on a proper-3CNF SAT
source and the proper-3CNF UNSAT source containing all eight sign patterns.
Physical/exact re-entry returned the exhaustive labels; sticky OOB and a
valid-domain-only wraparound accessor reversed both.  The audit also passed
7,990 bounded address-shadow membership checks and 7,990 erasure resets.
These logical checks are not native FLMV timings.  Artifacts:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1354_MAIN_ARC-GUEST-REENTRY-FIREWALL225.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1348_guarded_virtual_input_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_1348_guarded_virtual_input_audit.json}.

output bit but permits a full-scan/cache escape.  LSPV-2
VIRTUAL-SORT-ACCESS254 gives an exact polylogarithmic indexed-bit accessor for
an intermediate existential sorting consistency CNF, but the physical
ordinary encoding expands and is not power-short.  LSPV-3
ADDITIVE-MULTIPLICATIVE-GATE257 separates additive per-child scans from

ARC-JOINT-SLOPE-FRONTIER255 gives the conditional fixed point
\[
 K_*=\max\{1.1+\pi,1.2+2\delta\},\qquad L_*=1/2+\eta
\]
and strict tariff margins at the \(241/200\) FLMV slice.  None constructs the

\section*{LSPV-5 Reconciliation: ARC-TOTAL-VIRTUAL-INPUT-RECONCILIATION226}
\PROVED\ (independent project total-accessor lemma; primary-source attribution corrected).
Let a fixed deterministic indexed random-access guest \(A\) run on a complete
ordinary logical child \(G(x)\) of exact length \(m(x)\).  Suppose a total
charged accessor returns \(G(x)_j\) for \(j<m(x)\) and the ordinary blank
symbol otherwise.  A same-model simulator stores the exact logical guest input
head \(h\): copy the input-index address into \(h\) before indexed-jump
erasure, update \(h\) on ordinary moves, compare with \(m(x)\) on every
read, and invoke the accessor only in range.  If the guest uses \(S(m)\)
counted space and \(T(m)\) time, then
\[
 \operatorname{bitlength}(h)\le S(m)+\lceil\log_2(T(m)+2)\rceil+O(1).
\]
For compiler setup \(P,U\) and accessor time/scratch \(R,V\), this gives
\[
 T_{\rm sim}=O\!\left(P+T(m)(R+\log(m+T(m)+2))\right),\qquad
 S_{\rm sim}=O\!\left(U+V+S(m)+\log(T(m)+2)\right).
\]
The fixed simulator does not receive the guest exponent, a SAT label, or a
best-policy oracle.  This closes the read-only logical-input model bridge
\emph{under the complete total-access hypotheses}; it does not construct
those hypotheses or a smaller child.

\PROVED\ (implementation firewall).
Naive child materialization on a work tape, sticky out-of-range state, and an
accessor defined only on valid addresses remain invalid.  Far-jump/re-entry
counterexamples attack those partial implementations, not the independently
proved complete total-accessor simulation.  In matched SAT/UNSAT finite controls, 49,216 exhaustive
or seeded deterministic guest traces had identical complete states under
physical and total-virtual input, while sticky and valid-domain-only variants
failed on both labels.  These are \COMPUTATIONAL\ interface controls, not
native FLMV timings.

\noindent\textbf{Attribution firewall.}
Primary-source inspection shows that FLMV Theorem~3.4 constructs a particular
Cook-style formula for a nondeterministic multitape computation and locally
recomputes requested formula bits in polylogarithmic time and logarithmic
space.  It does not state the generic arbitrary-child total-accessor lemma
above.  The project lemma remains available under its explicit complete-access
hypotheses, but it is not attributed to FLMV Theorem~3.4.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-MULTIINDEX-GATE254 proves a restricted no-reprobe retention floor,
with source rescan and easy-grammar bypasses.  LSPV-2
VIRTUAL-STRICT22-COMPOSE255 gives a direct indexed accessor for a composed

LSPV-3 ARC-SIGNED-OVERLAP-CHILD-GATE258 gives exact direct-cofactor deletion

\section{LSPV-5 persistent-child source-traffic reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-batch-union-227}
\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted fixed-schedule theorem).}
For a source permutation \(\pi\in S_m\), suppose one persistent deterministic
controller retains \(s\) source-dependent bits and serves any number of child
rounds whose fixed public query union contains \(t\) distinct inverse demands.
If \(Q\) is the total number of source-word probes across the whole execution,
then
\[
  2^s m^Q\ge (m)_t,
  \qquad
  s+Q\log_2m\ge\log_2 (m)_t.
\]
The child-round count is absent.  Sixteen children repeatedly asking only
\(\pi^{-1}(0),\pi^{-1}(1)\) give an explicit countercontrol: one scan and a
six-bit cache answer all later children.  Exhaustion of all \(8!\) sources gives
2--8 probes (mean 6), whereas an invalid sixteenfold multiplication would demand
\(16\log_2 56\approx92.918\) bits.  Therefore per-child tensorization under
persistent caching is \FALSIFIED/\RETIRED unless a fresh-independent-demand,
state-erasure, or model-matched direct-sum theorem is separately proved.

\textbf{FLMV accounting consequence.}
Charge actual total source traffic and retained cache simultaneously.  A
controller may cache and pay space or discard and pay re-probes; it may not
multiply a one-child lower bound by the child count while retaining answers for
free.  This restricted metadata theorem neither supplies SAT hardness nor
constructs a complete power-short ordinary-SAT child.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-HEAD-HORIZON-REPLAY255 gives a uniform exponent-oblivious replay
schedule under bounded horizon, but no smaller child.  LSPV-2
AFFINE-GUARD257 proves that complete sign blocks yield exact XOR equations and
also gives same-core opposite-label controls; partial extracted affine theory is
not decision-complete.  LSPV-3 FRONTIER-TWO-SWEEP259 supplies useful restricted
frontier-DP evidence when order, width and table costs are paid, but the inspected
canonical report lacked its required final verification sentinel and therefore
needs a verified republish before serving as synthesis authority.  LSPV-4
ARC-SOURCE-PROBE-TRANSCRIPT257's one-batch transcript theorem is retained; the
new persistent-round audit blocks only an unjustified tensorization of it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The accompanying audit exhausts 120,960 permutation/schedule instances and
records same-source SAT and UNSAT clause-extreme and complete-DPLL controls.
These host-language counts are neither native FLMV transition counts nor an
arbitrary-algorithm lower bound.

\section{LSPV-5 reusable boundary-summary reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-reusable-boundary-228}
\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (restricted deterministic one-way theorem).}
For every \(f:\{0,1\}^b\to\{0,1\}\) there is a uniform proper 3-CNF
\(A_f(X,Y)\) with
\[
  \exists Y\,A_f(x,Y)=f(x)
\]
and fixed explicit encoding length \(N_b=\Theta(b^2 2^b)\).  Proper-3CNF
continuations \(B_a\) pin \(X=a\), hence
\[
  \operatorname{SAT}(A_f\land B_a)=f(a).
\]
If a deterministic preprocessor sees \(A_f\) before the continuation, stores a
summary \(S_f\), and every point continuation must be answered from
\((S_f,B_a)\) without rereading \(A_f\), then \(f\mapsto S_f\) is injective.
There are \(2^{2^b}\) functions, so some summary has at least
\[
  2^b=\Omega\!\left(\frac{N_b}{(\log N_b)^2}\right)=N_b^{1-o(1)}
\]
bits.  Thus no fixed \(N^\lambda\), \(\lambda<1\), gives a universal exact
reusable pre-continuation frontier summary on all CNFs.

\textbf{Scope firewall.}
This is the deterministic one-way INDEX/knowledge-compilation lower bound in an
explicit proper-3CNF realization.  It is not exponential in source length and
does not lower-bound a solver that sees both sides jointly, retains or rereads
the source, recomputes, creates continuation-specific children, or avoids
frontier summaries.  It therefore cannot be imported as a general SAT runtime
lower bound or an FLMV contradiction.  It retires only the declared universal
reusable-summary architecture.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-JUMP-RESET-BISIM256 corrects the earlier no-reentry sentence and
retains exact total jump/reset input-head semantics; no smaller child follows.
LSPV-2 AFFINE-INTERFACE258 proves an exact recognized-source rank quotient with
same-skeleton SAT/UNSAT controls, but emits no \(N^{1/2}\) physical child and its
charged hybrid increased measured total work.  LSPV-3
RECURSIVE-FRONTIER-REPLAY260 gives a valid paid Hirschberg-style replay schedule;
the reusable-summary theorem above blocks promotion of its frontier function to
a universally power-short pre-continuation message, while leaving its restricted
upper bound intact.  LSPV-4 ARC-RESPONSE-WIDTH258 gives the standard prescribed-
order residual-function state floor; paired order, replay and source access are
explicit escapes and must be charged.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
The reproducer exhausts all 65,792 functions for \(b=3,4\), performs 1,050,624
point-query checks, 87,360 existential chain checks through \(b=8\), and 32
proper-3CNF pin checks.  Matched 32-clause SAT/UNSAT controls retain every
original-clause \(S/U\) target and inversion, 992 pair compatibility records,
endpoints, flip trails and common-DPLL work.  These are host-language
construction checks, not native FLMV measurements.

\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1648_MAIN_ARC-REUSABLE-BOUNDARY-SUMMARY-FIREWALL228.tex},
\path{experiments/arc/2026-10-30_1648_reusable_boundary_summary_audit.py},
\path{results/raw/arc/2026-10-30_1648_reusable_boundary_summary_audit.json}, and
\path{research/coordination/2026-10-30_1648_LSPV5_TO_FIVE_ROLE_ARC_REUSABLE_BOUNDARY_SUMMARY228.md}.

For an actual proposed \(\Phi(A)\) stage, prove a universal execution-level

\[
 K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\},\qquad
 L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda L\}.
\]
No such dichotomy or complete hereditary power-short ordinary-SAT child is

\section{LSPV-5 realized-query tariff reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-realized-query-230}
\noindent\PROVED\ (restricted supplied-controller composition theorem).
On the deterministic indexed read-only-input random-access FLMV machine, fix
one uniform controller using serial ordinary-SAT guests.  Put every realized
non-guest transition---discovery, failed policies, address generation, all
source reads including repeats, child output/access, verification, joins,
replay and reconstruction---in $D(F)$.  Put caches, retained ancestors,
head shadows, accessor scratch and writable child buffers in peak live
$H(F)$.  If
\[
D\le N^{\alpha+o(1)},\quad q\le N^{\gamma+o(1)},\quad
m_i\le N^{\lambda+o(1)},\quad \rho_i\le N^{\nu+o(1)},\quad
H\le N^{\sigma+o(1)},
\]
then a same-model guest with simultaneous exponents $(K,L)$ yields
\[
K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\nu+\lambda K\},\qquad
L'\le\max\{\sigma,\lambda L\}.
\]
This partitions one actual native trace; it does not construct the controller,
discover $(K,L)$, or grant a best-policy oracle.

\noindent\FALSIFIED/\RETIRED\ (small distinct-probe union implies ARC).
For distinct source addresses $U$, total source reads $R$, and physical
child output $W=\sum_i m_i$, exactly
\[
U\le R\le D,\qquad W\le D
\]
for the declared bit-machine trace.  These are lower inclusions into local
work, not upper bounds.  On the same canonical proper-3CNF SAT/UNSAT family,
one-address exact wrappers had selected work $7$, $4103$, $896$,
$769$, and $8198$, while retained live state ranged from zero to
$8192$ bits; a compiler-layer zero-probe parent fallback used $589825$
selected events.  The independent checker passed 147 groups.  These wrappers
are counterexamples to an inference, not unavoidable costs for all algorithms.
Cell-probe and red--blue pebble results remain model-specific lower-bound tools;
small probe/I/O count is not a native-FLMV time upper bound.

\textbf{Specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1's cube-faithful projection criterion gives a sound guarded export but
generic recognition is not free.  LSPV-2's typed transactions preserve
source coverage after paid switching.  LSPV-3's source-RUP learned cores are
globally valid but discovery/minimization and retained clauses are charged.
LSPV-4's coupled physical-child recurrence and strict FLMV margins are retained;
instantiating its parent exponent requires the actual output/access/selection
sum, not separate caps or a distinct-address count.  None constructs the
all-input hereditary arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ stage.

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
For one proposed all-input controller, prove exponent-obliviously on both labels
\[
D(F)\le N^{11/10+o(1)},\quad H(F)\le N^{1/2+o(1)},\quad
\sum_i\rho_iT_A(m_i)\le N^{3/5+(1/2)K+o(1)},
\]
with physical output/access, failed policies and retained ancestors included.
Only then may the known total virtual-input transfer and actual strict FLMV

\section{LSPV-5 residual-cover reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-residual-cover-231}
\noindent\PROVED\ (exact source-relative residual composition theorem).
For a CNF source $F$, partial-assignment cubes $\rho_1,\ldots,\rho_q$, and
$\Gamma=\bigvee_i\operatorname{cube}(\rho_i)$, exact child answers always give
\[
 \bigvee_i \operatorname{SAT}(F\mathord\upharpoonright\rho_i)
 \Longrightarrow \operatorname{SAT}(F).
\]
If the separately authenticated source-relative cover $F\models\Gamma$ holds,
then the converse also holds, and therefore
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(F)
 \Longleftrightarrow
 \bigvee_i\operatorname{SAT}(F\mathord\upharpoonright\rho_i).
\]
Acquiring and checking a refutation of $F\wedge\neg\Gamma$, or an equivalent
coverage certificate, belongs to realized local work $D$; the certificate,
source parents, learned blockers, cache and checker scratch belong to peak live
$H$.  Pairwise cube intersections and exact residual oracles do not substitute
for this global source-relative implication.

\noindent\FALSIFIED/\RETIRED\ (pairwise-overlap UNSAT composition).
Let $F_7$ be the conjunction of the seven proper width-three clauses, one for
each assignment in $\{0,1\}^3\setminus\{111\}$, with each clause falsified by
its indexed assignment.  Then $F_7$ has the unique model $111$.  The three
cubes $x_1=0,x_2=0,x_3=0$ intersect pairwise and cover seven assignments, but
miss $111$; every corresponding residual is UNSAT.  Thus a controller that
returns UNSAT after those exact residual answers is wrong.  Adding the eighth
clause falsified by $111$ gives a same-skeleton UNSAT source on which the same
three residual answers occur; the added source clause is exactly the missing
coverage proof.  The counterexample is proper 3CNF and uses no supplied answer
bit.  More generally, if $\Gamma$ is not a tautology, a proper 3CNF can be
constructed with a unique model outside its cubes.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls.}
The reproducer records the matched $F_7$ SAT/$F_8$ UNSAT pair, hitting,
duplicate-support, Horn, padded-2SAT, XOR, odd-charge $K_4$ Tseitin, strict22
SAT/UNSAT and dense planted controls.  An independent checker passed 154
groups.  On the selected-event ledger the certified residual controller cost
243 versus 127 parent-DPLL events on $F_7$, and 231 versus 133 on $F_8$; hence
the sound certificate did not improve these finite cases.  These host-language
counts are neither native FLMV transition bounds nor arbitrary-SAT lower bounds.

\textbf{Specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1's paid batch selector is valid only under its supplied source-independent
generator and does not prove global cover.  LSPV-2's coherent guarded SCC export
can provide source-authenticated blockers, but non-RUP proof acquisition and
checking remain charged.  LSPV-3's exact two-pivot overlap spectrum is a local
selector statistic and likewise does not establish $F\models\Gamma$.
LSPV-4's full-index rescan theorem supplies a restricted same-model accounting
firewall.  Its corrected COVER-GATE-SCOPE262 report has now been republished
with the required header and final verification sentinel and is incorporated
in the reconciliation below.

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
Construct one exponent-oblivious all-input hereditary ordinary-SAT controller
whose chosen residual cubes either carry a paid certificate of
$F\models\bigvee_i\operatorname{cube}(\rho_i)$ or retain an exact fallback for
the uncovered region, while simultaneously satisfying the realized $D,H$ and
guest tariffs of Section~\ref{sec:lspv5-realized-query-230}.  No such
arbitrary-$A\mapsto\Phi(A)$ contractor is known.  The affine-small-bias theorem
remains restricted MCSP semantics, and the source-clause extreme tests remain
adversarial controls.  P versus NP remains OPEN.  Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_1948_MAIN_ARC-RESIDUAL-COVER-GATE231.tex}.

\section{LSPV-5 source-blocker compression reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-source-blocker-232}
\noindent\PROVED\ (exact representation firewall).
Let $F$ be an explicit CNF of encoded length $N$ and suppose every learned
clause $D_i$ is source-valid, $F\models D_i$.  Then
\[
 H=F\wedge\bigwedge_iD_i\equiv F,
 \qquad B=\bigwedge_iD_i,
 \qquad F\models B.
\]
In the declared append-only explicit encoding, $H$ contains the source
verbatim and therefore $|H|\ge N$; it is not a physical $N^\lambda$ child for
fixed $\lambda<1$.  The blocker-only object $B$ is a relaxation: UNSAT$(B)$
certifies UNSAT$(F)$ and SAT$(F)$ implies SAT$(B)$, but SAT$(B)$ need not imply
SAT$(F)$.  Dropping the source thus requires a separately checked equivalence
or completeness theorem.  Source-valid learning, complete refutation and
exact short re-encoding are distinct ARC endpoints.

\noindent\FALSIFIED/\RETIRED\ (free source deletion after valid learning).
For each $b\ge1$, the matched proper-3CNF pair in the source report has $3b$
variables, $8b$ clauses and the same unsigned support multiset.  The SAT member
has unique model $1^{3b}$; the UNSAT member differs only by replacing one
duplicate clause in its first block with the missing eighth sign clause.  Both
entail the same satisfiable blocker
\[
 B_b=x_1\wedge\cdots\wedge x_{3b},
\]
and every residual under $x_i=0$ is UNSAT on both labels.  Those cubes cover
all assignments except $1^{3b}$, whose exact evaluation distinguishes the
labels.  Therefore exact residual answers plus globally valid blockers still
need a complete top proof or an exact uncovered fallback.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls.}
Independent implementations checked $b=1,\ldots,5$ with 822 assertions.  At
$b=5$, direct parent-DPLL used 2,526 selected events on the SAT member and 517
on the UNSAT member; constructing and checking all certified residuals plus
the uncovered completion used 20,355 and 8,251 respectively.  These Python
event counts are reproducible controls, not native FLMV transition or space
bounds.  The raw record also retains the original-clause S/U occurrence
extremes, inversions, pair modes, endpoints and flip trails, together with the
project's Horn, 2SAT, XOR/Tseitin, strict22 and planted controls.

\textbf{Specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1's raw-cofactor parity cover floor
$q\ge4^b/2^{\lfloor s/2\rfloor}$ is correct under its restricted syntactic
hypotheses; direct Gaussian elimination is an easy paid escape, so it is not
an arbitrary-SAT lower bound.  LSPV-2's source-only DPLL assumption discharge
exports valid clauses on completed NO branches, but acquisition may be
exponential and retaining the source does not shrink it.  LSPV-3's parity
moment camouflage theorem is exact, yet paid signed-source recognition and
GF(2) elimination bypass it.  LSPV-4's satisfiable-source backbone reduction
correctly exposes a coNP-hard cover-checking subproblem and its primary-source
audit supplies the FLMV attribution correction recorded above.

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
Construct one uniform hereditary all-input transformation that, after paid

\section{LSPV-5 quadratic DP output reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-dp-output-234}
\noindent\PROVED\ (exact restricted representation firewall).
For \(r\ge1\), let
\[
 P_i=(x\vee a_i\vee b_i),\qquad
 N_j=(\neg x\vee c_j\vee d_j),
\]
with all gadget variables distinct.  Conjoin these with either the seven
sign clauses on three core variables that leave unique model \(111\), or all
eight sign clauses.  The resulting proper-3CNF twins share the pivot gadget
and are respectively SAT and UNSAT.  Complete Davis--Putnam elimination of
\(x\) produces exactly the \(r^2\) distinct, non-tautological,
non-subsuming resolvents
\[
 R_{ij}=(a_i\vee b_i\vee c_j\vee d_j).
\]
Thus a fixed-width indexed parent of length \(\Theta(r\log r)\) has an
explicit exact projection of length \(\Theta(r^2\log r)\).  Replacing each
\(R_{ij}\) by
\[
 (a_i\vee b_i\vee z_{ij})\wedge
 (\neg z_{ij}\vee c_j\vee d_j)
\]
is existentially exact proper-3CNF re-entry but still uses \(2r^2\) clauses.

\noindent\FALSIFIED/\RETIRED\ (exact separator implies short child).
Logical exactness alone supplies no output-size theorem.  A bounded-output
transaction may remain correct by discarding the partial elimination and
running an exact fallback on the original source; all attempted pairs,
tentative buffer writes and fallback work remain charged.  Retaining the
original formula, a selector or a compact DAG is a legal escape, so this is
not an arbitrary-SAT lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls.}
Independent implementations exhaustively checked pointwise projection on
69,632 boundary assignments over both labels, verified every \(r^2\) count,
and replayed \(r=2,4,8,16\).  At \(r=16\), SAT source/DP/proper-3 lengths
were 3,264/28,656/50,696 bits and UNSAT lengths were
3,352/28,744/50,784 bits.  The transaction aborted after 28 of 256 pairs
and was correct only through exact fallback.  Original-clause \(S/U\)
targets, inversions, four pair modes, endpoints and prefix flips remain
adversarial controls, not decision rules.

\textbf{Specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1 ARC-INDEXED-GUARD263 gives exact guarded-clause export when a charged
proof-bit generator is supplied; generator discovery and all-input short
proofs remain OPEN.  LSPV-2 BOUNDARY-RESOLUTION-SPLICE264 gives exact complete
private-variable elimination and source-proof splicing; the theorem above
supplies its explicit both-label quadratic-output control.  LSPV-3
CUT-ACQUISITION266 forces all blocks to be read only in its canonical
prism-XOR query model, while a linear-time streaming recognizer still emits
a constant child.  LSPV-4 ARC-ALL-LEAF-OUTPUT-TARIFF264 is exact for complete
unpruned trees that separately materialize every raw cofactor; certified
terminals, sharing, virtual accessors and nonlocal encodings escape it.
These results sharpen resource accounting but do not construct the universal
hereditary \(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) stage.

\textbf{Affine and FLMV firewall.}
AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2 remains restricted affine MCSP semantics with
corrected description count
\(\log_2 B_N=O((u_N+1)\log(m+u_N+2))\); nonlinear and source-dependent
representations escape.  Nothing here promotes it to SAT runtime hardness.
No verified controller enters the actual strict FLMV point
\[
 t<241/200,\qquad s<11642479/23232400.
\]

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
Prove for one uniform all-input, both-label, hereditary controller that every
committed child is an exact ordinary-SAT instance of length
\(N^{1/2+o(1)}\), while all discovery, failed transforms, tentative output,
rollback, guest access, proof checking, uncovered fallback and simultaneous
live state fit \(N^{11/10+o(1)}\) time and \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) space without a
best-policy oracle.  No such theorem is known.  P versus NP remains OPEN.
Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_2248_MAIN_ARC-DP-QUADRATIC-OUTPUT-FIREWALL234.tex}.


\section*{Synthesis update: indexed high-water scrub and newest peer cycle}

\noindent\textbf{Executive status.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, corrected simulator ledger:} geometric horizon replay for
the full indexed guest must charge physical high-water reset.  If \(T\) is the
actual guest time and \(W_A(T)\) is the sum of the simulated tape prefixes that
must be restored, exact replay has the bound
\[
 T_{\rm total}=O\!\left(
 T[b+\log^2(m+T+2)]+W_A(T)\log(T+2)\right),
 \qquad
 S_{\rm total}=O(W_A(T)+s_B+R+\log(T+2)).
\]
The first term charges every failed phase and accessor call; the second charges
every full blanking sweep.  TIMEOUT exports no SAT/UNSAT label.  A remote
indexed work-cell wrapper proves that replacing \(W_A(T)\) by \(O(T)\) is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: an indexed write at \(p(N)\) adds only
\(O(\log p(N))\) guest time but sequential prefix scrub costs
\(\Omega(p(N))\).

For \(q=N^{\gamma+o(1)}\) guests of length \(N^{\lambda+o(1)}\), guest time
exponent \(K\), guest space exponent \(L\), accessor exponent \(\beta\), and
local exponent \(\alpha\), the fully charged recurrence is
\[
 K'\le
 \max\{\alpha,\gamma+\beta+\lambda K,\gamma+\lambda L\}.
\]
On the intended diagonals \(K\ge L\), the new scrub term is absorbed.  With
\((\alpha,\gamma,\lambda)=(11/10,3/5,1/2)\), the conditional fixed point remains
\(K_*=6/5+2\beta\), and the fixed FLMV time test \(241/200\) still requires
\(\beta<1/400\).  Off diagonal, dropping \(\gamma+\lambda L\) is unsound.

\noindent\textbf{Independent affine referee verdict.}
AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2 is certified under its declared pointwise
all-input, source-independent randomized affine hypotheses.  Fourier expansion
of affine fibers gives the exact \(B\varepsilon\) comparison; circuit counting
and the explicit easy small-bias source yield error at least \(1/2-o(1)\).
This remains a restricted MCSP semantic no-go.  Nonlinear/source-dependent
preprocessing and arbitrary SAT runtime lie outside its quantifiers.

\noindent\textbf{Newest specialist evidence.}
LSPV-1's cube-signature theorem is a source-free contextual-artifact lower
bound, escaped by source reread and not decision hardness.  LSPV-2's affine
flip quotient supplies recognized easy both-label controls, not general
contraction.  LSPV-3's source-guarded RUP cache is sound under its decision
guard but increased total measured work on every tested source.  LSPV-4's
supplied-horizon epoch-index lift is adopted with the corrected high-water
scrub term above.  None constructs the all-input child/accessor.

The new producer/checker passed 472/474 checks on matched SAT/UNSAT sources,
six DELAY depths, original-occurrence \(S/U\) controls, and 15 far-index
wrappers.  The maximum replayed guest-step ratio was \(2.822<4\); the
transparent wrapper-work proxy increased on every tested source.  Finite event
counts are not native FLMV timings.

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
Construct or falsify a uniform all-input, exact both-label, hereditary ordinary
SAT child of length \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) with total reentrant source-authenticated
accessor exponent \(\beta<1/400\), all discovery, rejected policies, physical
output and proof checking within \(N^{11/10+o(1)}\), and simultaneous live
space \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\).  No such \(\Phi\) is known.  P versus NP remains OPEN.

Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-30_2348_MAIN_ARC-UNKNOWN-HORIZON-SCRUB-REFEREE235.tex}.



\section{00:48 semantic-selector and current specialist reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-semantic-selector-236}
\noindent\PROVED\ (restricted quantifier firewall).
For every nontrivial decision language \(L\), choose fixed strings
\(y_1\in L\) and \(y_0\notin L\).  Then
\[
 \Psi_L(x)=
 \begin{cases}
 y_1,&x\in L,\\
 y_0,&x\notin L
 \end{cases}
\]
is a total deterministic, constant-output, exactly label-preserving and
semantically hereditary map.  Given a decider \(A\), however, the direct
implementation that completes \(A(x)\) and then emits \(y_{A(x)}\) inherits
that parent call's time and peak workspace.  Conversely, a constructor for
this particular two-output map decides \(L\) by testing whether its output is
\(y_1\).  Thus the map proves semantic existence, not a resource improvement.

\noindent\FALSIFIED/\RETIRED\ (arbitrary nonlinear promotion and free short
child).  AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2 remains correct for its
source-independent affine maps \(R_z(x)=A_zx\oplus b_z\), with
\[
 \log_2 B_N=O((u_N+1)\log(m+u_N+2)).
\]
The semantic selector is nonlinear and branches on the full source label, so
counting cannot extend that affine no-go to all nonlinear transformations.
Likewise, a constant exact child does not instantiate ARC when its constructor
first consumes the parent SAT bit.

If the desired recurrence is
\[
 K'\le\max\{\alpha,\gamma+\lambda K\},\qquad 0\le\lambda<1,
\]
a mandatory completed parent call gives \(K'\ge K\) and contradicts the bound
whenever
\[
 K>\max\{\alpha,\gamma/(1-\lambda)\}.
\]
At \((\alpha,\gamma,\lambda)=(11/10,3/5,1/2)\), this threshold is \(6/5\).
DELAY-wrapped exact guests therefore defeat the direct semantic-selector
architecture on both labels.  This is an architecture firewall, not a SAT
lower bound.

\textbf{Current specialist reconciliation.}
LSPV-1's width-four pivot antichain is an exact local source blocker system,
not complete UNSAT coverage.  LSPV-2's sparse affine bypass emits genuine
power-short physical children on recognized redundancy-inflated affine
families, while W1/S15 remain unchanged; this is a useful restricted upper
bound, not all-input contraction.  LSPV-3's minimum definite-Horn guard problem
is NP-complete, but ordinary Horn UP avoids that optimization, so it is not a
mandatory arbitrary-solver cost.  LSPV-4's serial quiescence theorem correctly
removes a multiplicative sibling factor from peak space under its explicit
reset hypotheses, while retaining child multiplicity, source traffic, output,
guest and adapter costs in time.  None constructs the universal hereditary
stage.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL controls.}
The reproducer exhausts all 256 subformulas of the eight proper signed clauses
on three variables: 255 SAT and one UNSAT.  Fixed proper-3CNF SAT/UNSAT children
preserve every label.  The direct parent DPLL used 1,378 aggregate nodes and
the selected children another 1,290; decision-then-child used 2,668 selected
nodes.  The H7/H8 matched-support controls retain all local \(S/U\) inversions,
196 pair modes, endpoints and flip trails.  The producer passed 512 assertion
groups and an independently coded checker passed 565 checks.  These are
host-language controls, not native FLMV transition bounds.

\paragraph{Highest-value OPEN target.}
Construct or falsify a uniform source-computable, both-label, hereditary
\(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) that never consumes the parent answer bit, produces only
physically accessible \(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) ordinary-SAT children or a complete
checked terminal, and includes discovery, rejected policies, source probes,
output, access, reset, guest and ancestor state in the actual FLMV recurrence.
No such compiler is known.  P versus NP remains OPEN.

Source:
\path{research/main/2026-10-31_0048_MAIN_ARC-SEMANTIC-SELECTOR-FIREWALL236.tex}.


\section{01:48 quadratic small-bias firewall and five-role reconciliation}
\label{sec:lspv5-quadratic-smallbias-237}
\PROVED\ (exact proof-method boundary).
For \(x=(u_1,v_1,\ldots,u_r,v_r)\), let
\(q_r(x)=\bigoplus_{i=1}^r u_iv_i\) and let \(D_r\) be uniform on
\(q_r^{-1}(0)\).  The product Walsh calculation gives
\[
 \max_{a\ne0}\left|\mathbb E_{D_r}(-1)^{a\cdot X}\right|
 =\frac1{2^r+1},
 \qquad
 \Pr_U[q_r=1]-\Pr_{D_r}[q_r=1]
 =\frac{1-2^{-r}}2.
\]
Consequently a premise that controls only nonzero linear characters cannot
bound every source-independent quadratic output map.  Any nonlinear extension
of AFFINE-SMALLBIAS-DESCENT-GATE2 needs a construction-specific higher-degree
fooling theorem, in addition to both-label preservation and charged evaluation.
The archived affine MCSP theorem, including
\(\log_2B_N=O((u_N+1)\log(m+u_N+2))\), remains valid under its stated
source-independent affine hypotheses.

\COMPUTATIONAL\ (independent replay).
Exact enumeration for \(r=1,\ldots,5\) covered 1,364 ambient points and
577,251 linear-character terms; the independent checker passed 129 checks.
At \(r=5\), the linear bias is \(1/33\) and the quadratic gap is \(31/64\).
The matched original-source H7/H8 controls retain every occurrence-indexed
\(S/U\) target and inversion, all 49 clause-pair mode matrices, coherent
endpoints, flips and complements.  H7 has one model and H8 none; the common
DPLL used 3 and 7 nodes respectively.  These controls authenticate both labels
but are not native FLMV timings or a SAT reduction.

The newest specialist evidence remains restricted and mutually consistent.
LSPV-1's prime-chain guard proves unbounded indispensable resolution-source
support for one conclusion but an easy linear scan; LSPV-2 supplies the exact
distinct-pin parity child and total symbolic character accessor; LSPV-3 proves
linear static Krom/Horn backdoors on a uniformly easy strict22 family; and
LSPV-4 proves a nearly linear changed-pin source-probe tariff that one paid scan
amortizes.  LSPV-5 composes the parity child with every fixed total indexed
guest after authentication, but malformed/nonmember fallback and hereditary
closure remain absent.  None closes the source-computable all-input
\(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) arrow.  The next target is a certified all-input partition of the canonical
Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) language whose every cell either emits a complete power-short
blank-total ordinary child or terminates with a checkable exact certificate,
with discovery, rejected cells, source migration and simultaneous peak space
inside the strict FLMV margins.  P versus NP remains OPEN.

Sources:

and

\section{LSPV-5 target-scope and FLMV reconciliation (31 October 2026, 11:48 PDT)}
\label{sec:target-scope-flmv-referee247}
\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT.}
The total duplicate-free proper Monotone 3-SAT-(2,2) language is sufficient
for the qualitative \(P=NP\) and \(P\ne NP\) implications.  The charged
transport theorem composes length, setup, arbitrary-bit access and target
decider exponents by
\(\max\{u,q+aK\}\) in time and \(\max\{v,r,aL\}\) in space.
Consequently NP-completeness alone does not enter the numerical FLMV box.

\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.}
A same-source pair of valid 198-variable, 264-clause targets was checked on
both labels.  The UNSAT member has an empty intersection of six independently
reproduced boundary-row sets with admissible counts
\((48,56,56,48,56,48)\).  The SAT member has a replayed witness with
\((N_0,N_1,N_2,N_3)=(0,158,80,26)\).  All 792 signed occurrences and all
34,716 indexed clause pairs were accounted once; both complement endpoints
fail on the SAT member.  An external HiGHS 1.8.0 feasibility-MILP baseline
independently proved the UNSAT member infeasible in 46,035 branch-and-bound
nodes and found the SAT member in one node.

\noindent\textbf{OPEN.}
The first bit-local quasilinear source-to-Monotone-3-SAT\(^{*}\)-(2,2) leg,
hereditary target closure or charged re-encoding, universal adaptive policy
selection, and the ARC contraction into the strict FLMV box remain open.

\section{Endpoint--flux decomposition of XOR churn (LSPV-5, 16:48)}
\label{sec:xor-flux-churn-referee252}

\paragraph{PROVED/EXACT.}
For every valid duplicate-free proper Monotone \(3\)-SAT-\((2,2)\)
instance and every finite, possibly backtracking, variable-flip trace, let
\(R_0,F_0\) be the total \(0\!\to\!1\) and \(1\!\to\!0\) clause-count
crossings, and let \(R_1,F_1\) be the total \(1\!\to\!2\) and
\(2\!\to\!1\) crossings. With
\(C_0=\min\{R_0,F_0\}\), \(C_1=\min\{R_1,F_1\}\), the complete
source-indexed logical XOR totals satisfy
\[
 \sum_t d_{\mathrm{gray}}(t)
 =5T+\lvert\Delta N_0\rvert+2C_0,
\]
\[
 \sum_t d_{\mathrm{binary}}(t)
 =5T+\lvert\Delta N_0\rvert+
   \lvert\Delta(N_0+N_1)\rvert+2(C_0+C_1).
\]
This is a same-source SAT/UNSAT-correct accounting identity: it uses only
the strict \(2\)P\(2\)N occurrence law and transition conservation, not a
semantic oracle. The terms \(2C_0\) and \(2C_1\) exactly charge
changed-then-restored boundary traffic invisible at the endpoints.

\paragraph{COMPUTATIONAL.}
Independent exhaustive verification covered all \(16{,}576\) Y6 paths of
length at most three (\(132{,}608\) assertions), plus independently
certified U198 UNSAT and V198 SAT controls. A \(1000\)-flip restored loop
has zero endpoint delta but logical totals \(7000\) in both encodings;
random \(1000\)-flip traces gave gray/binary totals \(6034/7917\) on U198
and \(6036/7916\) on V198. These are finite controls, not asymptotic
lower bounds.

\paragraph{FALSIFIED/RETIRED.}
Cumulative logical XOR is not an endpoint potential, a monotone SAT
progress measure, a free best-policy selector, or an arbitrary-machine
runtime lower bound. Lazy flags, source rereads, read-only delay loops,
unaligned encodings and Gray-versus-binary representation choices remain
valid adversarial controls. Only in a declared fixed materialized
\(k\)-tape layout does the elementary representation-specific implication
\(T\geq \sum_t d_{\mathrm{phys}}(t)/k\) follow over disjoint checkpoint
intervals; online tracing itself must be charged.

\paragraph{OPEN status.}
No hereditary fully charged arbitrary-\(A\mapsto\Phi(A)\) contraction to
the strict FLMV box has been proved. The affine-small-bias theorem remains
a restricted MCSP semantic no-go and supplies no universal SAT runtime
lower bound. P versus NP remains OPEN.
